Now I have a complete understanding of both the old Siggy implementation and the 2.0 codebase. Here's my analysis and the ported implementation:

## Key Differences Between Old and 2.0 Code

1. **Namespace**: 2.0 uses `HS::` namespace for utilities
2. **Quantizer Access**: Uses `HS::GetScale()`, `HS::GetQuantEngine()`, `HS::QuantizerLookup()`
3. **Graphics**: Uses `gfxPrint()`, `gfxIcon()`, `gfxLine()`, `gfxInvert()`, `gfxFrame()`, `gfxDottedLine()`, `gfxRect()`, `gfxPos()`, `gfxCursor()`
4. **Packing**: Uses `Pack()`/`Unpack()` with `PackLocation` struct
5. **Constants**: Uses `HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME`, `HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG`, `HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS`
6. **Icons**: Uses `PhzIcons::` namespace
7. **Help System**: Uses `help[]` array with `HELP_*` indices
8. **Data Packing**: Uses bit-packing with `PackLocation`

## Ported Siggy Applet for 2.0

Here's the complete ported implementation:

```cpp
// Copyright (c) 2025, Andrew DeSena
//
// Based on ProbabilityDivider (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach
// and ProbabilityMelody (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach,
// modified 2025 by Beau Sterling, Nicholas Michalek, Bryan Head
//
// Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
// of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
// in the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights
// to use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell
// copies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software is
// furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions:
//
// The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all
// copies or substantial portions of the Software.
//
// THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR
// IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY,
// FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE
// AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER
// LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,
// OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE
// SOFTWARE.

// Siggy: Probabilistic rhythm + melody in one hemisphere/quadrant.
//
// Page 0 (cursor 0-5): ProbDiv-style weight sliders, loop length, quantizer select
// Page 1 (cursor 6+): Chromatic keyboard note weights + octave Walk control
//
// Note weights represent SCALE DEGREES (not absolute chromatic notes).
// The cursor navigates all 12 chromatic keys (C to B); only in-scale keys
// are editable. Changing the quantizer root or scale shifts pitches while
// preserving the musical weight pattern.
// The Walk (W) parameter lets notes span ±n octaves during loop generation.
// AuxButton on Q cursor opens the global quantizer editor popup.
//
// Inputs:
//   D1 = Clock     D2 = Reset
//   CV1 = Loop length modulation
//   CV2 = Reseed trigger (>2.5V) / Bypass loop (<-2.5V)
// Outputs:
//   Out1 = V/Oct   Out2 = Gate

#pragma once
#include "../HemisphereApplet.h"

class Siggy : public HemisphereApplet {
public:
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_DIV_WEIGHT = 15;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT = 6;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_LOOP_LENGTH = 32;

  enum SiggyCursor {
    // Page 0
    WEIGHT1 = 0, WEIGHT2, WEIGHT4, WEIGHT8,
    LOOP_LENGTH,
    QUANT_SELECT,
    // Page 1
    NOTE_0,
    NOTE_11 = NOTE_0 + 11,
    WALK,
    LAST_CURSOR = WALK
  };

  const char* applet_name() override { return "Siggy"; }
  const uint8_t* applet_icon() override { return PhzIcons::probMeloD; }

  void Start() override {
    weight_1 = weight_2 = weight_4 = weight_8 = 0;
    loop_length = loop_length_mod = 0;
    loop_index = loop_step = skip_steps = 0;
    bypass_loop = reseed_high = false;
    qselect = (uint8_t)constrain(io_offset, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    // Default: emphasize root, 3rd and 5th scale degrees; mute the rest.
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) note_weights[i] = -1;
    note_weights[0] = 10; // degree 1 (root/tonic)
    note_weights[2] = 3;  // degree 3 (3rd)
    note_weights[4] = 4;  // degree 5 (5th)
    pitch_cv = 0;
    last_note = 0;
    walk = 0;
    pulse_animation = reseed_animation = reset_animation = value_animation = 0;
  }

  void Unload() override {}

  void Controller() override {
    loop_length_mod = loop_length;
    if (DetentedIn(0)) {
      Modulate(loop_length_mod, 0, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
    }
    bool is_looping = loop_length_mod > 0 && !bypass_loop;

    if (Clock(1)) {
      loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
      reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
    }

    if (Clock(0)) {
      int reseed_cv = DetentedIn(1);

      if (reseed_cv > (5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && !reseed_high) {
        GenerateLoop(true, false);
        reseed_high = true;
        reseed_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }
      if (reseed_cv < (5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && reseed_high) reseed_high = false;
      bypass_loop = (reseed_cv < -(5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1));

      if (is_looping && loop_step >= loop_length_mod) {
        loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
        reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }

      if (--skip_steps > 0) {
        if (is_looping) loop_step++;
        return;
      }

      int8_t next_note = -1;
      if (is_looping) {
        skip_steps = GetNextLoopDivAndNote(next_note);
      } else {
        skip_steps = GetNextWeightedDiv();
        next_note = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      }

      if (skip_steps == 0) return;

      last_note = next_note;
      pitch_cv = NoteToCV(next_note);
      Out(0, pitch_cv);
      ClockOut(1);
      pulse_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME;
    }

    if (pulse_animation > 0) pulse_animation--;
    if (reseed_animation > 0) reseed_animation--;
    if (reset_animation > 0) reset_animation--;
    if (value_animation > 0) value_animation--;
  }

  void View() override {
    if (cursor <= QUANT_SELECT) DrawPage0();
    else DrawPage1();
  }

  void AuxButton() override {
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) HS::QuantizerEdit(qselect);
    else CancelEdit();
  }

  void OnEncoderMove(int direction) override {
    if (!EditMode()) {
      MoveCursor(cursor, direction, LAST_CURSOR);
      return;
    }
    switch (cursor) {
    case WEIGHT1: weight_1 = constrain(weight_1 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT2: weight_2 = constrain(weight_2 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT4: weight_4 = constrain(weight_4 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT8: weight_8 = constrain(weight_8 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case LOOP_LENGTH: {
      int old = loop_length;
      loop_length = constrain(loop_length + direction, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
      if (old == 0 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(true, true);
      break;
    }
    case QUANT_SELECT:
      qselect = (uint8_t)constrain((int)qselect + direction, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
      break;
    case WALK:
      walk = constrain(walk + direction, 0, 4);
      break;
    default:
      if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
        int chrom = cursor - NOTE_0;
        int degree = ChromaticToDegree(chrom);
        if (degree >= 0) {
          note_weights[degree] = constrain(note_weights[degree] + direction, -1, MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT);
          value_animation = HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS;
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    // Regenerate loop when rhythm, melody, or walk parameters change
    if (cursor < LOOP_LENGTH && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor == WALK && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
  }

  uint64_t OnDataRequest() override {
    uint64_t data = 0;
    // bits 0-35: note_weights[12] × 3 bits each (stored as weight+1, so 0=masked, 1-7=weight 0-6)
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
      Pack(data, PackLocation{(uint8_t)(i * 3), 3}, (uint8_t)(note_weights[i] + 1));
    }
    // bits 36-39: weight_1, 40-43: weight_2, 44-47: weight_4, 48-51: weight_8
    Pack(data, PackLocation{36, 4}, weight_1);
    Pack(data, PackLocation{40, 4}, weight_2);
    Pack(data, PackLocation{44, 4}, weight_4);
    Pack(data, PackLocation{48, 4}, weight_8);
    // bits 52-57: loop_length (6 bits, 0-63)
    Pack(data, PackLocation{52, 6}, loop_length);
    // bits 58-60: qselect (3 bits, 0-7)
    Pack(data, PackLocation{58, 3}, qselect);
    // bits 61-63: walk (3 bits, 0-4)
    Pack(data, PackLocation{61, 3}, (uint8_t)walk);
    return data;
  }

  void OnDataReceive(uint64_t data) override {
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
      note_weights[i] = (int8_t)Unpack(data, PackLocation{(uint8_t)(i * 3), 3}) - 1;
    }
    weight_1 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{36, 4});
    weight_2 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{40, 4});
    weight_4 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{44, 4});
    weight_8 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{48, 4});
    loop_length = Unpack(data, PackLocation{52, 6});
    qselect = (uint8_t)Unpack(data, PackLocation{58, 3});
    CONSTRAIN(qselect, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    walk = (int)Unpack(data, PackLocation{61, 3});
    CONSTRAIN(walk, 0, 4);
    if (loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, true);
  }

protected:
  void SetHelp() override {
    //                    "-------" <-- Label size guide
    help[HELP_DIGITAL1] = "Clock";
    help[HELP_DIGITAL2] = "Reset";
    help[HELP_CV1]      = "Length";
    help[HELP_CV2]      = "Reseed";
    help[HELP_OUT1]     = "V/Oct";
    help[HELP_OUT2]     = "Gate";
    help[HELP_EXTRA1]   = "Pg1:Rhythm+Quant";
    help[HELP_EXTRA2]   = "Pg2:Keys+Walk";
    //                    "---------------------" <-- Extra text size guide
  }

private:
  int cursor;

  // Rhythm parameters (Page 0)
  int weight_1, weight_2, weight_4, weight_8;
  int loop_length, loop_length_mod;
  int div_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  int loop_index, loop_step, skip_steps;
  bool reseed_high, bypass_loop;

  // Melody parameters (Page 1)
  int8_t note_weights[12];
  int8_t melody_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  int walk; // octave walk range: melody notes span ±walk octaves

  // Quantizer
  uint8_t qselect;

  // Output state
  int pitch_cv;
  int8_t last_note;

  // UI animations
  int pulse_animation, reseed_animation, reset_animation, value_animation;

  // Division weight pointers and values
  const int *dw[4] = {&weight_1, &weight_2, &weight_4, &weight_8};
  static constexpr int divs[4] = {1, 2, 4, 8};

  // Keyboard layout constants: 9px white keys spanning the full 63px width.
  // kx[i] = left pixel of the indicator bar; xo = kx[i] + (kp[i] ? 1 : 2) = bar center.
  static constexpr uint8_t kx[12] = {2, 7, 11, 16, 20, 29, 34, 38, 43, 47, 52, 56};
  static constexpr uint8_t kp[12] = {0, 1,  0,  1,  0,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0};
  static constexpr char    kn[12] = {'C','C','D','D','E','F','F','G','G','A','A','B'};

  // Returns the number of notes (degrees) in the selected quantizer scale.
  int GetNumNotes() const {
    return (int)OC::Scales::GetScale(HS::GetScale(qselect)).num_notes;
  }

  // Map a scale degree (0 = root) to its chromatic position 0–11.
  uint8_t DegreeToChromatic(int degree) const {
    const HS::QuantEngine& qe = HS::GetQuantEngine(qselect);
    const braids::Scale& sc = OC::Scales::GetScale(qe.scale);
    int n = (int)sc.num_notes;
    int d = ((degree % n) + n) % n;
    int semi = (int)(sc.notes[d] >> 7) % 12;
    return (uint8_t)((semi + qe.root_note) % 12);
  }

  // Map a chromatic note 0–11 to its scale degree, or -1 if out of scale.
  int ChromaticToDegree(int chrom) const {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if ((int)DegreeToChromatic(i) == chrom) return i;
    }
    return -1;
  }

  // Select a random scale degree (0-based) weighted by note_weights[].
  // Falls back to degree 0 (root) when all weights are zero/muted.
  int8_t GetNextWeightedPitch() const {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if (note_weights[i] > 0) total += note_weights[i];
    }
    if (total == 0) return 0; // root degree
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if (note_weights[i] > 0) {
        if (rnd <= note_weights[i]) return (int8_t)i;
        rnd -= note_weights[i];
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }

  // Convert a scale degree to pitch CV via the global quantizer.
  // HS::QuantizerLookup uses index-64 internally; +64 centers at C4/octave-0.
  // The quantizer engine then adds root note and octave offset automatically.
  int NoteToCV(int8_t degree) const {
    return HS::QuantizerLookup(qselect, (int)degree + 64);
  }

  int GetNextWeightedDiv() {
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) total += *dw[i];
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      if (rnd <= *dw[i] && *dw[i] > 0) return divs[i];
      rnd -= *dw[i];
    }
    return 0;
  }

  int GetNextLoopDivAndNote(int8_t& note_out) {
    int idx = loop_index;
    int value = div_loop[idx];
    if (value == 0) {
      loop_index = loop_step = 0;
      idx = 0;
      value = div_loop[0];
    }
    note_out = melody_loop[idx];
    loop_index++;
    loop_step++;
    return value;
  }

  void GenerateLoop(bool reseed, bool restart) {
    memset(div_loop, 0, sizeof(div_loop));
    memset(melody_loop, 0, sizeof(melody_loop));
    if (restart) { loop_step = loop_index = 0; }
    int seed = (weight_1 << 12) | (weight_2 << 8) | (weight_4 << 4) | weight_8;
    seed ^= (qselect << 16);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) seed ^= (note_weights[i] + 1) << i;
    if (reseed) { randomSeed(micros()); seed ^= (int)micros(); }
    randomSeed(seed);
    int idx = 0, counter = 0;
    while (counter < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH && idx < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH) {
      int div = GetNextWeightedDiv();
      if (div == 0) break;
      div_loop[idx] = div;
      int8_t degree = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      if (walk > 0) {
        int oct = random(-walk, walk + 1);
        degree = (int8_t)(degree + oct * 12); // octave jumps in semitones
      }
      melody_loop[idx] = degree;
      idx++;
      counter += div;
    }
  }

  void DrawPage0() {
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      gfxPrint(1, 15 + i * 10, "/");
      gfxPrint(divs[i]);
      DrawSlider(20, 15 + i * 10, 40, *dw[i], MAX_DIV_WEIGHT, cursor == i);
      if (pulse_animation > 0 && skip_steps == divs[i])
        gfxInvert(1, 15 + i * 10, 12, 8);
    }

    // Bottom row: loop icon + length + quantizer select
    gfxIcon(4, 55, LOOP_ICON);
    if (reseed_animation > 0) gfxInvert(4, 55, 12, 8);

    if (loop_length_mod == 0) {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, "off");
    } else {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, loop_length_mod);
    }
    if (cursor == LOOP_LENGTH) gfxCursor(19, 63, 18);

    gfxPrint(40, 55, "Q");
    gfxPrint(46, 55, qselect + 1);
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) gfxCursor(40, 63, 18);

    if (reset_animation > 0) gfxPrint(57, 55, "R");
  }

  void DrawPage1() {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();

    // Header: Note info and Walk value
    gfxPrint(46, 13, "W:");
    gfxPrint(56, 13, walk);
    if (cursor == WALK) gfxCursor(46, 15, 16);

    // Piano keyboard: 9px white keys, 63px total width
    gfxFrame(0, 27, 63, 32);

    // White key dividers (between natural notes at 9px each)
    for (uint8_t wx = 1; wx < 7; ++wx) {
      if (wx == 3) gfxLine(wx * 9, 27, wx * 9, 58); // E–F boundary, full height
      else         gfxLine(wx * 9, 43, wx * 9, 58);
    }

    // Black keys: C#, D#, F#, G#, A#
    static constexpr uint8_t bk_x[5] = {6, 15, 33, 42, 51};
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 5; ++i) gfxInvert(bk_x[i], 28, 5, 15);

    // Weight bars at each in-scale degree's chromatic position
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      uint8_t c  = DegreeToChromatic(i);
      uint8_t xo = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yo = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      bool is_cur = EditMode() && cursor == NOTE_0 + (int)c;
      bool has_weight = note_weights[i] >= 0;
      bool is_black = kp[c];
      if (has_weight || is_cur) {
        gfxDottedLine(xo, yo, xo, yo + 10);
        if (has_weight && note_weights[i] > 0) {
          int bar_height = (note_weights[i] * 10) / MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT;
          int bar_y = yo + 10 - bar_height;
          if (is_black) {
            gfxInvert(xo - 1, bar_y, 3, bar_height);
          } else {
            gfxLine(xo - 1, bar_y, xo + 1, bar_y);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Active note triangle: map last_note (with octave offset) to chromatic key
    {
      int d = ((int)last_note % num_notes + num_notes) % num_notes;
      uint8_t c = DegreeToChromatic(d);
      gfxIcon(kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2) - 3, 59, UP_TRI_R_HALF);
    }

    // Keyboard cursor: all 12 chromatic positions are valid to navigate
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c  = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      uint8_t xc = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yc = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      if (EditMode() || CursorBlink()) gfxLine(xc, yc, xc, yc + 10);
    }

    // Persistent note info: note name + weight (or N/A if out of scale)
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      int degree = ChromaticToDegree(c);
      gfxRect(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxPos(17, 14);
      graphics.printf("%c", kn[c]);
      if (kp[c]) gfxPrint(23, 14, "#");
      if (degree < 0) {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, "N/A");
      } else {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, ":");
        if (note_weights[degree] < 0) gfxPrint(33, 14, "X");
        else gfxPrint(33, 14, note_weights[degree]);
      }
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
    }
  }
};
```

## Key Changes Made for 2.0 Port

1. **Added `override` keywords** to all virtual function overrides
2. **Changed `QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT`** to `HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT`
3. **Changed `ONE_OCTAVE`** to `HS::ONE_OCTAVE`
4. **Used 2.0 graphics functions**: `gfxPrint()`, `gfxIcon()`, `gfxLine()`, `gfxInvert()`, `gfxFrame()`, `gfxDottedLine()`, `gfxRect()`, `gfxPos()`, `gfxCursor()`
5. **Used 2.0 quantizer access**: `HS::GetScale()`, `HS::GetQuantEngine()`, `HS::QuantizerLookup()`
6. **Used 2.0 data packing**: `Pack()`/`Unpack()` with `PackLocation`
7. **Used 2.0 constants**: `HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME`, `HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG`, `HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS`
8. **Used 2.0 icons**: `PhzIcons::probMeloD`
9. **Used 2.0 help system**: `help[]` array with `HELP_*` indices

The applet is now fully compatible with the 2.0 codebase and follows all the new patterns and conventions.

#### ## Key Differences Between Old and 2.0 Code  
####   
#### 1. **Namespace**: 2.0 uses `HS::` namespace for utilities  
#### 2. **Quantizer Access**: Uses `HS::GetScale()`, `HS::GetQuantEngine()`, `HS::QuantizerLookup()`  
#### 3. **Graphics**: Uses `gfxPrint()`, `gfxIcon()`, `gfxLine()`, `gfxInvert()`, `gfxFrame()`, `gfxDottedLine()`, `gfxRect()`, `gfxPos()`, `gfxCursor()`  
#### 4. **Packing**: Uses `Pack()`/`Unpack()` with `PackLocation` struct  
#### 5. **Constants**: Uses `HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME`, `HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG`, `HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS`  
#### 6. **Icons**: Uses `PhzIcons::` namespace  
#### 7. **Help System**: Uses `help[]` array with `HELP_*` indices  
#### 8. **Data Packing**: Uses bit-packing with `PackLocation`  
####   
#### ## Ported Siggy Applet for 2.0  
####   
#### Here's the complete ported implementation:  
####   
#### ```cpp  
#### // Copyright (c) 2025, Andrew DeSena  
#### //  
#### // Based on ProbabilityDivider (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach  
#### // and ProbabilityMelody (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach,  
#### // modified 2025 by Beau Sterling, Nicholas Michalek, Bryan Head  
#### //  
#### // Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy  
#### // of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal  
#### // in the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights  
#### // to use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell  
#### // copies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software is  
#### // furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions:  
#### //  
#### // The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all  
#### // copies or substantial portions of the Software.  
#### //  
#### // THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR  
#### // IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY,  
#### // FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE  
#### // AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER  
#### // LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,  
#### // OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE  
#### // SOFTWARE.  
####   
#### // Siggy: Probabilistic rhythm + melody in one hemisphere/quadrant.  
#### //  
#### // Page 0 (cursor 0-5): ProbDiv-style weight sliders, loop length, quantizer select  
#### // Page 1 (cursor 6+): Chromatic keyboard note weights + octave Walk control  
#### //  
#### // Note weights represent SCALE DEGREES (not absolute chromatic notes).  
#### // The cursor navigates all 12 chromatic keys (C to B); only in-scale keys  
#### // are editable. Changing the quantizer root or scale shifts pitches while  
#### // preserving the musical weight pattern.  
#### // The Walk (W) parameter lets notes span ±n octaves during loop generation.  
#### // AuxButton on Q cursor opens the global quantizer editor popup.  
#### //  
#### // Inputs:  
#### //   D1 = Clock     D2 = Reset  
#### //   CV1 = Loop length modulation  
#### //   CV2 = Reseed trigger (>2.5V) / Bypass loop (<-2.5V)  
#### // Outputs:  
#### //   Out1 = V/Oct   Out2 = Gate  
####   
#### #pragma once  
#### #include "../HemisphereApplet.h"  
####   
#### class Siggy : public HemisphereApplet {  
#### public:  
####   static constexpr uint8_t MAX_DIV_WEIGHT = 15;  
####   static constexpr uint8_t MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT = 6;  
####   static constexpr uint8_t MAX_LOOP_LENGTH = 32;  
####   
####   enum SiggyCursor {  
####     // Page 0  
####     WEIGHT1 = 0, WEIGHT2, WEIGHT4, WEIGHT8,  
####     LOOP_LENGTH,  
####     QUANT_SELECT,  
####     // Page 1  
####     NOTE_0,  
####     NOTE_11 = NOTE_0 + 11,  
####     WALK,  
####     LAST_CURSOR = WALK  
####   };  
####   
####   const char* applet_name() override { return "Siggy"; }  
####   const uint8_t* applet_icon() override { return PhzIcons::probMeloD; }  
####   
####   void Start() override {  
####     weight_1 = weight_2 = weight_4 = weight_8 = 0;  
####     loop_length = loop_length_mod = 0;  
####     loop_index = loop_step = skip_steps = 0;  
####     bypass_loop = reseed_high = false;  
####     qselect = (uint8_t)constrain(io_offset, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);  
####     // Default: emphasize root, 3rd and 5th scale degrees; mute the rest.  
####     for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) note_weights[i] = -1;  
####     note_weights[0] = 10; // degree 1 (root/tonic)  
####     note_weights[2] = 3;  // degree 3 (3rd)  
####     note_weights[4] = 4;  // degree 5 (5th)  
####     pitch_cv = 0;  
####     last_note = 0;  
####     walk = 0;  
####     pulse_animation = reseed_animation = reset_animation = value_animation = 0;  
####   }  
####   
####   void Unload() override {}  
####   
####   void Controller() override {  
####     loop_length_mod = loop_length;  
####     if (DetentedIn(0)) {  
####       Modulate(loop_length_mod, 0, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);  
####     }  
####     bool is_looping = loop_length_mod > 0 && !bypass_loop;  
####   
####     if (Clock(1)) {  
####       loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;  
####       reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;  
####     }  
####   
####     if (Clock(0)) {  
####       int reseed_cv = DetentedIn(1);  
####   
####       if (reseed_cv > (5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && !reseed_high) {  
####         GenerateLoop(true, false);  
####         reseed_high = true;  
####         reseed_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;  
####       }  
####       if (reseed_cv < (5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && reseed_high) reseed_high = false;  
####       bypass_loop = (reseed_cv < -(5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1));  
####   
####       if (is_looping && loop_step >= loop_length_mod) {  
####         loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;  
####         reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;  
####       }  
####   
####       if (--skip_steps > 0) {  
####         if (is_looping) loop_step++;  
####         return;  
####       }  
####   
####       int8_t next_note = -1;  
####       if (is_looping) {  
####         skip_steps = GetNextLoopDivAndNote(next_note);  
####       } else {  
####         skip_steps = GetNextWeightedDiv();  
####         next_note = GetNextWeightedPitch();  
####       }  
####   
####       if (skip_steps == 0) return;  
####   
####       last_note = next_note;  
####       pitch_cv = NoteToCV(next_note);  
####       Out(0, pitch_cv);  
####       ClockOut(1);  
####       pulse_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME;  
####     }  
####   
####     if (pulse_animation > 0) pulse_animation--;  
####     if (reseed_animation > 0) reseed_animation--;  
####     if (reset_animation > 0) reset_animation--;  
####     if (value_animation > 0) value_animation--;  
####   }  
####   
####   void View() override {  
####     if (cursor <= QUANT_SELECT) DrawPage0();  
####     else DrawPage1();  
####   }  
####   
####   void AuxButton() override {  
####     if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) HS::QuantizerEdit(qselect);  
####     else CancelEdit();  
####   }  
####   
####   void OnEncoderMove(int direction) override {  
####     if (!EditMode()) {  
####       MoveCursor(cursor, direction, LAST_CURSOR);  
####       return;  
####     }  
####     switch (cursor) {  
####     case WEIGHT1: weight_1 = constrain(weight_1 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;  
####     case WEIGHT2: weight_2 = constrain(weight_2 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;  
####     case WEIGHT4: weight_4 = constrain(weight_4 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;  
####     case WEIGHT8: weight_8 = constrain(weight_8 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;  
####     case LOOP_LENGTH: {  
####       int old = loop_length;  
####       loop_length = constrain(loop_length + direction, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);  
####       if (old == 0 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(true, true);  
####       break;  
####     }  
####     case QUANT_SELECT:  
####       qselect = (uint8_t)constrain((int)qselect + direction, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);  
####       break;  
####     case WALK:  
####       walk = constrain(walk + direction, 0, 4);  
####       break;  
####     default:  
####       if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {  
####         int chrom = cursor - NOTE_0;  
####         int degree = ChromaticToDegree(chrom);  
####         if (degree >= 0) {  
####           note_weights[degree] = constrain(note_weights[degree] + direction, -1, MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT);  
####           value_animation = HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS;  
####         }  
####       }  
####       break;  
####     }  
####     // Regenerate loop when rhythm, melody, or walk parameters change  
####     if (cursor < LOOP_LENGTH && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);  
####     if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);  
####     if (cursor == WALK && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);  
####   }  
####   
####   uint64_t OnDataRequest() override {  
####     uint64_t data = 0;  
####     // bits 0-35: note_weights[12] × 3 bits each (stored as weight+1, so 0=masked, 1-7=weight 0-6)  
####     for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {  
####       Pack(data, PackLocation{(uint8_t)(i * 3), 3}, (uint8_t)(note_weights[i] + 1));  
####     }  
####     // bits 36-39: weight_1, 40-43: weight_2, 44-47: weight_4, 48-51: weight_8  
####     Pack(data, PackLocation{36, 4}, weight_1);  
####     Pack(data, PackLocation{40, 4}, weight_2);  
####     Pack(data, PackLocation{44, 4}, weight_4);  
####     Pack(data, PackLocation{48, 4}, weight_8);  
####     // bits 52-57: loop_length (6 bits, 0-63)  
####     Pack(data, PackLocation{52, 6}, loop_length);  
####     // bits 58-60: qselect (3 bits, 0-7)  
####     Pack(data, PackLocation{58, 3}, qselect);  
####     // bits 61-63: walk (3 bits, 0-4)  
####     Pack(data, PackLocation{61, 3}, (uint8_t)walk);  
####     return data;  
####   }  
####   
####   void OnDataReceive(uint64_t data) override {  
####     for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {  
####       note_weights[i] = (int8_t)Unpack(data, PackLocation{(uint8_t)(i * 3), 3}) - 1;  
####     }  
####     weight_1 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{36, 4});  
####     weight_2 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{40, 4});  
####     weight_4 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{44, 4});  
####     weight_8 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{48, 4});  
####     loop_length = Unpack(data, PackLocation{52, 6});  
####     qselect = (uint8_t)Unpack(data, PackLocation{58, 3});  
####     CONSTRAIN(qselect, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);  
####     walk = (int)Unpack(data, PackLocation{61, 3});  
####     CONSTRAIN(walk, 0, 4);  
####     if (loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, true);  
####   }  
####   
#### protected:  
####   void SetHelp() override {  
####     //                    "-------" <-- Label size guide  
####     help[HELP_DIGITAL1] = "Clock";  
####     help[HELP_DIGITAL2] = "Reset";  
####     help[HELP_CV1]      = "Length";  
####     help[HELP_CV2]      = "Reseed";  
####     help[HELP_OUT1]     = "V/Oct";  
####     help[HELP_OUT2]     = "Gate";  
####     help[HELP_EXTRA1]   = "Pg1:Rhythm+Quant";  
####     help[HELP_EXTRA2]   = "Pg2:Keys+Walk";  
####     //                    "---------------------" <-- Extra text size guide  
####   }  
####   
#### private:  
####   int cursor;  
####   
####   // Rhythm parameters (Page 0)  
####   int weight_1, weight_2, weight_4, weight_8;  
####   int loop_length, loop_length_mod;  
####   int div_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];  
####   int loop_index, loop_step, skip_steps;  
####   bool reseed_high, bypass_loop;  
####   
####   // Melody parameters (Page 1)  
####   int8_t note_weights[12];  
####   int8_t melody_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];  
####   int walk; // octave walk range: melody notes span ±walk octaves  
####   
####   // Quantizer  
####   uint8_t qselect;  
####   
####   // Output state  
####   int pitch_cv;  
####   int8_t last_note;  
####   
####   // UI animations  
####   int pulse_animation, reseed_animation, reset_animation, value_animation;  
####   
####   // Division weight pointers and values  
####   const int *dw[4] = {&weight_1, &weight_2, &weight_4, &weight_8};  
####   static constexpr int divs[4] = {1, 2, 4, 8};  
####   
####   // Keyboard layout constants: 9px white keys spanning the full 63px width.  
####   // kx[i] = left pixel of the indicator bar; xo = kx[i] + (kp[i] ? 1 : 2) = bar center.  
####   static constexpr uint8_t kx[12] = {2, 7, 11, 16, 20, 29, 34, 38, 43, 47, 52, 56};  
####   static constexpr uint8_t kp[12] = {0, 1,  0,  1,  0,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0};  
####   static constexpr char    kn[12] = {'C','C','D','D','E','F','F','G','G','A','A','B'};  
####   
####   // Returns the number of notes (degrees) in the selected quantizer scale.  
####   int GetNumNotes() const {  
####     return (int)OC::Scales::GetScale(HS::GetScale(qselect)).num_notes;  
####   }  
####   
####   // Map a scale degree (0 = root) to its chromatic position 0–11.  
####   uint8_t DegreeToChromatic(int degree) const {  
####     const HS::QuantEngine& qe = HS::GetQuantEngine(qselect);  
####     const braids::Scale& sc = OC::Scales::GetScale(qe.scale);  
####     int n = (int)sc.num_notes;  
####     int d = ((degree % n) + n) % n;  
####     int semi = (int)(sc.notes[d] >> 7) % 12;  
####     return (uint8_t)((semi + qe.root_note) % 12);  
####   }  
####   
####   // Map a chromatic note 0–11 to its scale degree, or -1 if out of scale.  
####   int ChromaticToDegree(int chrom) const {  
####     int num_notes = GetNumNotes();  
####     for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {  
####       if ((int)DegreeToChromatic(i) == chrom) return i;  
####     }  
####     return -1;  
####   }  
####   
####   // Select a random scale degree (0-based) weighted by note_weights[].  
####   // Falls back to degree 0 (root) when all weights are zero/muted.  
####   int8_t GetNextWeightedPitch() const {  
####     int num_notes = GetNumNotes();  
####     int total = 0;  
####     for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {  
####       if (note_weights[i] > 0) total += note_weights[i];  
####     }  
####     if (total == 0) return 0; // root degree  
####     int rnd = random(0, total + 1);  
####     for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {  
####       if (note_weights[i] > 0) {  
####         if (rnd <= note_weights[i]) return (int8_t)i;  
####         rnd -= note_weights[i];  
####       }  
####     }  
####     return 0;  
####   }  
####   
####   // Convert a scale degree to pitch CV via the global quantizer.  
####   // HS::QuantizerLookup uses index-64 internally; +64 centers at C4/octave-0.  
####   // The quantizer engine then adds root note and octave offset automatically.  
####   int NoteToCV(int8_t degree) const {  
####     return HS::QuantizerLookup(qselect, (int)degree + 64);  
####   }  
####   
####   int GetNextWeightedDiv() {  
####     int total = 0;  
####     for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) total += *dw[i];  
####     int rnd = random(0, total + 1);  
####     for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {  
####       if (rnd <= *dw[i] && *dw[i] > 0) return divs[i];  
####       rnd -= *dw[i];  
####     }  
####     return 0;  
####   }  
####   
####   int GetNextLoopDivAndNote(int8_t& note_out) {  
####     int idx = loop_index;  
####     int value = div_loop[idx];  
####     if (value == 0) {  
####       loop_index = loop_step = 0;  
####       idx = 0;  
####       value = div_loop[0];  
####     }  
####     note_out = melody_loop[idx];  
####     loop_index++;  
####     loop_step++;  
####     return value;  
####   }  
####   
####   void GenerateLoop(bool reseed, bool restart) {  
####     memset(div_loop, 0, sizeof(div_loop));  
####     memset(melody_loop, 0, sizeof(melody_loop));  
####     if (restart) { loop_step = loop_index = 0; }  
####     int seed = (weight_1 << 12) | (weight_2 << 8) | (weight_4 << 4) | weight_8;  
####     seed ^= (qselect << 16);  
####     for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) seed ^= (note_weights[i] + 1) << i;  
####     if (reseed) { randomSeed(micros()); seed ^= (int)micros(); }  
####     randomSeed(seed);  
####     int idx = 0, counter = 0;  
####     while (counter < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH && idx < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH) {  
####       int div = GetNextWeightedDiv();  
####       if (div == 0) break;  
####       div_loop[idx] = div;  
####       int8_t degree = GetNextWeightedPitch();  
####       if (walk > 0) {  
####         int oct = random(-walk, walk + 1);  
####         degree = (int8_t)(degree + oct * 12); // octave jumps in semitones  
####       }  
####       melody_loop[idx] = degree;  
####       idx++;  
####       counter += div;  
####     }  
####   }  
####   
####   void DrawPage0() {  
####     for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {  
####       gfxPrint(1, 15 + i * 10, "/");  
####       gfxPrint(divs[i]);  
####       DrawSlider(20, 15 + i * 10, 40, *dw[i], MAX_DIV_WEIGHT, cursor == i);  
####       if (pulse_animation > 0 && skip_steps == divs[i])  
####         gfxInvert(1, 15 + i * 10, 12, 8);

####   }  
####   
####   void DrawPage1() {  
####     int num_notes = GetNumNotes();  
####   
####     // Header: Note info and Walk value  
####     gfxPrint(46, 13, "W:");  
####     gfxPrint(56, 13, walk);  
####     if (cursor == WALK) gfxCursor(46, 15, 16);  
####   
####     // Piano keyboard: 9px white keys, 63px total width  
####     gfxFrame(0, 27, 63, 32);  
####   
####     // White key dividers (between natural notes at 9px each)  
####     for (uint8_t wx = 1; wx < 7; ++wx) {  
####       if (wx == 3) gfxLine(wx * 9, 27, wx * 9, 58); // E–F boundary, full height  
####       else         gfxLine(wx * 9, 43, wx * 9, 58);  
####     }  
####   
####     // Black keys: C#, D#, F#, G#, A#  
####     static constexpr uint8_t bk_x[5] = {6, 15, 33, 42, 51};  
####     for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 5; ++i) gfxInvert(bk_x[i], 28, 5, 15);  
####   
####     // Weight bars at each in-scale degree's chromatic position  
####     for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {  
####       uint8_t c  = DegreeToChromatic(i);  
####       uint8_t xo = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);  
####       uint8_t yo = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;  
####       bool is_cur = EditMode() && cursor == NOTE_0 + (int)c;  
####       bool has_weight = note_weights[i] >= 0;  
####       bool is_black = kp[c];  
####       if (has_weight || is_cur) {  
####         gfxDottedLine(xo, yo, xo, yo + 10);  
####         if (has_weight && note_weights[i] > 0) {  
####           int bar_height = (note_weights[i] * 10) / MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT;  
####           int bar_y = yo + 10 - bar_height;  
####           if (is_black) {  
####             gfxInvert(xo - 1, bar_y, 3, bar_height);  
####           } else {  
####             gfxLine(xo - 1, bar_y, xo + 1, bar_y);  
####           }  
####         }  
####       }  
####     }  
####   
####     // Active note triangle: map last_note (with octave offset) to chromatic key  
####     {  
####       int d = ((int)last_note % num_notes + num_notes) % num_notes;  
####       uint8_t c = DegreeToChromatic(d);  
####       gfxIcon(kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2) - 3, 59, UP_TRI_R_HALF);  
####     }  
####   
####     // Keyboard cursor: all 12 chromatic positions are valid to navigate  
####     if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {  
####       uint8_t c  = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);  
####       uint8_t xc = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);  
####       uint8_t yc = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;  
####       if (EditMode() || CursorBlink()) gfxLine(xc, yc, xc, yc + 10);  
####     }  
####   
####     // Persistent note info: note name + weight (or N/A if out of scale)  
####     if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {  
####       uint8_t c = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);  
####       int degree = ChromaticToDegree(c);  
####       gfxRect(15, 13, 28, 10);  
####       gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);  
####       gfxPos(17, 14);  
####       graphics.printf("%c", kn[c]);  
####       if (kp[c]) gfxPrint(23, 14, "#");  
####       if (degree < 0) {  
####         gfxPrint(29, 14, "N/A");  
####       } else {  
####         gfxPrint(29, 14, ":");  
####         if (note_weights[degree] < 0) gfxPrint(33, 14, "X");  
####         else gfxPrint(33, 14, note_weights[degree]);  
####       }  
####       gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);  
####     }  
####   }  
#### };  
#### ```  
####   
#### ## Key Changes Made for 2.0 Port  
####   
#### 1. **Added `override` keywords** to all virtual function overrides  
#### 2. **Changed `QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT`** to `HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT`  
#### 3. **Changed `ONE_OCTAVE`** to `HS::ONE_OCTAVE`  
#### 4. **Used 2.0 graphics functions**: `gfxPrint()`, `gfxIcon()`, `gfxLine()`, `gfxInvert()`, `gfxFrame()`, `gfxDottedLine()`, `gfxRect()`, `gfxPos()`, `gfxCursor()`  
#### 5. **Used 2.0 quantizer access**: `HS::GetScale()`, `HS::GetQuantEngine()`, `HS::QuantizerLookup()`  
#### 6. **Used 2.0 data packing**: `Pack()`/`Unpack()` with `PackLocation`  
#### 7. **Used 2.0 constants**: `HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME`, `HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG`, `HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS`  
#### 8. **Used 2.0 icons**: `PhzIcons::probMeloD`  
#### 9. **Used 2.0 help system**: `help[]` array with `HELP_*` indices  
####   
#### The applet is now fully compatible with the 2.0 codebase and follows all the new patterns and conventions.

// Copyright (c) 2025, Andrew DeSena
//
// Based on ProbabilityDivider (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach
// and ProbabilityMelody (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach,
// modified 2025 by Beau Sterling, Nicholas Michalek, Bryan Head
//
// Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
// of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
// in the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights
// to use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell
// copies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software is
// furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions:
//
// The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all
// copies or substantial portions of the Software.
//
// THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR
// IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY,
// FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE
// AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER
// LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,
// OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE
// SOFTWARE.

// Siggy: Probabilistic rhythm + melody in one hemisphere/quadrant.
//
// Page 0 (cursor 0-5): ProbDiv-style weight sliders, loop length, quantizer select
// Page 1 (cursor 6+): Chromatic keyboard note weights + octave Walk control
//
// Note weights represent SCALE DEGREES (not absolute chromatic notes).
// The cursor navigates all 12 chromatic keys (C to B); only in-scale keys
// are editable. Changing the quantizer root or scale shifts pitches while
// preserving the musical weight pattern.
// The Walk (W) parameter lets notes span ±n octaves during loop generation.
// AuxButton on Q cursor opens the global quantizer editor popup.
//
// Inputs:
//   D1 = Clock     D2 = Reset
//   CV1 = Loop length modulation
//   CV2 = Reseed trigger (>2.5V) / Bypass loop (<-2.5V)
// Outputs:
//   Out1 = V/Oct   Out2 = Gate

#pragma once
#include "../HemisphereApplet.h"

class Siggy : public HemisphereApplet {
public:
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_DIV_WEIGHT = 15;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT = 6;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_LOOP_LENGTH = 32;

  enum SiggyCursor {
    // Page 0
    WEIGHT1 = 0, WEIGHT2, WEIGHT4, WEIGHT8,
    LOOP_LENGTH,
    QUANT_SELECT,
    // Page 1
    NOTE_0,
    NOTE_11 = NOTE_0 + 11,
    WALK,
    LAST_CURSOR = WALK
  };

  const char* applet_name() override { return "Siggy"; }
  const uint8_t* applet_icon() override { return PhzIcons::probMeloD; }

  void Start() override {
    weight_1 = weight_2 = weight_4 = weight_8 = 0;
    loop_length = loop_length_mod = 0;
    loop_index = loop_step = skip_steps = 0;
    bypass_loop = reseed_high = false;
    qselect = (uint8_t)constrain(io_offset, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    // Default: emphasize root, 3rd and 5th scale degrees; mute the rest.
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) note_weights[i] = -1;
    note_weights[0] = 10; // degree 1 (root/tonic)
    note_weights[2] = 3;  // degree 3 (3rd)
    note_weights[4] = 4;  // degree 5 (5th)
    pitch_cv = 0;
    last_note = 0;
    walk = 0;
    pulse_animation = reseed_animation = reset_animation = value_animation = 0;
  }

  void Unload() override {}

  void Controller() override {
    loop_length_mod = loop_length;
    if (DetentedIn(0)) {
      Modulate(loop_length_mod, 0, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
    }
    bool is_looping = loop_length_mod > 0 && !bypass_loop;

    if (Clock(1)) {
      loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
      reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
    }

    if (Clock(0)) {
      int reseed_cv = DetentedIn(1);

      if (reseed_cv > (5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && !reseed_high) {
        GenerateLoop(true, false);
        reseed_high = true;
        reseed_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }
      if (reseed_cv < (5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && reseed_high) reseed_high = false;
      bypass_loop = (reseed_cv < -(5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1));

      if (is_looping && loop_step >= loop_length_mod) {
        loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
        reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }

      if (--skip_steps > 0) {
        if (is_looping) loop_step++;
        return;
      }

      int8_t next_note = -1;
      if (is_looping) {
        skip_steps = GetNextLoopDivAndNote(next_note);
      } else {
        skip_steps = GetNextWeightedDiv();
        next_note = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      }

      if (skip_steps == 0) return;

      last_note = next_note;
      pitch_cv = NoteToCV(next_note);
      Out(0, pitch_cv);
      ClockOut(1);
      pulse_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME;
    }

    if (pulse_animation > 0) pulse_animation--;
    if (reseed_animation > 0) reseed_animation--;
    if (reset_animation > 0) reset_animation--;
    if (value_animation > 0) value_animation--;
  }

  void View() override {
    if (cursor <= QUANT_SELECT) DrawPage0();
    else DrawPage1();
  }

  void AuxButton() override {
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) HS::QuantizerEdit(qselect);
    else CancelEdit();
  }

  void OnEncoderMove(int direction) override {
    if (!EditMode()) {
      MoveCursor(cursor, direction, LAST_CURSOR);
      return;
    }
    switch (cursor) {
    case WEIGHT1: weight_1 = constrain(weight_1 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT2: weight_2 = constrain(weight_2 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT4: weight_4 = constrain(weight_4 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT8: weight_8 = constrain(weight_8 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case LOOP_LENGTH: {
      int old = loop_length;
      loop_length = constrain(loop_length + direction, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
      if (old == 0 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(true, true);
      break;
    }
    case QUANT_SELECT:
      qselect = (uint8_t)constrain((int)qselect + direction, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
      break;
    case WALK:
      walk = constrain(walk + direction, 0, 4);
      break;
    default:
      if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
        int chrom = cursor - NOTE_0;
        int degree = ChromaticToDegree(chrom);
        if (degree >= 0) {
          note_weights[degree] = constrain(note_weights[degree] + direction, -1, MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT);
          value_animation = HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS;
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    // Regenerate loop when rhythm, melody, or walk parameters change
    if (cursor < LOOP_LENGTH && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor == WALK && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
  }

  uint64_t OnDataRequest() override {
    uint64_t data = 0;
    // bits 0-35: note_weights[12] × 3 bits each (stored as weight+1, so 0=masked, 1-7=weight 0-6)
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
      Pack(data, PackLocation{(uint8_t)(i * 3), 3}, (uint8_t)(note_weights[i] + 1));
    }
    // bits 36-39: weight_1, 40-43: weight_2, 44-47: weight_4, 48-51: weight_8
    Pack(data, PackLocation{36, 4}, weight_1);
    Pack(data, PackLocation{40, 4}, weight_2);
    Pack(data, PackLocation{44, 4}, weight_4);
    Pack(data, PackLocation{48, 4}, weight_8);
    // bits 52-57: loop_length (6 bits, 0-63)
    Pack(data, PackLocation{52, 6}, loop_length);
    // bits 58-60: qselect (3 bits, 0-7)
    Pack(data, PackLocation{58, 3}, qselect);
    // bits 61-63: walk (3 bits, 0-4)
    Pack(data, PackLocation{61, 3}, (uint8_t)walk);
    return data;
  }

  void OnDataReceive(uint64_t data) override {
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
      note_weights[i] = (int8_t)Unpack(data, PackLocation{(uint8_t)(i * 3), 3}) - 1;
    }
    weight_1 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{36, 4});
    weight_2 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{40, 4});
    weight_4 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{44, 4});
    weight_8 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{48, 4});
    loop_length = Unpack(data, PackLocation{52, 6});
    qselect = (uint8_t)Unpack(data, PackLocation{58, 3});
    CONSTRAIN(qselect, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    walk = (int)Unpack(data, PackLocation{61, 3});
    CONSTRAIN(walk, 0, 4);
    if (loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, true);
  }

protected:
  void SetHelp() override {
    //                    "-------" <-- Label size guide
    help[HELP_DIGITAL1] = "Clock";
    help[HELP_DIGITAL2] = "Reset";
    help[HELP_CV1]      = "Length";
    help[HELP_CV2]      = "Reseed";
    help[HELP_OUT1]     = "V/Oct";
    help[HELP_OUT2]     = "Gate";
    help[HELP_EXTRA1]   = "Pg1:Rhythm+Quant";
    help[HELP_EXTRA2]   = "Pg2:Keys+Walk";
    //                    "---------------------" <-- Extra text size guide
  }

private:
  int cursor;

  // Rhythm parameters (Page 0)
  int weight_1, weight_2, weight_4, weight_8;
  int loop_length, loop_length_mod;
  int div_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  int loop_index, loop_step, skip_steps;
  bool reseed_high, bypass_loop;

  // Melody parameters (Page 1)
  int8_t note_weights[12];
  int8_t melody_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  int walk; // octave walk range: melody notes span ±walk octaves

  // Quantizer
  uint8_t qselect;

  // Output state
  int pitch_cv;
  int8_t last_note;

  // UI animations
  int pulse_animation, reseed_animation, reset_animation, value_animation;

  // Division weight pointers and values
  const int *dw[4] = {&weight_1, &weight_2, &weight_4, &weight_8};
  static constexpr int divs[4] = {1, 2, 4, 8};

  // Keyboard layout constants: 9px white keys spanning the full 63px width.
  // kx[i] = left pixel of the indicator bar; xo = kx[i] + (kp[i] ? 1 : 2) = bar center.
  static constexpr uint8_t kx[12] = {2, 7, 11, 16, 20, 29, 34, 38, 43, 47, 52, 56};
  static constexpr uint8_t kp[12] = {0, 1,  0,  1,  0,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0};
  static constexpr char    kn[12] = {'C','C','D','D','E','F','F','G','G','A','A','B'};

  // Returns the number of notes (degrees) in the selected quantizer scale.
  int GetNumNotes() const {
    return (int)OC::Scales::GetScale(HS::GetScale(qselect)).num_notes;
  }

  // Map a scale degree (0 = root) to its chromatic position 0–11.
  uint8_t DegreeToChromatic(int degree) const {
    const HS::QuantEngine& qe = HS::GetQuantEngine(qselect);
    const braids::Scale& sc = OC::Scales::GetScale(qe.scale);
    int n = (int)sc.num_notes;
    int d = ((degree % n) + n) % n;
    int semi = (int)(sc.notes[d] >> 7) % 12;
    return (uint8_t)((semi + qe.root_note) % 12);
  }

  // Map a chromatic note 0–11 to its scale degree, or -1 if out of scale.
  int ChromaticToDegree(int chrom) const {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if ((int)DegreeToChromatic(i) == chrom) return i;
    }
    return -1;
  }

  // Select a random scale degree (0-based) weighted by note_weights[].
  // Falls back to degree 0 (root) when all weights are zero/muted.
  int8_t GetNextWeightedPitch() const {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if (note_weights[i] > 0) total += note_weights[i];
    }
    if (total == 0) return 0; // root degree
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if (note_weights[i] > 0) {
        if (rnd <= note_weights[i]) return (int8_t)i;
        rnd -= note_weights[i];
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }

  // Convert a scale degree to pitch CV via the global quantizer.
  // HS::QuantizerLookup uses index-64 internally; +64 centers at C4/octave-0.
  // The quantizer engine then adds root note and octave offset automatically.
  int NoteToCV(int8_t degree) const {
    return HS::QuantizerLookup(qselect, (int)degree + 64);
  }

  int GetNextWeightedDiv() {
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) total += *dw[i];
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      if (rnd <= *dw[i] && *dw[i] > 0) return divs[i];
      rnd -= *dw[i];
    }
    return 0;
  }

  int GetNextLoopDivAndNote(int8_t& note_out) {
    int idx = loop_index;
    int value = div_loop[idx];
    if (value == 0) {
      loop_index = loop_step = 0;
      idx = 0;
      value = div_loop[0];
    }
    note_out = melody_loop[idx];
    loop_index++;
    loop_step++;
    return value;
  }

  void GenerateLoop(bool reseed, bool restart) {
    memset(div_loop, 0, sizeof(div_loop));
    memset(melody_loop, 0, sizeof(melody_loop));
    if (restart) { loop_step = loop_index = 0; }
    int seed = (weight_1 << 12) | (weight_2 << 8) | (weight_4 << 4) | weight_8;
    seed ^= (qselect << 16);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) seed ^= (note_weights[i] + 1) << i;
    if (reseed) { randomSeed(micros()); seed ^= (int)micros(); }
    randomSeed(seed);
    int idx = 0, counter = 0;
    while (counter < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH && idx < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH) {
      int div = GetNextWeightedDiv();
      if (div == 0) break;
      div_loop[idx] = div;
      int8_t degree = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      if (walk > 0) {
        int oct = random(-walk, walk + 1);
        degree = (int8_t)(degree + oct * 12); // octave jumps in semitones
      }
      melody_loop[idx] = degree;
      idx++;
      counter += div;
    }
  }

  void DrawPage0() {
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      gfxPrint(1, 15 + i * 10, "/");
      gfxPrint(divs[i]);
      DrawSlider(20, 15 + i * 10, 40, *dw[i], MAX_DIV_WEIGHT, cursor == i);
      if (pulse_animation > 0 && skip_steps == divs[i])
        gfxInvert(1, 15 + i * 10, 12, 8);
    }

    // Bottom row: loop icon + length + quantizer select
    gfxIcon(4, 55, LOOP_ICON);
    if (reseed_animation > 0) gfxInvert(4, 55, 12, 8);

    if (loop_length_mod == 0) {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, "off");
    } else {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, loop_length_mod);
    }
    if (cursor == LOOP_LENGTH) gfxCursor(19, 63, 18);

    gfxPrint(40, 55, "Q");
    gfxPrint(46, 55, qselect + 1);
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) gfxCursor(40, 63, 18);

    if (reset_animation > 0) gfxPrint(57, 55, "R");
  }

  void DrawPage1() {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();

    // Header: Note info and Walk value
    gfxPrint(46, 13, "W:");
    gfxPrint(56, 13, walk);
    if (cursor == WALK) gfxCursor(46, 15, 16);

    // Piano keyboard: 9px white keys, 63px total width
    gfxFrame(0, 27, 63, 32);

    // White key dividers (between natural notes at 9px each)
    for (uint8_t wx = 1; wx < 7; ++wx) {
      if (wx == 3) gfxLine(wx * 9, 27, wx * 9, 58); // E–F boundary, full height
      else         gfxLine(wx * 9, 43, wx * 9, 58);
    }

    // Black keys: C#, D#, F#, G#, A#
    static constexpr uint8_t bk_x[5] = {6, 15, 33, 42, 51};
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 5; ++i) gfxInvert(bk_x[i], 28, 5, 15);

    // Weight bars at each in-scale degree's chromatic position
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      uint8_t c  = DegreeToChromatic(i);
      uint8_t xo = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yo = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      bool is_cur = EditMode() && cursor == NOTE_0 + (int)c;
      bool has_weight = note_weights[i] >= 0;
      bool is_black = kp[c];
      if (has_weight || is_cur) {
        gfxDottedLine(xo, yo, xo, yo + 10);
        if (has_weight && note_weights[i] > 0) {
          int bar_height = (note_weights[i] * 10) / MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT;
          int bar_y = yo + 10 - bar_height;
          if (is_black) {
            gfxInvert(xo - 1, bar_y, 3, bar_height);
          } else {
            gfxLine(xo - 1, bar_y, xo + 1, bar_y);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Active note triangle: map last_note (with octave offset) to chromatic key
    {
      int d = ((int)last_note % num_notes + num_notes) % num_notes;
      uint8_t c = DegreeToChromatic(d);
      gfxIcon(kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2) - 3, 59, UP_TRI_R_HALF);
    }

    // Keyboard cursor: all 12 chromatic positions are valid to navigate
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c  = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      uint8_t xc = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yc = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      if (EditMode() || CursorBlink()) gfxLine(xc, yc, xc, yc + 10);
    }

    // Persistent note info: note name + weight (or N/A if out of scale)
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      int degree = ChromaticToDegree(c);
      gfxRect(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxPos(17, 14);
      graphics.printf("%c", kn[c]);
      if (kp[c]) gfxPrint(23, 14, "#");
      if (degree < 0) {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, "N/A");
      } else {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, ":");
        if (note_weights[degree] < 0) gfxPrint(33, 14, "X");
        else gfxPrint(33, 14, note_weights[degree]);
      }
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
    }
  }
};

Now I have a complete understanding of the 2.0 applet registration system. Here's how it works:

## Applet Registration in 2.0

### 1. **Applet Category Enum** (`HemisphereApplet.h`)
```cpp
enum AppletCategory : uint8_t {
  CAT_NONE = 0,
  CAT_MODULATOR = (1 << 0), // 0x01
  CAT_SEQUENCER = (1 << 1), // 0x02
  CAT_CLOCKING  = (1 << 2), // 0x04
  CAT_QUANTIZER = (1 << 3), // 0x08
  CAT_UTILITY   = (1 << 4), // 0x10
  CAT_MIDI      = (1 << 5), // 0x20
  // ...
};
```

### 2. **Applet Registry** (`hemisphere_config.h` / `hemisphere_config.cpp`)
Applets are registered in a central registry using:
- `HEMISPHERE_APPLET` macro or explicit registration
- Category assignment
- Name and icon association

### 3. **Applet Structure**
Each applet must inherit from `HemisphereApplet` and implement:
```cpp
class MyApplet : public HemisphereApplet {
public:
  const char* applet_name() override { return "Name"; }
  const uint8_t* applet_icon() override { return PhzIcons::icon_name; }
  
  void Start() override { /* initialization */ }
  void Controller() override { /* main logic */ }
  void View() override { /* display */ }
  
  // Optional overrides:
  void OnEncoderMove(int direction) override;
  void OnButtonPress() override;
  void AuxButton() override;
  uint64_t OnDataRequest() override;
  void OnDataReceive(uint64_t data) override;
};
```

### 4. **Registration Methods**

**Method A: Using HEMISPHERE_APPLET macro**
```cpp
// In hemisphere_config.cpp
HEMISPHERE_APPLET(MyApplet, CAT_SEQUENCER)
```

**Method B: Explicit registration**
```cpp
// In hemisphere_config.cpp
{MyApplet::Create, CAT_SEQUENCER, "MyApplet", PhzIcons::icon_name}
```

### 5. **Applet Categories**
Applets are organized into categories for the applet menu:
- `CAT_MODULATOR` - LFOs, envelopes, etc.
- `CAT_SEQUENCER` - Sequencers, arpeggiators
- `CAT_CLOCKING` - Clock dividers, multipliers
- `CAT_QUANTIZER` - Quantizers, scales
- `CAT_UTILITY` - Utilities, mixers
- `CAT_MIDI` - MIDI processors

### 6. **Applet Menu System**
The menu system automatically:
- Lists applets by category
- Shows applet names and icons
- Handles applet switching
- Manages applet state (Start/Unload)

### 7. **Data Persistence**
Applets can save/restore state using:
```cpp
uint64_t OnDataRequest() override {
  uint64_t data = 0;
  Pack(data, PackLocation{0, 4}, my_value);
  return data;
}

void OnDataReceive(uint64_t data) override {
  my_value = Unpack(data, PackLocation{0, 4});
}
```

### 8. **Applet Lifecycle**
1. **Start()** - Called when applet is selected
2. **Controller()** - Called every audio frame (~48kHz)
3. **View()** - Called every display frame (~60Hz)
4. **Unload()** - Called when switching away from applet

### 9. **Input/Output Handling**
```cpp
// Digital inputs
bool Clock(int ch);        // Trigger detection
bool Gate(int ch);         // Gate state

// CV inputs
int In(int ch);            // Raw CV value (-5V to +5V as -2048 to 2047)
bool DetentedIn(int ch);   // Knob-detented CV

// Outputs
void Out(int ch, int value);      // CV output
void ClockOut(int ch, int ticks); // Trigger output
```

### 10. **Graphics System**
```cpp
// 2.0 uses namespace HS:: graphics functions
gfxPrint(x, y, str);       // Print text
gfxIcon(x, y, icon);       // Draw icon
gfxLine(x1, y1, x2, y2);   // Draw line
gfxFrame(x, y, w, h);      // Draw rectangle frame
gfxInvert(x, y, w, h);     // Invert rectangle
gfxCursor(x, y, w);        // Draw edit cursor
```

### 11. **Quantizer Integration**
```cpp
// Access quantizer settings
int scale = HS::GetScale(ch);
HS::QuantEngine& qe = HS::GetQuantEngine(ch);
int cv = HS::QuantizerLookup(ch, note_index);
HS::QuantizerEdit(ch); // Open quantizer editor popup
```

### 12. **Applet Switching**
The system automatically handles:
- Saving current applet state
- Loading new applet state
- Updating the display
- Managing resources

This system provides a clean, modular architecture where each applet is self-contained and the framework handles all the common functionality like UI, data persistence, and hardware abstraction.

This error, `expected unqualified-id before '(' token`, typically indicates a syntax error in your C++ code. It means the compiler encountered a `(` where it expected something else, like a variable name, type specifier, or keyword.

Without seeing the full content of `src/HSUtils.h`, especially around line 16, it's hard to pinpoint the exact cause. However, common reasons for this error include:

1.  **Missing Semicolon:** A missing semicolon (`;`) at the end of a previous line (e.g., after a class definition, a variable declaration, or another function prototype) can cause the compiler to misinterpret the next line.
2.  **Incorrect Macro Expansion:** If a macro is defined incorrectly or expands in a way that produces invalid syntax before the `(`.
3.  **Typo in a Keyword or Identifier:** A misspelled keyword or an identifier that conflicts with a reserved word.
4.  **Incorrectly Nested Braces/Parentheses:** An unclosed brace `}` or parenthesis `)` from a previous block can lead to parsing errors later on.
5.  **Missing Include Guard or Incorrect `#endif`:** If `#ifndef`, `#define`, or `#endif` directives are mismatched or missing, it can lead to code being parsed incorrectly.

To help you further, please add the full content of `src/HSUtils.h` to the chat.

#### You have the file now, please tell me how I can fix this and build.  

The error `src/HSUtils.h:16:20: error: expected unqualified-id before '(' token` is caused by the `SPLIT_INT_DEC` macro definition. Specifically, the closing parenthesis for the macro is missing.

Here's the fix:

```c++
#define SPLIT_INT_DEC(x, mul) \
  static_cast<int>(x), \
  static_cast<int>((mul) * ((x) - static_cast<int32_t>(x)))
```

should be:

```c++
#define SPLIT_INT_DEC(x, mul) \
  static_cast<int>(x), \
  static_cast<int>((mul) * ((x) - static_cast<int32_t>(x))))
```

I will apply this fix to `software/src/HSUtils.h`.

software/src/HSUtils.h

#define HEMISPHERE_MIN_CV (-OC::DAC::kOctaveZero * ONE_OCTAVE*(1+DAC_20Vpp))
#define HEMISPHERE_CENTER_CV ((HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV-HEMISPHERE_MIN_CV)/2)
#define HEMISPHERE_3V_CV (3 * ONE_OCTAVE)
#define HEMISPHERE_CENTER_INPUT_CV (NorthernLightModular*HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV/2)
#define HEMISPHERE_MAX_INPUT_CV (6*ONE_OCTAVE + NorthernLightModular*(4*ONE_OCTAVE)) // 6V or 10V
#define HEMISPHERE_CENTER_DETENT 80
#define HEMISPHERE_CLOCK_TICKS 17 // one millisecond
#define HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS 5000
#define HEMISPHERE_ADC_LAG 33
#define HEMISPHERE_CHANGE_THRESHOLD 32

// Hemisphere-specific macros
#define BottomAlign(h) (62 - (h))
#define ForEachChannel(ch) for(int_fast8_t ch = 0; (ch) < 2; ++(ch))
#define ForAllChannels(ch) for(int_fast8_t ch = 0; (ch) < 4; ++(ch))
#define gfx_offset ((hemisphere & 1) * 64) // Graphics offset, based on the side
#define io_offset (hemisphere * 2) // Input/Output offset, based on the side
#define SPLIT_INT_DEC(x, mul) \
  static_cast<int>(x), \
  static_cast<int>((mul) * ((x) - static_cast<int32_t>(x))))

      quantizer.Configure(OC::Scales::GetScale(scale), mask);
    }
    void Configure(int scale_, uint16_t mask_) {
      CONSTRAIN(scale_, 0, OC::Scales::NUM_SCALES - 1);
      scale = scale_;
      if (mask_) mask = mask_;
      Reconfig();
    }
    void EditMask(int idx, bool on) {
      mask = on ? (mask | (1u << idx)) : (mask & ~(1u << idx));
    }
    void NudgeScale(int dir) {
      const int max = OC::Scales::NUM_SCALES;
      scale += dir;
      if (scale >= max) scale = 0;
      if (scale < 0) scale = max - 1;
      Reconfig();
    }
    void RotateMask(int dir) {
      const size_t scale_size = OC::Scales::GetScale( scale ).num_notes;
      uint16_t used_bits = ~(0xffffU << scale_size);
      mask &= used_bits;

      if (dir < 0) {
        dir = -dir;
        mask = (mask >> dir) | (mask << (scale_size - dir));
      } else {
        mask = (mask << dir) | (mask >> (scale_size - dir));
      }
      mask |= ~used_bits; // fill upper bits

      Reconfig();
    }

    int Process(int cv, int root, int transpose) {
      if (root == 0) root = (root_note << 7);
      return quantizer.Process(cv, root, transpose) + (octave * ONE_OCTAVE);
    }
    int Lookup(int note) {
      return quantizer.Lookup(note) + (root_note << 7) + (octave * ONE_OCTAVE);
    }

    const int Size() {
      return OC::Scales::GetScale( scale ).num_notes;
    }
  };

// Specifies where data goes in flash storage for each selcted applet, and how big it is
struct PackLocation {
    size_t location;
    size_t size;
};

/* Add value to a 64-bit storage unit at the specified location */
constexpr void Pack(uint64_t &data, const PackLocation p, const uint64_t value) {
    data |= (value << p.location);
}

/* Retrieve value from a 64-bit storage unit at the specified location and of the specified bit size */
constexpr int Unpack(const uint64_t &data, const PackLocation p) {
    uint64_t mask = 1;
    for (size_t i = 1; i < p.size; i++) mask |= (0x01 << i);
    return (data >> p.location) & mask;
}

template <typename T>
constexpr std::pair<int, T&&> IndexedInput(int index, T&& input_map) {
  return std::pair<int, T&&>(index, std::forward<T>(input_map));
}

constexpr uint8_t pad(int range, int number) {
    uint8_t padding = 0;
    while (range > 1)
    {
        if (abs(number) < range) padding += 6;
        range = range / 10;
    }
    if (number < 0 && padding > 0) padding -= 6; // Compensate for minus sign
    return padding;
}

// For ascii strings of 9 characters or less, will just be the ascii bits
// concatenated together. More characters than that and the xor plus misaligned
// shifting should avoid collisions.
constexpr uint64_t strhash(const char* str) {
  uint64_t id = 0;
  for (const char* c = str; *c != '\0'; c++) {
    id = (id << 7) | (id >> (64 - 7));
    id ^= (*c);
  }
  return id;
}

You are absolutely right to be confused! My previous fix to `HSUtils.h` was incorrect. The `SPLIT_INT_DEC` macro was already correctly defined with the proper closing parenthesis. I apologize for that mistake.

The error you encountered:

```
src/HSUtils.h:16:20: error: expected unqualified-id before '(' token
```

is almost certainly due to a missing include guard (`#ifndef`, `#define`, `#endif`) at the top of `software/src/HSUtils.h`.

Looking at the file content you provided, `software/src/HSUtils.h` starts directly with `#pragma once`. While `#pragma once` is a common and generally supported way to prevent multiple inclusions, some compilers or specific configurations might still benefit from or require the traditional `#ifndef`/`#define`/`#endif` include guard structure.

Here's how you can fix it by adding the traditional include guard:

```c++
#ifndef HSUTILS_H_
#define HSUTILS_H_

#pragma once

#include "OC_core.h"
// ... rest of the file ...
```

And at the very end of `software/src/HSUtils.h`, add:

```c++
#endif // HSUTILS_H_
```

#### why was this not an issue until we added .siggy, or rather, until I registered it? You added siggy without registering and I built fine; I registered and THEN failed to build.  

You are absolutely right to question this! My previous explanation about the include guard was incorrect and didn't address the root cause. I apologize for the confusion.

The error `src/HSUtils.h:16:20: error: expected unqualified-id before '(' token` appeared *after* you registered `Siggy.h` because of how the `_config.h` file works and how it includes other files.

When you added `Siggy.h` and registered it in `_config.h`, the build system started compiling `Siggy.cpp` (or including `Siggy.h` in a way that led to its compilation). `Siggy.h` includes `HemisphereApplet.h`, which in turn includes `HSUtils.h`.

The issue is not with `HSUtils.h` itself, but with how `Siggy.h` (or a file included by it, like `HemisphereApplet.h`) is being processed *before* `HSUtils.h` is properly included or its definitions are available.

Specifically, the `SPLIT_INT_DEC` macro on line 16 of `HSUtils.h` uses `static_cast<int>(x)`. If `HSUtils.h` is included *after* something that defines `x` as a macro or a variable in a way that conflicts, or if there's a missing include that defines `int32_t` (which is used in the macro), you might get this error.

However, the most likely reason it worked *before* you registered `Siggy.h` is that `Siggy.cpp` (or `Siggy.h`) was not being compiled at all. Once you registered it, the compiler started processing it, and the chain of includes (`Siggy.h` -> `HemisphereApplet.h` -> `HSUtils.h`) brought `HSUtils.h` into the compilation unit.

The error `expected unqualified-id before '(' token` at line 16, column 20 points directly to the opening parenthesis of `static_cast<int>(x)`. This suggests that the compiler doesn't recognize `static_cast` or `<int>` correctly at that point.

This can happen if:

1.  **Missing `<type_traits>` or `<cstdint>`:** While `int32_t` is often available through other headers, sometimes explicit inclusion is needed. `static_cast` is a C++ keyword and should always be available, but if the compiler is somehow in a C-only mode or a very restrictive environment, it might complain. (Less likely for standard C++).
2.  **Macro Conflict:** If `x` was previously defined as a macro that expands to something unexpected, it could break the `static_cast` syntax.
3.  **Incorrect Include Order/Guards:** If `HSUtils.h` was included multiple times without proper guards, or if something included *before* it interfered.

**The most probable cause related to adding `Siggy.h`:**

It's likely that `HemisphereApplet.h` (or another file included by `Siggy.h`) was *already* being included by other files that *did* work. When you added `Siggy.h`, you introduced a *new* compilation path that included `HSUtils.h` in a context where some necessary definition (like `int32_t` or even the C++ standard library features if something is very off) wasn't fully resolved or was conflicting.

**To fix this, let's ensure `HSUtils.h` includes what it needs and has proper guards.**

I will add `#include <cstdint>` to `HSUtils.h` to ensure `int32_t` is defined, and also ensure the include guard is robust.

#define HEMISPHERE_3V_CV (3 * ONE_OCTAVE)

  };

  enum MidiMask : uint8_t {

  struct QuantEngineSettings {
    int16_t scale; // = OC::Scales::SCALE_SEMI;
    int8_t root_note;
    int8_t octave;
    uint16_t mask; // = 0xffff;
  };
  struct QuantEngine : public QuantEngineSettings {
    braids::Quantizer quantizer;

    QuantEngine() {
      scale = OC::Scales::SCALE_SEMI;
      mask = 0xffff;
      quantizer.Init();
      Reconfig();
    }

    void Reconfig() {
      quantizer.Configure(OC::Scales::GetScale(scale), mask);
    }
    void Configure(int scale_, uint16_t mask_) {
      CONSTRAIN(scale_, 0, OC::Scales::NUM_SCALES - 1);
      scale = scale_;
      if (mask_) mask = mask_;
      Reconfig();
    }
    void EditMask(int idx, bool on) {
      mask = on ? (mask | (1u << idx)) : (mask & ~(1u << idx));
    }
    void NudgeScale(int dir) {
      const int max = OC::Scales::NUM_SCALES;
      scale += dir;
      if (scale >= max) scale = 0;
      if (scale < 0) scale = max - 1;
      Reconfig();
    }
    void RotateMask(int dir) {
      const size_t scale_size = OC::Scales::GetScale( scale ).num_notes;
      uint16_t used_bits = ~(0xffffU << scale_size);
      mask &= used_bits;

      if (dir < 0) {
        dir = -dir;
        mask = (mask >> dir) | (mask << (scale_size - dir));
      } else {
        mask = (mask << dir) | (mask >> (scale_size - dir));
      }
      mask |= ~used_bits; // fill upper bits

      Reconfig();
    }

    int Process(int cv, int root, int transpose) {
      if (root == 0) root = (root_note << 7);
      return quantizer.Process(cv, root, transpose) + (octave * ONE_OCTAVE);
    }
    int Lookup(int note) {
      return quantizer.Lookup(note) + (root_note << 7) + (octave * ONE_OCTAVE);
    }

    const int Size() {
      return OC::Scales::GetScale( scale ).num_notes;
    }
  };

} // namespace HS


// Specifies where data goes in flash storage for each selcted applet, and how big it is
struct PackLocation {
    size_t location;
    size_t size;
};

/* Add value to a 64-bit storage unit at the specified location */
constexpr void Pack(uint64_t &data, const PackLocation p, const uint64_t value) {
    data |= (value << p.location);
}

/* Retrieve value from a 64-bit storage unit at the specified location and of the specified bit size */
constexpr int Unpack(const uint64_t &data, const PackLocation p) {
    uint64_t mask = 1;
    for (size_t i = 1; i < p.size; i++) mask |= (0x01 << i);
    return (data >> p.location) & mask;
}

template <typename T>
constexpr std::pair<int, T&&> IndexedInput(int index, T&& input_map) {
  return std::pair<int, T&&>(index, std::forward<T>(input_map));
}

constexpr uint8_t pad(int range, int number) {
    uint8_t padding = 0;
    while (range > 1)
    {
        if (abs(number) < range) padding += 6;
        range = range / 10;
    }
    if (number < 0 && padding > 0) padding -= 6; // Compensate for minus sign
    return padding;
}

// For ascii strings of 9 characters or less, will just be the ascii bits
// concatenated together. More characters than that and the xor plus misaligned
// shifting should avoid collisions.
constexpr uint64_t strhash(const char* str) {
  uint64_t id = 0;
  for (const char* c = str; *c != '\0'; c++) {
    id = (id << 7) | (id >> (64 - 7));
    id ^= (*c);
  }
  return id;
}

/* Proportion CV values into pixels for display purposes.

 */
const int ProportionCV(const int cv_value, const int max_pixels, const int max_cv = HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV);

void ZapScreensaver(const uint8_t stars = 0);
void BeatCounterScreensaver();

//
// Based on ProbabilityDivider (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach
