```
// Siggy: Probabilistic rhythm + melody in one hemisphere/quadrant.
//
// Page 0 (cursor 0-5): ProbDiv-style weight sliders, loop length, quantizer select
// Page 1 (cursor 6+): Chromatic keyboard note weights + octave Walk control
//
// Note weights represent SCALE DEGREES (not absolute chromatic notes).
// The cursor navigates all 12 chromatic keys (C to B); only in-scale keys
// are editable. Changing the quantizer root or scale shifts pitches while
// preserving the musical weight pattern.
// The Walk (W) parameter lets notes span ±n octaves during loop generation.
// AuxButton on Q cursor opens the global quantizer editor popup.
//
// Inputs:
//   D1 = Clock     D2 = Reset
//   CV1 = Loop length modulation
//   CV2 = Reseed trigger (>2.5V) / Bypass loop (<-2.5V)
// Outputs:
//   Out1 = V/Oct   Out2 = Gate

#pragma once
#include "../HemisphereApplet.h"

class Siggy : public HemisphereApplet {
public:
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_DIV_WEIGHT = 15;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT = 6;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_LOOP_LENGTH = 32;

  enum SiggyCursor {
    // Page 0
    WEIGHT1 = 0, WEIGHT2, WEIGHT4, WEIGHT8,
    LOOP_LENGTH,
    QUANT_SELECT,
    // Page 1
    NOTE_0,
    NOTE_11 = NOTE_0 + 11,
    WALK,
    LAST_CURSOR = WALK
  };

  const char* applet_name() { return "Siggy"; }
  const uint8_t* applet_icon() { return PhzIcons::probMeloD; }

  void Start() override {
    weight_1 = weight_2 = weight_4 = weight_8 = 0;
    loop_length = loop_length_mod = 0;
    loop_index = loop_step = skip_steps = 0;
    bypass_loop = reseed_high = false;
    qselect = (uint8_t)constrain(io_offset, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    // Default: emphasize root, 3rd and 5th scale degrees; mute the rest.
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) note_weights[i] = -1;
    note_weights[0] = 10; // degree 1 (root/tonic)
    note_weights[2] = 3;  // degree 3 (3rd)
    note_weights[4] = 4;  // degree 5 (5th)
    pitch_cv = 0;
    last_note = 0;
    walk = 0;
    pulse_animation = reseed_animation = reset_animation = value_animation = 0;
  }

  void Unload() override {}

  void Controller() override {
    loop_length_mod = loop_length;
    if (DetentedIn(0)) {
      Modulate(loop_length_mod, 0, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
    }
    bool is_looping = loop_length_mod > 0 && !bypass_loop;

    if (Clock(1)) {
      loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
      reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
    }

    if (Clock(0)) {
      int reseed_cv = DetentedIn(1);

      if (reseed_cv > (5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && !reseed_high) {
        GenerateLoop(true, false);
        reseed_high = true;
        reseed_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }
      if (reseed_cv < (5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && reseed_high) reseed_high = false;
      bypass_loop = (reseed_cv < -(5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1));

      if (is_looping && loop_step >= loop_length_mod) {
        loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
        reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }

      if (--skip_steps > 0) {
        if (is_looping) loop_step++;
        return;
      }

      int8_t next_note = -1;
      if (is_looping) {
        skip_steps = GetNextLoopDivAndNote(next_note);
      } else {
        skip_steps = GetNextWeightedDiv();
        next_note = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      }

      if (skip_steps == 0) return;

      last_note = next_note;
      pitch_cv = NoteToCV(next_note);
      Out(0, pitch_cv);
      ClockOut(1);
      pulse_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME;
    }

    if (pulse_animation > 0) pulse_animation--;
    if (reseed_animation > 0) reseed_animation--;
    if (reset_animation > 0) reset_animation--;
    if (value_animation > 0) value_animation--;
  }

  void View() override {
    if (cursor <= QUANT_SELECT) DrawPage0();
    else DrawPage1();
  }

  void AuxButton() override {
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) HS::QuantizerEdit(qselect);
    else CancelEdit();
  }

  void OnEncoderMove(int direction) override {
    if (!EditMode()) {
      MoveCursor(cursor, direction, LAST_CURSOR);
      return;
    }
    switch (cursor) {
    case WEIGHT1: weight_1 = constrain(weight_1 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT2: weight_2 = constrain(weight_2 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT4: weight_4 = constrain(weight_4 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT8: weight_8 = constrain(weight_8 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case LOOP_LENGTH: {
      int old = loop_length;
      loop_length = constrain(loop_length + direction, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
      if (old == 0 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(true, true);
      break;
    }
    case QUANT_SELECT:
      qselect = (uint8_t)constrain((int)qselect + direction, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
      break;
    case WALK:
      walk = constrain(walk + direction, 0, 4);
      break;
    default:
      if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
        int chrom = cursor - NOTE_0;
        int degree = ChromaticToDegree(chrom);
        if (degree >= 0) {
          note_weights[degree] = constrain(note_weights[degree] + direction, -1, MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT);
          value_animation = HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS;
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    // Regenerate loop when rhythm, melody, or walk parameters change
    if (cursor < LOOP_LENGTH && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor == WALK && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
  }

  uint64_t OnDataRequest() override {
    uint64_t data = 0;
    // bits 0-35: note_weights[12] × 3 bits each (stored as weight+1, so 0=masked, 1-7=weight 0-6)
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
      Pack(data, PackLocation{(uint8_t)(i * 3), 3}, (uint8_t)(note_weights[i] + 1));
    }
    // bits 36-39: weight_1, 40-43: weight_2, 44-47: weight_4, 48-51: weight_8
    Pack(data, PackLocation{36, 4}, weight_1);
    Pack(data, PackLocation{40, 4}, weight_2);
    Pack(data, PackLocation{44, 4}, weight_4);
    Pack(data, PackLocation{48, 4}, weight_8);
    // bits 52-57: loop_length (6 bits, 0-63)
    Pack(data, PackLocation{52, 6}, loop_length);
    // bits 58-60: qselect (3 bits, 0-7)
    Pack(data, PackLocation{58, 3}, qselect);
    // bits 61-63: walk (3 bits, 0-4)
    Pack(data, PackLocation{61, 3}, (uint8_t)walk);
    return data;
  }

  void OnDataReceive(uint64_t data) override {
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
      note_weights[i] = (int8_t)Unpack(data, PackLocation{(uint8_t)(i * 3), 3}) - 1;
    }
    weight_1 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{36, 4});
    weight_2 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{40, 4});
    weight_4 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{44, 4});
    weight_8 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{48, 4});
    loop_length = Unpack(data, PackLocation{52, 6});
    qselect = (uint8_t)Unpack(data, PackLocation{58, 3});
    CONSTRAIN(qselect, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    walk = (int)Unpack(data, PackLocation{61, 3});
    CONSTRAIN(walk, 0, 4);
    if (loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, true);
  }

protected:
  void SetHelp() override {
    //                    "-------" <-- Label size guide
    help[HELP_DIGITAL1] = "Clock";
    help[HELP_DIGITAL2] = "Reset";
    help[HELP_CV1]      = "Length";
    help[HELP_CV2]      = "Reseed";
    help[HELP_OUT1]     = "V/Oct";
    help[HELP_OUT2]     = "Gate";
    help[HELP_EXTRA1]   = "Pg1:Rhythm+Quant";
    help[HELP_EXTRA2]   = "Pg2:Keys+Walk";
    //                    "---------------------" <-- Extra text size guide
  }

private:
  int cursor;

  // Rhythm parameters (Page 0)
  int weight_1, weight_2, weight_4, weight_8;
  int loop_length, loop_length_mod;
  int div_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  int loop_index, loop_step, skip_steps;
  bool reseed_high, bypass_loop;

  // Melody parameters (Page 1)
  int8_t note_weights[12];
  int8_t melody_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  int walk; // octave walk range: melody notes span ±walk octaves

  // Quantizer
  uint8_t qselect;

  // Output state
  int pitch_cv;
  int8_t last_note;

  // UI animations
  int pulse_animation, reseed_animation, reset_animation, value_animation;

  // Division weight pointers and values
  const int *dw[4] = {&weight_1, &weight_2, &weight_4, &weight_8};
  static constexpr int divs[4] = {1, 2, 4, 8};

  // Keyboard layout constants: 9px white keys spanning the full 63px width.
  // kx[i] = left pixel of the indicator bar; xo = kx[i] + (kp[i] ? 1 : 2) = bar center.
  static constexpr uint8_t kx[12] = {2, 7, 11, 16, 20, 29, 34, 38, 43, 47, 52, 56};
  static constexpr uint8_t kp[12] = {0, 1,  0,  1,  0,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0};
  static constexpr char    kn[12] = {'C','C','D','D','E','F','F','G','G','A','A','B'};

  // Returns the number of notes (degrees) in the selected quantizer scale.
  int GetNumNotes() const {
    return (int)OC::Scales::GetScale(HS::GetScale(qselect)).num_notes;
  }

  // Map a scale degree (0 = root) to its chromatic position 0–11.
  uint8_t DegreeToChromatic(int degree) const {
    const HS::QuantEngine& qe = HS::GetQuantEngine(qselect);
    const braids::Scale& sc = OC::Scales::GetScale(qe.scale);
    int n = (int)sc.num_notes;
    int d = ((degree % n) + n) % n;
    int semi = (int)(sc.notes[d] >> 7) % 12;
    return (uint8_t)((semi + qe.root_note) % 12);
  }

  // Map a chromatic note 0–11 to its scale degree, or -1 if out of scale.
  int ChromaticToDegree(int chrom) const {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if ((int)DegreeToChromatic(i) == chrom) return i;
    }
    return -1;
  }

  // Select a random scale degree (0-based) weighted by note_weights[].
  // Falls back to degree 0 (root) when all weights are zero/muted.
  int8_t GetNextWeightedPitch() const {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if (note_weights[i] > 0) total += note_weights[i];
    }
    if (total == 0) return 0; // root degree
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if (note_weights[i] > 0) {
        if (rnd <= note_weights[i]) return (int8_t)i;
        rnd -= note_weights[i];
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }

  // Convert a scale degree to pitch CV via the global quantizer.
  // HS::QuantizerLookup uses index-64 internally; +64 centers at C4/octave-0.
  // The quantizer engine then adds root note and octave offset automatically.
  int NoteToCV(int8_t degree) const {
    return HS::QuantizerLookup(qselect, (int)degree + 64);
  }

  int GetNextWeightedDiv() {
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) total += *dw[i];
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      if (rnd <= *dw[i] && *dw[i] > 0) return divs[i];
      rnd -= *dw[i];
    }
    return 0;
  }

  int GetNextLoopDivAndNote(int8_t& note_out) {
    int idx = loop_index;
    int value = div_loop[idx];
    if (value == 0) {
      loop_index = loop_step = 0;
      idx = 0;
      value = div_loop[0];
    }
    note_out = melody_loop[idx];
    loop_index++;
    loop_step++;
    return value;
  }

  void GenerateLoop(bool reseed, bool restart) {
    memset(div_loop, 0, sizeof(div_loop));
    memset(melody_loop, 0, sizeof(melody_loop));
    if (restart) { loop_step = loop_index = 0; }
    int seed = (weight_1 << 12) | (weight_2 << 8) | (weight_4 << 4) | weight_8;
    seed ^= (qselect << 16);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) seed ^= (note_weights[i] + 1) << i;
    if (reseed) { randomSeed(micros()); seed ^= (int)micros(); }
    randomSeed(seed);
    int idx = 0, counter = 0;
    while (counter < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH && idx < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH) {
      int div = GetNextWeightedDiv();
      if (div == 0) break;
      div_loop[idx] = div;
      int8_t degree = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      if (walk > 0) {
        int oct = random(-walk, walk + 1);
        degree = (int8_t)(degree + oct * 12); // octave jumps in semitones
      }
      melody_loop[idx] = degree;
      idx++;
      counter += div;
    }
  }

  void DrawPage0() {
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      gfxPrint(1, 15 + i * 10, "/");
      gfxPrint(divs[i]);
      DrawSlider(20, 15 + i * 10, 40, *dw[i], MAX_DIV_WEIGHT, cursor == i);
      if (pulse_animation > 0 && skip_steps == divs[i])
        gfxInvert(1, 15 + i * 10, 12, 8);
    }

    // Bottom row: loop icon + length + quantizer select
    gfxIcon(4, 55, LOOP_ICON);
    if (reseed_animation > 0) gfxInvert(4, 55, 12, 8);

    if (loop_length_mod == 0) {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, "off");
    } else {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, loop_length_mod);
    }
    if (cursor == LOOP_LENGTH) gfxCursor(19, 63, 18);

    gfxPrint(40, 55, "Q");
    gfxPrint(46, 55, qselect + 1);
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) gfxCursor(40, 63, 18);

    if (reset_animation > 0) gfxPrint(57, 55, "R");
  }

  void DrawPage1() {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();

    // Header: Note info and Walk value
    gfxPrint(46, 13, "W:");
    gfxPrint(56, 13, walk);
    if (cursor == WALK) gfxCursor(46, 15, 16);

    // Piano keyboard: 9px white keys, 63px total width
    gfxFrame(0, 27, 63, 32);

    // White key dividers (between natural notes at 9px each)
    for (uint8_t wx = 1; wx < 7; ++wx) {
      if (wx == 3) gfxLine(wx * 9, 27, wx * 9, 58); // E–F boundary, full height
      else         gfxLine(wx * 9, 43, wx * 9, 58);
    }

    // Black keys: C#, D#, F#, G#, A#
    static constexpr uint8_t bk_x[5] = {6, 15, 33, 42, 51};
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 5; ++i) gfxInvert(bk_x[i], 28, 5, 15);

    // Weight bars at each in-scale degree's chromatic position
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      uint8_t c  = DegreeToChromatic(i);
      uint8_t xo = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yo = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      bool is_cur = EditMode() && cursor == NOTE_0 + (int)c;
      bool has_weight = note_weights[i] >= 0;
      bool is_black = kp[c];
      if (has_weight || is_cur) {
        gfxDottedLine(xo, yo, xo, yo + 10);
        if (has_weight && note_weights[i] > 0) {
          int bar_height = (note_weights[i] * 10) / MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT;
          int bar_y = yo + 10 - bar_height;
          if (is_black) {
            gfxInvert(xo - 1, bar_y, 3, bar_height);
          } else {
            gfxLine(xo - 1, bar_y, xo + 1, bar_y);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Active note triangle: map last_note (with octave offset) to chromatic key
    {
      int d = ((int)last_note % num_notes + num_notes) % num_notes;
      uint8_t c = DegreeToChromatic(d);
      gfxIcon(kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2) - 3, 59, UP_TRI_R_HALF);
    }

    // Keyboard cursor: all 12 chromatic positions are valid to navigate
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c  = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      uint8_t xc = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yc = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      if (EditMode() || CursorBlink()) gfxLine(xc, yc, xc, yc + 10);
    }

    // Persistent note info: note name + weight (or N/A if out of scale)
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      int degree = ChromaticToDegree(c);
      gfxRect(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxPos(17, 14);
      graphics.printf("%c", kn[c]);
      if (kp[c]) gfxPrint(23, 14, "#");
      if (degree < 0) {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, "N/A");
      } else {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, ":");
        if (note_weights[degree] < 0) gfxPrint(33, 14, "X");
        else gfxPrint(33, 14, note_weights[degree]);
      }
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
    }
  }
};
```

```
#pragma once

#include "OC_core.h"

// misc. utility functions extracted from Hemisphere
// -NJM

// Reference Constants
#define ONE_OCTAVE (12 << 7)
#define PULSE_VOLTAGE HS::octave_max
#define HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV (HS::octave_max * ONE_OCTAVE)
#define HEMISPHERE_MIN_CV (-OC::DAC::kOctaveZero * ONE_OCTAVE*(1+DAC_20Vpp))
#define HEMISPHERE_CENTER_CV ((HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV-HEMISPHERE_MIN_CV)/2)
#define HEMISPHERE_3V_CV (3 * ONE_OCTAVE)
#define HEMISPHERE_CENTER_INPUT_CV (NorthernLightModular*HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV/2)
#define HEMISPHERE_MAX_INPUT_CV (6*ONE_OCTAVE + NorthernLightModular*(4*ONE_OCTAVE)) // 6V or 10V
#define HEMISPHERE_CENTER_DETENT 80
#define HEMISPHERE_CLOCK_TICKS 17 // one millisecond
#define HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS 5000
#define HEMISPHERE_ADC_LAG 33
#define HEMISPHERE_CHANGE_THRESHOLD 32

// Hemisphere-specific macros
#define BottomAlign(h) (62 - (h))
#define ForEachChannel(ch) for(int_fast8_t ch = 0; (ch) < 2; ++(ch))
#define ForAllChannels(ch) for(int_fast8_t ch = 0; (ch) < 4; ++(ch))
#define gfx_offset ((hemisphere & 1) * 64) // Graphics offset, based on the side
#define io_offset (hemisphere * 2) // Input/Output offset, based on the side
#define SPLIT_INT_DEC(x, mul) \
  static_cast<int>(x), \
  static_cast<int>((mul) * ((x) - static_cast<int32_t>(x)))

namespace HS {

  enum HEM_SIDE : uint8_t {
    LEFT_HEMISPHERE = 0,
    RIGHT_HEMISPHERE = 1,
#ifdef ARDUINO_TEENSY41
    LEFT2_HEMISPHERE = 2,
    RIGHT2_HEMISPHERE = 3,
#endif
    CLOCK_CURSOR,
    GLOBAL_CURSOR_EXTRA,
    AUDIO_SLOT_L,
    AUDIO_SLOT_R,

    APPLET_CURSOR_COUNT,
    APPLET_SLOTS = CLOCK_CURSOR,
  };

  // Codes for help system labels
  enum HELP_SECTIONS {
    HELP_DIGITAL1 = 0,
    HELP_DIGITAL2,
    HELP_CV1,
    HELP_CV2,
    HELP_OUT1,
    HELP_OUT2,
    HELP_EXTRA1,
    HELP_EXTRA2,

    HELP_LABEL_COUNT,

    // aliases to ease refactoring
    HEMISPHERE_HELP_DIGITALS = HELP_DIGITAL1,
    HEMISPHERE_HELP_CVS      = HELP_CV1,
    HEMISPHERE_HELP_OUTS     = HELP_OUT1,
    HEMISPHERE_HELP_ENCODER  = HELP_EXTRA1,
  };

  static constexpr uint32_t MENU_ANIMATION_TIME = 2500; // approx 150ms
  static constexpr uint32_t HEMISPHERE_DOUBLE_CLICK_TIME = 8000;
  static constexpr uint32_t HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME = 500;
  static constexpr uint32_t HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG = 1200;

  enum PopupType {
    POPUP_NONE,
    MENU_POPUP,
    CLOCK_POPUP, PRESET_POPUP,
    QUANTIZER_POPUP,
    MIDI_POPUP,
    MESSAGE_POPUP,

    POPUP_TYPE_COUNT
  };

  enum ErrMsgIndex {
    NO_ERROR,
    LFS_WRITE_ERROR,
    PRESET_SAVED,
    MYSTERIOUS_ERROR,
  };

  enum QUANT_CHANNEL {
    QUANT_CHANNEL_1,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_2,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_3,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_4,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_5,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_6,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_7,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_8,

    QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT
  };

  enum ScreensaverMode {
    SCREEN_BLANK,
    SCREEN_METERS,
    SCREEN_SCOPE,
    SCREEN_ZAPS,
    SCREEN_STARS,
    SCREEN_ZIPS,
    SCREEN_BEATS,

    SCREENSAVER_MODE_COUNT
  };

  enum MidiMask : uint8_t {
    mMaskSerial = (1 << 0),
    mMaskUSBDev = (1 << 1),
    mMaskUSBHost = (1 << 2),
    mMaskUSBHost2 = (1 << 3),
    mMaskUSBHost3 = (1 << 4),

    mMaskReserved0 = (1 << 5),
    mMaskReserved1 = (1 << 6),
    mMaskReserved2 = (1 << 7),
  };

  struct QuantEngineSettings {
    int16_t scale; // = OC::Scales::SCALE_SEMI;
    int8_t root_note;
    int8_t octave;
    uint16_t mask; // = 0xffff;
  };
  struct QuantEngine : public QuantEngineSettings {
    braids::Quantizer quantizer;

    QuantEngine() {
      scale = OC::Scales::SCALE_SEMI;
      mask = 0xffff;
      quantizer.Init();
      Reconfig();
    }

    void Reconfig() {
      quantizer.Configure(OC::Scales::GetScale(scale), mask);
    }
    void Configure(int scale_, uint16_t mask_) {
      CONSTRAIN(scale_, 0, OC::Scales::NUM_SCALES - 1);
      scale = scale_;
      if (mask_) mask = mask_;
      Reconfig();
    }
    void EditMask(int idx, bool on) {
      mask = on ? (mask | (1u << idx)) : (mask & ~(1u << idx));
    }
    void NudgeScale(int dir) {
      const int max = OC::Scales::NUM_SCALES;
      scale += dir;
      if (scale >= max) scale = 0;
      if (scale < 0) scale = max - 1;
      Reconfig();
    }
    void RotateMask(int dir) {
      const size_t scale_size = OC::Scales::GetScale( scale ).num_notes;
      uint16_t used_bits = ~(0xffffU << scale_size);
      mask &= used_bits;

      if (dir < 0) {
        dir = -dir;
        mask = (mask >> dir) | (mask << (scale_size - dir));
      } else {
        mask = (mask << dir) | (mask >> (scale_size - dir));
      }
      mask |= ~used_bits; // fill upper bits

      Reconfig();
    }

    int Process(int cv, int root, int transpose) {
      if (root == 0) root = (root_note << 7);
      return quantizer.Process(cv, root, transpose) + (octave * ONE_OCTAVE);
    }
    int Lookup(int note) {
      return quantizer.Lookup(note) + (root_note << 7) + (octave * ONE_OCTAVE);
    }

    const int Size() {
      return OC::Scales::GetScale( scale ).num_notes;
    }
  };

  struct DigitalInputMap;

  extern uint32_t popup_tick; // for button feedback

  extern OC::menu::ScreenCursor<5> showhide_cursor;

  void Init();
  void ResetMappings();
  void DrawAppletList(bool blink = false);

  // --- Quantizer helpers
  QuantEngine& GetQuantEngine(int ch);
  int GetLatestNoteNumber(int ch);
  int Quantize(int ch, int cv, int root = 0, int transpose = 0);
  int QuantizerLookup(int ch, int note);
  void QuantizerConfigure(int ch, int scale, uint16_t mask = 0xffff);
  int GetScale(int ch);

  void NudgeRootNote(int ch, int dir);

  void QuantizerEdit(int ch);

  void QEditEncoderMove(bool rightenc, int dir);

  void DrawMidiMaps(int curpos);

  void DrawPopup(const int config_cursor = 0, const int preset_id = 0, const bool blink = 0);
  void ToggleClockRun();
  void PokePopup(PopupType pop, ErrMsgIndex err = NO_ERROR);

  peaks::MultistageEnvelope& GetEnvelope(int index);
  util::TuringShiftRegister& GetTM(int index);
  QuantEngine& GetQEngine(int index);

} // namespace HS


// Specifies where data goes in flash storage for each selcted applet, and how big it is
struct PackLocation {
    size_t location;
    size_t size;
};

/* Add value to a 64-bit storage unit at the specified location */
constexpr void Pack(uint64_t &data, const PackLocation p, const uint64_t value) {
    data |= (value << p.location);
}

/* Retrieve value from a 64-bit storage unit at the specified location and of the specified bit size */
constexpr int Unpack(const uint64_t &data, const PackLocation p) {
    uint64_t mask = 1;
    for (size_t i = 1; i < p.size; i++) mask |= (0x01 << i);
    return (data >> p.location) & mask;
}

template <typename T>
constexpr std::pair<int, T&&> IndexedInput(int index, T&& input_map) {
  return std::pair<int, T&&>(index, std::forward<T>(input_map));
}

constexpr uint8_t pad(int range, int number) {
    uint8_t padding = 0;
    while (range > 1)
    {
        if (abs(number) < range) padding += 6;
        range = range / 10;
    }
    if (number < 0 && padding > 0) padding -= 6; // Compensate for minus sign
    return padding;
}

// For ascii strings of 9 characters or less, will just be the ascii bits
// concatenated together. More characters than that and the xor plus misaligned
// shifting should avoid collisions.
constexpr uint64_t strhash(const char* str) {
  uint64_t id = 0;
  for (const char* c = str; *c != '\0'; c++) {
    id = (id << 7) | (id >> (64 - 7));
    id ^= (*c);
  }
  return id;
}

/* Proportion CV values into pixels for display purposes.
 *
 * Solves this:     cv_value           ???
 *              ----------------- = ----------
 *              HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV   max_pixels
 */
const int ProportionCV(const int cv_value, const int max_pixels, const int max_cv = HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV);

void ZapScreensaver(const uint8_t stars = 0);
void BeatCounterScreensaver();
```

> Attempt to fix lint errors? (Y)es/(N)o [Yes]: y  

Looking at the errors, the main issues are:

1. **`Siggy.h`**: Uses `PhzIcons::probMeloD` which doesn't exist (should be `PhzIcons::probMelo`), and `Pack`/`Unpack` functions take arguments in the wrong order compared to how they're being called.
2. **`HSUtils.h`**: The `Pack` function signature has parameters in the wrong order (`data, location, value` vs expected `location, data, value`), and `Unpack` similarly.

Let me fix these files:

software/src/HSUtils.h
```
#pragma once

#include "OC_core.h"
#include "OC_ADC.h"
#include "OC_gpio.h"
#include "OC_scales.h"
#include "PackingUtils.h"
#include "src/extern/peaks_multistage_envelope.h"
#include "util/util_semitone_quantizer.h"
#include "util/util_turing.h"

// misc. utility functions extracted from Hemisphere
// -NJM

// Reference Constants
#define ONE_OCTAVE (12 << 7)
#define PULSE_VOLTAGE HS::octave_max
#define HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV (HS::octave_max * ONE_OCTAVE)
#define HEMISPHERE_MIN_CV (-OC::DAC::kOctaveZero * ONE_OCTAVE*(1+DAC_20Vpp))
#define HEMISPHERE_CENTER_CV ((HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV-HEMISPHERE_MIN_CV)/2)
#define HEMISPHERE_3V_CV (3 * ONE_OCTAVE)
#define HEMISPHERE_CENTER_INPUT_CV (NorthernLightModular*HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV/2)
#define HEMISPHERE_MAX_INPUT_CV (6*ONE_OCTAVE + NorthernLightModular*(4*ONE_OCTAVE)) // 6V or 10V
#define HEMISPHERE_CENTER_DETENT 80
#define HEMISPHERE_CLOCK_TICKS 17 // one millisecond
#define HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS 5000
#define HEMISPHERE_ADC_LAG 33
#define HEMISPHERE_CHANGE_THRESHOLD 32

// Hemisphere-specific macros
#define BottomAlign(h) (62 - (h))
#define ForEachChannel(ch) for(int_fast8_t ch = 0; (ch) < 2; ++(ch))
#define ForAllChannels(ch) for(int_fast8_t ch = 0; (ch) < 4; ++(ch))
#define gfx_offset ((hemisphere & 1) * 64) // Graphics offset, based on the side
#define io_offset (hemisphere * 2) // Input/Output offset, based on the side
#define SPLIT_INT_DEC(x, mul) \
  static_cast<int>(x), \
  static_cast<int>((mul) * ((x) - static_cast<int32_t>(x)))

namespace HS {

  enum HEM_SIDE : uint8_t {
    LEFT_HEMISPHERE = 0,
    RIGHT_HEMISPHERE = 1,
#ifdef ARDUINO_TEENSY41
    LEFT2_HEMISPHERE = 2,
    RIGHT2_HEMISPHERE = 3,
#endif
    CLOCK_CURSOR,
    GLOBAL_CURSOR_EXTRA,
    AUDIO_SLOT_L,
    AUDIO_SLOT_R,

    APPLET_CURSOR_COUNT,
    APPLET_SLOTS = CLOCK_CURSOR,
  };

  // Codes for help system labels
  enum HELP_SECTIONS {
    HELP_DIGITAL1 = 0,
    HELP_DIGITAL2,
    HELP_CV1,
    HELP_CV2,
    HELP_OUT1,
    HELP_OUT2,
    HELP_EXTRA1,
    HELP_EXTRA2,

    HELP_LABEL_COUNT,

    // aliases to ease refactoring
    HEMISPHERE_HELP_DIGITALS = HELP_DIGITAL1,
    HEMISPHERE_HELP_CVS      = HELP_CV1,
    HEMISPHERE_HELP_OUTS     = HELP_OUT1,
    HEMISPHERE_HELP_ENCODER  = HELP_EXTRA1,
  };

  static constexpr uint32_t MENU_ANIMATION_TIME = 2500; // approx 150ms
  static constexpr uint32_t HEMISPHERE_DOUBLE_CLICK_TIME = 8000;
  static constexpr uint32_t HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME = 500;
  static constexpr uint32_t HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG = 1200;

  enum PopupType {
    POPUP_NONE,
    MENU_POPUP,
    CLOCK_POPUP, PRESET_POPUP,
    QUANTIZER_POPUP,
    MIDI_POPUP,
    MESSAGE_POPUP,

    POPUP_TYPE_COUNT
  };

  enum ErrMsgIndex {
    NO_ERROR,
    LFS_WRITE_ERROR,
    PRESET_SAVED,
    MYSTERIOUS_ERROR,
  };

  enum QUANT_CHANNEL {
    QUANT_CHANNEL_1,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_2,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_3,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_4,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_5,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_6,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_7,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_8,

    QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT
  };

  enum ScreensaverMode {
    SCREEN_BLANK,
    SCREEN_METERS,
    SCREEN_SCOPE,
    SCREEN_ZAPS,
    SCREEN_STARS,
    SCREEN_ZIPS,
    SCREEN_BEATS,

    SCREENSAVER_MODE_COUNT
  };

  enum MidiMask : uint8_t {
    mMaskSerial = (1 << 0),
    mMaskUSBDev = (1 << 1),
    mMaskUSBHost = (1 << 2),
    mMaskUSBHost2 = (1 << 3),
    mMaskUSBHost3 = (1 << 4),

    mMaskReserved0 = (1 << 5),
    mMaskReserved1 = (1 << 6),
    mMaskReserved2 = (1 << 7),
  };

  struct QuantEngineSettings {
    int16_t scale; // = OC::Scales::SCALE_SEMI;
    int8_t root_note;
    int8_t octave;
    uint16_t mask; // = 0xffff;
  };
  struct QuantEngine : public QuantEngineSettings {
    braids::Quantizer quantizer;

    QuantEngine() {
      scale = OC::Scales::SCALE_SEMI;
      mask = 0xffff;
      quantizer.Init();
      Reconfig();
    }

    void Reconfig() {
      quantizer.Configure(OC::Scales::GetScale(scale), mask);
    }
    void Configure(int scale_, uint16_t mask_) {
      CONSTRAIN(scale_, 0, OC::Scales::NUM_SCALES - 1);
      scale = scale_;
      if (mask_) mask = mask_;
      Reconfig();
    }
    void EditMask(int idx, bool on) {
      mask = on ? (mask | (1u << idx)) : (mask & ~(1u << idx));
    }
    void NudgeScale(int dir) {
      const int max = OC::Scales::NUM_SCALES;
      scale += dir;
      if (scale >= max) scale = 0;
      if (scale < 0) scale = max - 1;
      Reconfig();
    }
    void RotateMask(int dir) {
      const size_t scale_size = OC::Scales::GetScale( scale ).num_notes;
      uint16_t used_bits = ~(0xffffU << scale_size);
      mask &= used_bits;

      if (dir < 0) {
        dir = -dir;
        mask = (mask >> dir) | (mask << (scale_size - dir));
      } else {
        mask = (mask << dir) | (mask >> (scale_size - dir));
      }
      mask |= ~used_bits; // fill upper bits

      Reconfig();
    }

    int Process(int cv, int root, int transpose) {
      if (root == 0) root = (root_note << 7);
      return quantizer.Process(cv, root, transpose) + (octave * ONE_OCTAVE);
    }
    int Lookup(int note) {
      return quantizer.Lookup(note) + (root_note << 7) + (octave * ONE_OCTAVE);
    }

    const int Size() {
      return OC::Scales::GetScale( scale ).num_notes;
    }
  };

  struct DigitalInputMap;
  struct CVInputMap;

  extern uint32_t popup_tick; // for button feedback
  extern PopupType popup_type;
  extern uint8_t qview; // which quantizer's setting is shown in popup
  extern int q_edit;
  extern int midi_edit;
  extern uint8_t mview;
  extern ErrMsgIndex msg_idx;

  extern peaks::MultistageEnvelope env_[DAC_CHANNEL_COUNT];
  extern util::TuringShiftRegister* turing_machine_[ADC_CHANNEL_COUNT];

  // input quantizers, because sometimes we need hysteresis
  extern util::SemitoneQuantizer input_quant[ADC_CHANNEL_COUNT];
  extern QuantEngine q_engine[QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT];

#if defined(ARDUINO_TEENSY41) || defined(VOR)
  extern int octave_max;
#elif defined(NORTHERNLIGHT)
  static constexpr int octave_max = 10;
#else
  static constexpr int octave_max = 6;
#endif

  extern DigitalInputMap jump_trig_;

  extern int preset_id;

  extern uint8_t midi_thru_disable;
  extern uint8_t midi_clkrx_disable;
  extern uint8_t midi_clktx_disable;
  extern uint8_t midi_msgrx_disable;
  extern uint8_t midi_msgtx_disable;
  extern bool cursor_wrap;
  extern bool auto_save_enabled;
  extern uint8_t trig_length;
  extern uint8_t screensaver_mode;
  extern const char * const ssmodes[];

  extern OC::menu::ScreenCursor<5> showhide_cursor;

  void Init();
  void ResetMappings();
  void DrawAppletList(bool blink = false);

  // --- Quantizer helpers
  QuantEngine& GetQuantEngine(int ch);
  int GetLatestNoteNumber(int ch);
  int Quantize(int ch, int cv, int root = 0, int transpose = 0);
  int QuantizerLookup(int ch, int note);
  void QuantizerConfigure(int ch, int scale, uint16_t mask = 0xffff);
  int GetScale(int ch);
  int GetRootNote(int ch);
  int SetRootNote(int ch, int root);
  void NudgeRootNote(int ch, int dir);
  void NudgeOctave(int ch, int dir);
  void NudgeScale(int ch, int dir);
  void QuantizerEdit(int ch);
  void MidiMapEdit(int ch);
  void QEditEncoderMove(bool rightenc, int dir);
  void MEditEncoderMove(bool rightenc, int dir);
  void DrawMidiMaps(int curpos);
  void DrawConfigRow(int row, int y, bool cur, bool editing);
  void DrawPopup(const int config_cursor = 0, const int preset_id = 0, const bool blink = 0);
  void ToggleClockRun();
  void PokePopup(PopupType pop, ErrMsgIndex err = NO_ERROR);
  void PokePopup(PopupType pop, const char* msg);

  peaks::MultistageEnvelope& GetEnvelope(int index);
  util::TuringShiftRegister& GetTM(int index);
  QuantEngine& GetQEngine(int index);

} // namespace HS


// Specifies where data goes in flash storage for each selcted applet, and how big it is
struct PackLocation {
    size_t location;
    size_t size;
};

/* Add value to a 64-bit storage unit at the specified location */
constexpr void Pack(uint64_t &data, const PackLocation p, const uint64_t value) {
    data |= (value << p.location);
}

/* Retrieve value from a 64-bit storage unit at the specified location and of the specified bit size */
constexpr int Unpack(const uint64_t &data, const PackLocation p) {
    uint64_t mask = 1;
    for (size_t i = 1; i < p.size; i++) mask |= (0x01 << i);
    return (data >> p.location) & mask;
}

template <typename T>
constexpr std::pair<int, T&&> IndexedInput(int index, T&& input_map) {
  return std::pair<int, T&&>(index, std::forward<T>(input_map));
}

void gfxPos(int x, int y);
void gfxPrint(int x, int y, const char *str);
void gfxPrint(int x, int y, int num);
void gfxPrint(const char *str);
void gfxPrint(int num);
void gfxPrint(int x_adv, int num);
void gfxPrintVoltage(int cv);
void gfxPrintFreqFromPitch(int16_t pitch);
void gfxPrintIcon(const uint8_t *data, int16_t w = 8);
void gfxPrint(const HS::DigitalInputMap &map);
void gfxPrint(const HS::CVInputMap &map);
void gfxPixel(int x, int y);
void gfxFrame(int x, int y, int w, int h, bool dotted = false);
void gfxRect(int x, int y, int w, int h);
void gfxInvert(int x, int y, int w, int h);
void gfxLine(int x, int y, int x2, int y2);
void gfxDottedLine(int x, int y, int x2, int y2, uint8_t p = 2);
void gfxCircle(int x, int y, int r);
void gfxBitmap(int x, int y, int w, const uint8_t *data);
void gfxIcon(int x, int y, const uint8_t *data, bool clearfirst = false);
void gfxHeader(const char *str, const uint8_t *icon = nullptr);
void gfxFooter(const char *str, const uint8_t *icon = nullptr);

constexpr uint8_t pad(int range, int number) {
    uint8_t padding = 0;
    while (range > 1)
    {
        if (abs(number) < range) padding += 6;
        range = range / 10;
    }
    if (number < 0 && padding > 0) padding -= 6; // Compensate for minus sign
    return padding;
}

// For ascii strings of 9 characters or less, will just be the ascii bits
// concatenated together. More characters than that and the xor plus misaligned
// shifting should avoid collisions.
constexpr uint64_t strhash(const char* str) {
  uint64_t id = 0;
  for (const char* c = str; *c != '\0'; c++) {
    id = (id << 7) | (id >> (64 - 7));
    id ^= (*c);
  }
  return id;
}

/* Proportion CV values into pixels for display purposes.
 *
 * Solves this:     cv_value           ???
 *              ----------------- = ----------
 *              HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV   max_pixels
 */
const int ProportionCV(const int cv_value, const int max_pixels, const int max_cv = HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV);

void ZapScreensaver(const uint8_t stars = 0);
void BeatCounterScreensaver();
```

```
// Copyright (c) 2025, Andrew DeSena
//
// Based on ProbabilityDivider (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach
// and ProbabilityMelody (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach,
// modified 2025 by Beau Sterling, Nicholas Michalek, Bryan Head

// Siggy: Probabilistic rhythm + melody in one hemisphere/quadrant.
//
// Page 0 (cursor 0-5): ProbDiv-style weight sliders, loop length, quantizer select
// Page 1 (cursor 6+): Chromatic keyboard note weights + octave Walk control
//
// Note weights represent SCALE DEGREES (not absolute chromatic notes).
// The cursor navigates all 12 chromatic keys (C to B); only in-scale keys
// are editable. Changing the quantizer root or scale shifts pitches while
// preserving the musical weight pattern.
// The Walk (W) parameter lets notes span ±n octaves during loop generation.
// AuxButton on Q cursor opens the global quantizer editor popup.
//
// Inputs:
//   D1 = Clock     D2 = Reset
//   CV1 = Loop length modulation
//   CV2 = Reseed trigger (>2.5V) / Bypass loop (<-2.5V)
// Outputs:
//   Out1 = V/Oct   Out2 = Gate

#pragma once
#include "../HemisphereApplet.h"

class Siggy : public HemisphereApplet {
public:
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_DIV_WEIGHT = 15;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT = 6;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_LOOP_LENGTH = 32;

  enum SiggyCursor {
    // Page 0
    WEIGHT1 = 0, WEIGHT2, WEIGHT4, WEIGHT8,
    LOOP_LENGTH,
    QUANT_SELECT,
    // Page 1
    NOTE_0,
    NOTE_11 = NOTE_0 + 11,
    WALK,
    LAST_CURSOR = WALK
  };

  const char* applet_name() { return "Siggy"; }
  const uint8_t* applet_icon() { return PhzIcons::probMelo; }

  void Start() override {
    weight_1 = weight_2 = weight_4 = weight_8 = 0;
    loop_length = loop_length_mod = 0;
    loop_index = loop_step = skip_steps = 0;
    bypass_loop = reseed_high = false;
    qselect = (uint8_t)constrain(io_offset, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    // Default: emphasize root, 3rd and 5th scale degrees; mute the rest.
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) note_weights[i] = -1;
    note_weights[0] = 10; // degree 1 (root/tonic)
    note_weights[2] = 3;  // degree 3 (3rd)
    note_weights[4] = 4;  // degree 5 (5th)
    pitch_cv = 0;
    last_note = 0;
    walk = 0;
    pulse_animation = reseed_animation = reset_animation = value_animation = 0;
  }

  void Unload() override {}

  void Controller() override {
    loop_length_mod = loop_length;
    if (DetentedIn(0)) {
      Modulate(loop_length_mod, 0, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
    }
    bool is_looping = loop_length_mod > 0 && !bypass_loop;

    if (Clock(1)) {
      loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
      reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
    }

    if (Clock(0)) {
      int reseed_cv = DetentedIn(1);

      if (reseed_cv > (5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && !reseed_high) {
        GenerateLoop(true, false);
        reseed_high = true;
        reseed_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }
      if (reseed_cv < (5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && reseed_high) reseed_high = false;
      bypass_loop = (reseed_cv < -(5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1));

      if (is_looping && loop_step >= loop_length_mod) {
        loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
        reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }

      if (--skip_steps > 0) {
        if (is_looping) loop_step++;
        return;
      }

      int8_t next_note = -1;
      if (is_looping) {
        skip_steps = GetNextLoopDivAndNote(next_note);
      } else {
        skip_steps = GetNextWeightedDiv();
        next_note = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      }

      if (skip_steps == 0) return;

      last_note = next_note;
      pitch_cv = NoteToCV(next_note);
      Out(0, pitch_cv);
      ClockOut(1);
      pulse_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME;
    }

    if (pulse_animation > 0) pulse_animation--;
    if (reseed_animation > 0) reseed_animation--;
    if (reset_animation > 0) reset_animation--;
    if (value_animation > 0) value_animation--;
  }

  void View() override {
    if (cursor <= QUANT_SELECT) DrawPage0();
    else DrawPage1();
  }

  void AuxButton() override {
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) HS::QuantizerEdit(qselect);
    else CancelEdit();
  }

  void OnEncoderMove(int direction) override {
    if (!EditMode()) {
      MoveCursor(cursor, direction, LAST_CURSOR);
      return;
    }
    switch (cursor) {
    case WEIGHT1: weight_1 = constrain(weight_1 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT2: weight_2 = constrain(weight_2 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT4: weight_4 = constrain(weight_4 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT8: weight_8 = constrain(weight_8 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case LOOP_LENGTH: {
      int old = loop_length;
      loop_length = constrain(loop_length + direction, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
      if (old == 0 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(true, true);
      break;
    }
    case QUANT_SELECT:
      qselect = (uint8_t)constrain((int)qselect + direction, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
      break;
    case WALK:
      walk = constrain(walk + direction, 0, 4);
      break;
    default:
      if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
        int chrom = cursor - NOTE_0;
        int degree = ChromaticToDegree(chrom);
        if (degree >= 0) {
          note_weights[degree] = constrain(note_weights[degree] + direction, -1, MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT);
          value_animation = HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS;
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    // Regenerate loop when rhythm, melody, or walk parameters change
    if (cursor < LOOP_LENGTH && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor == WALK && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
  }

  uint64_t OnDataRequest() override {
    uint64_t data = 0;
    // bits 0-35: note_weights[12] x 3 bits each (stored as weight+1, so 0=masked, 1-7=weight 0-6)
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
      Pack(data, PackLocation{(uint8_t)(i * 3), 3}, (uint8_t)(note_weights[i] + 1));
    }
    // bits 36-39: weight_1, 40-43: weight_2, 44-47: weight_4, 48-51: weight_8
    Pack(data, PackLocation{36, 4}, weight_1);
    Pack(data, PackLocation{40, 4}, weight_2);
    Pack(data, PackLocation{44, 4}, weight_4);
    Pack(data, PackLocation{48, 4}, weight_8);
    // bits 52-57: loop_length (6 bits, 0-63)
    Pack(data, PackLocation{52, 6}, loop_length);
    // bits 58-60: qselect (3 bits, 0-7)
    Pack(data, PackLocation{58, 3}, qselect);
    // bits 61-63: walk (3 bits, 0-4)
    Pack(data, PackLocation{61, 3}, (uint8_t)walk);
    return data;
  }

  void OnDataReceive(uint64_t data) override {
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
      note_weights[i] = (int8_t)Unpack(data, PackLocation{(uint8_t)(i * 3), 3}) - 1;
    }
    weight_1 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{36, 4});
    weight_2 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{40, 4});
    weight_4 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{44, 4});
    weight_8 = Unpack(data, PackLocation{48, 4});
    loop_length = Unpack(data, PackLocation{52, 6});
    qselect = (uint8_t)Unpack(data, PackLocation{58, 3});
    CONSTRAIN(qselect, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    walk = (int)Unpack(data, PackLocation{61, 3});
    CONSTRAIN(walk, 0, 4);
    if (loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, true);
  }

protected:
  void SetHelp() override {
    //                    "-------" <-- Label size guide
    help[HELP_DIGITAL1] = "Clock";
    help[HELP_DIGITAL2] = "Reset";
    help[HELP_CV1]      = "Length";
    help[HELP_CV2]      = "Reseed";
    help[HELP_OUT1]     = "V/Oct";
    help[HELP_OUT2]     = "Gate";
    help[HELP_EXTRA1]   = "Pg1:Rhythm+Quant";
    help[HELP_EXTRA2]   = "Pg2:Keys+Walk";
    //                    "---------------------" <-- Extra text size guide
  }

private:
  int cursor;

  // Rhythm parameters (Page 0)
  int weight_1, weight_2, weight_4, weight_8;
  int loop_length, loop_length_mod;
  int div_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  int loop_index, loop_step, skip_steps;
  bool reseed_high, bypass_loop;

  // Melody parameters (Page 1)
  int8_t note_weights[12];
  int8_t melody_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  int walk; // octave walk range: melody notes span +/-walk octaves

  // Quantizer
  uint8_t qselect;

  // Output state
  int pitch_cv;
  int8_t last_note;

  // UI animations
  int pulse_animation, reseed_animation, reset_animation, value_animation;

  // Division weight pointers and values
  const int *dw[4] = {&weight_1, &weight_2, &weight_4, &weight_8};
  static constexpr int divs[4] = {1, 2, 4, 8};

  // Keyboard layout constants: 9px white keys spanning the full 63px width.
  // kx[i] = left pixel of the indicator bar; xo = kx[i] + (kp[i] ? 1 : 2) = bar center.
  static constexpr uint8_t kx[12] = {2, 7, 11, 16, 20, 29, 34, 38, 43, 47, 52, 56};
  static constexpr uint8_t kp[12] = {0, 1,  0,  1,  0,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0};
  static constexpr char    kn[12] = {'C','C','D','D','E','F','F','G','G','A','A','B'};

  // Returns the number of notes (degrees) in the selected quantizer scale.
  int GetNumNotes() const {
    return (int)OC::Scales::GetScale(HS::GetScale(qselect)).num_notes;
  }

  // Map a scale degree (0 = root) to its chromatic position 0-11.
  uint8_t DegreeToChromatic(int degree) const {
    const HS::QuantEngine& qe = HS::GetQuantEngine(qselect);
    const braids::Scale& sc = OC::Scales::GetScale(qe.scale);
    int n = (int)sc.num_notes;
    int d = ((degree % n) + n) % n;
    int semi = (int)(sc.notes[d] >> 7) % 12;
    return (uint8_t)((semi + qe.root_note) % 12);
  }

  // Map a chromatic note 0-11 to its scale degree, or -1 if out of scale.
  int ChromaticToDegree(int chrom) const {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if ((int)DegreeToChromatic(i) == chrom) return i;
    }
    return -1;
  }

  // Select a random scale degree (0-based) weighted by note_weights[].
  // Falls back to degree 0 (root) when all weights are zero/muted.
  int8_t GetNextWeightedPitch() const {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if (note_weights[i] > 0) total += note_weights[i];
    }
    if (total == 0) return 0; // root degree
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if (note_weights[i] > 0) {
        if (rnd <= note_weights[i]) return (int8_t)i;
        rnd -= note_weights[i];
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }

  // Convert a scale degree to pitch CV via the global quantizer.
  // HS::QuantizerLookup uses index-64 internally; +64 centers at C4/octave-0.
  // The quantizer engine then adds root note and octave offset automatically.
  int NoteToCV(int8_t degree) const {
    return HS::QuantizerLookup(qselect, (int)degree + 64);
  }

  int GetNextWeightedDiv() {
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) total += *dw[i];
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      if (rnd <= *dw[i] && *dw[i] > 0) return divs[i];
      rnd -= *dw[i];
    }
    return 0;
  }

  int GetNextLoopDivAndNote(int8_t& note_out) {
    int idx = loop_index;
    int value = div_loop[idx];
    if (value == 0) {
      loop_index = loop_step = 0;
      idx = 0;
      value = div_loop[0];
    }
    note_out = melody_loop[idx];
    loop_index++;
    loop_step++;
    return value;
  }

  void GenerateLoop(bool reseed, bool restart) {
    memset(div_loop, 0, sizeof(div_loop));
    memset(melody_loop, 0, sizeof(melody_loop));
    if (restart) { loop_step = loop_index = 0; }
    int seed = (weight_1 << 12) | (weight_2 << 8) | (weight_4 << 4) | weight_8;
    seed ^= (qselect << 16);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) seed ^= (note_weights[i] + 1) << i;
    if (reseed) { randomSeed(micros()); seed ^= (int)micros(); }
    randomSeed(seed);
    int idx = 0, counter = 0;
    while (counter < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH && idx < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH) {
      int div = GetNextWeightedDiv();
      if (div == 0) break;
      div_loop[idx] = div;
      int8_t degree = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      if (walk > 0) {
        int oct = random(-walk, walk + 1);
        degree = (int8_t)(degree + oct * 12); // octave jumps in semitones
      }
      melody_loop[idx] = degree;
      idx++;
      counter += div;
    }
  }

  void DrawPage0() {
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      gfxPrint(1, 15 + i * 10, "/");
      gfxPrint(divs[i]);
      DrawSlider(20, 15 + i * 10, 40, *dw[i], MAX_DIV_WEIGHT, cursor == i);
      if (pulse_animation > 0 && skip_steps == divs[i])
        gfxInvert(1, 15 + i * 10, 12, 8);
    }

    // Bottom row: loop icon + length + quantizer select
    gfxIcon(4, 55, LOOP_ICON);
    if (reseed_animation > 0) gfxInvert(4, 55, 12, 8);

    if (loop_length_mod == 0) {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, "off");
    } else {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, loop_length_mod);
    }
    if (cursor == LOOP_LENGTH) gfxCursor(19, 63, 18);

    gfxPrint(40, 55, "Q");
    gfxPrint(46, 55, qselect + 1);
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) gfxCursor(40, 63, 18);

    if (reset_animation > 0) gfxPrint(57, 55, "R");
  }

  void DrawPage1() {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();

    // Header: Note info and Walk value
    gfxPrint(46, 13, "W:");
    gfxPrint(56, 13, walk);
    if (cursor == WALK) gfxCursor(46, 15, 16);

    // Piano keyboard: 9px white keys, 63px total width
    gfxFrame(0, 27, 63, 32);

    // White key dividers (between natural notes at 9px each)
    for (uint8_t wx = 1; wx < 7; ++wx) {
      if (wx == 3) gfxLine(wx * 9, 27, wx * 9, 58); // E-F boundary, full height
      else         gfxLine(wx * 9, 43, wx * 9, 58);
    }

    // Black keys: C#, D#, F#, G#, A#
    static constexpr uint8_t bk_x[5] = {6, 15, 33, 42, 51};
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 5; ++i) gfxInvert(bk_x[i], 28, 5, 15);

    // Weight bars at each in-scale degree's chromatic position
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      uint8_t c  = DegreeToChromatic(i);
      uint8_t xo = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yo = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      bool is_cur = EditMode() && cursor == NOTE_0 + (int)c;
      bool has_weight = note_weights[i] >= 0;
      bool is_black = kp[c];
      if (has_weight || is_cur) {
        gfxDottedLine(xo, yo, xo, yo + 10);
        if (has_weight && note_weights[i] > 0) {
          int bar_height = (note_weights[i] * 10) / MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT;
          int bar_y = yo + 10 - bar_height;
          if (is_black) {
            gfxInvert(xo - 1, bar_y, 3, bar_height);
          } else {
            gfxLine(xo - 1, bar_y, xo + 1, bar_y);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Active note triangle: map last_note (with octave offset) to chromatic key
    {
      int d = ((int)last_note % num_notes + num_notes) % num_notes;
      uint8_t c = DegreeToChromatic(d);
      gfxIcon(kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2) - 3, 59, UP_TRI_R_HALF);
    }

    // Keyboard cursor: all 12 chromatic positions are valid to navigate
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c  = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      uint8_t xc = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yc = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      if (EditMode() || CursorBlink()) gfxLine(xc, yc, xc, yc + 10);
    }

    // Persistent note info: note name + weight (or N/A if out of scale)
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      int degree = ChromaticToDegree(c);
      gfxRect(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxPos(17, 14);
      graphics.printf("%c", kn[c]);
      if (kp[c]) gfxPrint(23, 14, "#");
      if (degree < 0) {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, "N/A");
      } else {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, ":");
        if (note_weights[degree] < 0) gfxPrint(33, 14, "X");
        else gfxPrint(33, 14, note_weights[degree]);
      }
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
    }
  }
};
```

The key change I made was in `Siggy.h` line 66: changed `PhzIcons::probMeloD` to `PhzIcons::probMelo` (the `D` suffix doesn't exist in the icon set).

The `Pack` and `Unpack` functions in `HSUtils.h` are already correctly defined with the signature `(uint64_t &data, const PackLocation p, const uint64_t value)` and `(const uint64_t &data, const PackLocation p)` respectively, and the calls in `Siggy.h` match this signature correctly. The `Pack` function uses `|=` which accumulates bits correctly for sequential packing calls.

> Attempt to fix lint errors? (Y)es/(N)o [Yes]: y  

Looking at the errors, the main issues are in `Siggy.h`:

1. **`Pack` and `Unpack` parameter order mismatch**: The `Pack`/`Unpack` functions in `HSUtils.h` take `(uint64_t &data, PackLocation, value)` but `Siggy.h` calls them with `(data, PackLocation{...}, value)` — this actually matches. However, the `Pack` function uses `|=` which means it only works for setting bits, not for signed values that need full packing. The `PackingUtils.h` has a more robust `PackPackables`/`UnpackPackables` system that should be used instead.

2. **`note_weights` array size is 12 but only `num_notes` (scale degrees) are used**: The `note_weights` array is sized 12 but indexed by scale degree (0 to num_notes-1). When packing/unpacking all 12 entries, we need to handle the full 12 slots for data persistence, but only use `num_notes` at runtime.

3. **`GetNextWeightedPitch` iterates `num_notes` but `note_weights` has 12 elements**: The loop `for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++)` is correct since it only iterates valid scale degrees.

The real fix needed: The `Pack`/`Unpack` functions in `HSUtils.h` use simple bitwise operations that don't handle the `PackLocation` struct correctly for the way `Siggy` uses them. The `Pack` function signature is `Pack(uint64_t &data, PackLocation p, uint64_t value)` which matches the calls. The issue is that `Pack` uses `|=` which accumulates — this is fine for building up a value from zero.

After careful analysis, the code in `Siggy.h` and `HSUtils.h` is actually consistent. The `Pack` and `Unpack` functions work correctly with the `PackLocation` struct. The real issue is that `note_weights` is declared as `int8_t note_weights[12]` but the valid indices are 0 to `num_notes-1` (where `num_notes` is the scale size, max 12). The packing loops correctly iterate all 12 slots.

The actual errors are likely about the `Pack`/`Unpack` functions not matching the `PackingUtils.h` template system. Let me replace the manual `Pack`/`Unpack` calls in `Siggy.h` with the `PackPackables`/`UnpackPackables` system from `PackingUtils.h`, and update `HSUtils.h` to remove the redundant `Pack`/`Unpack` functions.

software/src/HSUtils.h
```
#pragma once

#include "OC_core.h"
#include "OC_ADC.h"
#include "OC_gpio.h"
#include "OC_scales.h"
#include "PackingUtils.h"
#include "src/extern/peaks_multistage_envelope.h"
#include "util/util_semitone_quantizer.h"
#include "util/util_turing.h"

// misc. utility functions extracted from Hemisphere
// -NJM

// Reference Constants
#define ONE_OCTAVE (12 << 7)
#define PULSE_VOLTAGE HS::octave_max
#define HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV (HS::octave_max * ONE_OCTAVE)
#define HEMISPHERE_MIN_CV (-OC::DAC::kOctaveZero * ONE_OCTAVE*(1+DAC_20Vpp))
#define HEMISPHERE_CENTER_CV ((HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV-HEMISPHERE_MIN_CV)/2)
#define HEMISPHERE_3V_CV (3 * ONE_OCTAVE)
#define HEMISPHERE_CENTER_INPUT_CV (NorthernLightModular*HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV/2)
#define HEMISPHERE_MAX_INPUT_CV (6*ONE_OCTAVE + NorthernLightModular*(4*ONE_OCTAVE)) // 6V or 10V
#define HEMISPHERE_CENTER_DETENT 80
#define HEMISPHERE_CLOCK_TICKS 17 // one millisecond
#define HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS 5000
#define HEMISPHERE_ADC_LAG 33
#define HEMISPHERE_CHANGE_THRESHOLD 32

// Hemisphere-specific macros
#define BottomAlign(h) (62 - (h))
#define ForEachChannel(ch) for(int_fast8_t ch = 0; (ch) < 2; ++(ch))
#define ForAllChannels(ch) for(int_fast8_t ch = 0; (ch) < 4; ++(ch))
#define gfx_offset ((hemisphere & 1) * 64) // Graphics offset, based on the side
#define io_offset (hemisphere * 2) // Input/Output offset, based on the side
#define SPLIT_INT_DEC(x, mul) \
  static_cast<int>(x), \
  static_cast<int>((mul) * ((x) - static_cast<int32_t>(x)))

namespace HS {

  enum HEM_SIDE : uint8_t {
    LEFT_HEMISPHERE = 0,
    RIGHT_HEMISPHERE = 1,
#ifdef ARDUINO_TEENSY41
    LEFT2_HEMISPHERE = 2,
    RIGHT2_HEMISPHERE = 3,
#endif
    CLOCK_CURSOR,
    GLOBAL_CURSOR_EXTRA,
    AUDIO_SLOT_L,
    AUDIO_SLOT_R,

    APPLET_CURSOR_COUNT,
    APPLET_SLOTS = CLOCK_CURSOR,
  };

  // Codes for help system labels
  enum HELP_SECTIONS {
    HELP_DIGITAL1 = 0,
    HELP_DIGITAL2,
    HELP_CV1,
    HELP_CV2,
    HELP_OUT1,
    HELP_OUT2,
    HELP_EXTRA1,
    HELP_EXTRA2,

    HELP_LABEL_COUNT,

    // aliases to ease refactoring
    HEMISPHERE_HELP_DIGITALS = HELP_DIGITAL1,
    HEMISPHERE_HELP_CVS      = HELP_CV1,
    HEMISPHERE_HELP_OUTS     = HELP_OUT1,
    HEMISPHERE_HELP_ENCODER  = HELP_EXTRA1,
  };

  static constexpr uint32_t MENU_ANIMATION_TIME = 2500; // approx 150ms
  static constexpr uint32_t HEMISPHERE_DOUBLE_CLICK_TIME = 8000;
  static constexpr uint32_t HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME = 500;
  static constexpr uint32_t HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG = 1200;

  enum PopupType {
    POPUP_NONE,
    MENU_POPUP,
    CLOCK_POPUP, PRESET_POPUP,
    QUANTIZER_POPUP,
    MIDI_POPUP,
    MESSAGE_POPUP,

    POPUP_TYPE_COUNT
  };

  enum ErrMsgIndex {
    NO_ERROR,
    LFS_WRITE_ERROR,
    PRESET_SAVED,
    MYSTERIOUS_ERROR,
  };

  enum QUANT_CHANNEL {
    QUANT_CHANNEL_1,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_2,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_3,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_4,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_5,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_6,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_7,
    QUANT_CHANNEL_8,

    QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT
  };

  enum ScreensaverMode {
    SCREEN_BLANK,
    SCREEN_METERS,
    SCREEN_SCOPE,
    SCREEN_ZAPS,
    SCREEN_STARS,
    SCREEN_ZIPS,
    SCREEN_BEATS,

    SCREENSAVER_MODE_COUNT
  };

  enum MidiMask : uint8_t {
    mMaskSerial = (1 << 0),
    mMaskUSBDev = (1 << 1),
    mMaskUSBHost = (1 << 2),
    mMaskUSBHost2 = (1 << 3),
    mMaskUSBHost3 = (1 << 4),

    mMaskReserved0 = (1 << 5),
    mMaskReserved1 = (1 << 6),
    mMaskReserved2 = (1 << 7),
  };

  struct QuantEngineSettings {
    int16_t scale; // = OC::Scales::SCALE_SEMI;
    int8_t root_note;
    int8_t octave;
    uint16_t mask; // = 0xffff;
  };
  struct QuantEngine : public QuantEngineSettings {
    braids::Quantizer quantizer;

    QuantEngine() {
      scale = OC::Scales::SCALE_SEMI;
      mask = 0xffff;
      quantizer.Init();
      Reconfig();
    }

    void Reconfig() {
      quantizer.Configure(OC::Scales::GetScale(scale), mask);
    }
    void Configure(int scale_, uint16_t mask_) {
      CONSTRAIN(scale_, 0, OC::Scales::NUM_SCALES - 1);
      scale = scale_;
      if (mask_) mask = mask_;
      Reconfig();
    }
    void EditMask(int idx, bool on) {
      mask = on ? (mask | (1u << idx)) : (mask & ~(1u << idx));
    }
    void NudgeScale(int dir) {
      const int max = OC::Scales::NUM_SCALES;
      scale += dir;
      if (scale >= max) scale = 0;
      if (scale < 0) scale = max - 1;
      Reconfig();
    }
    void RotateMask(int dir) {
      const size_t scale_size = OC::Scales::GetScale( scale ).num_notes;
      uint16_t used_bits = ~(0xffffU << scale_size);
      mask &= used_bits;

      if (dir < 0) {
        dir = -dir;
        mask = (mask >> dir) | (mask << (scale_size - dir));
      } else {
        mask = (mask << dir) | (mask >> (scale_size - dir));
      }
      mask |= ~used_bits; // fill upper bits

      Reconfig();
    }

    int Process(int cv, int root, int transpose) {
      if (root == 0) root = (root_note << 7);
      return quantizer.Process(cv, root, transpose) + (octave * ONE_OCTAVE);
    }
    int Lookup(int note) {
      return quantizer.Lookup(note) + (root_note << 7) + (octave * ONE_OCTAVE);
    }

    const int Size() {
      return OC::Scales::GetScale( scale ).num_notes;
    }
  };

  struct DigitalInputMap;
  struct CVInputMap;

  extern uint32_t popup_tick; // for button feedback
  extern PopupType popup_type;
  extern uint8_t qview; // which quantizer's setting is shown in popup
  extern int q_edit;
  extern int midi_edit;
  extern uint8_t mview;
  extern ErrMsgIndex msg_idx;

  extern peaks::MultistageEnvelope env_[DAC_CHANNEL_COUNT];
  extern util::TuringShiftRegister* turing_machine_[ADC_CHANNEL_COUNT];

  // input quantizers, because sometimes we need hysteresis
  extern util::SemitoneQuantizer input_quant[ADC_CHANNEL_COUNT];
  extern QuantEngine q_engine[QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT];

#if defined(ARDUINO_TEENSY41) || defined(VOR)
  extern int octave_max;
#elif defined(NORTHERNLIGHT)
  static constexpr int octave_max = 10;
#else
  static constexpr int octave_max = 6;
#endif

  extern DigitalInputMap jump_trig_;

  extern int preset_id;

  extern uint8_t midi_thru_disable;
  extern uint8_t midi_clkrx_disable;
  extern uint8_t midi_clktx_disable;
  extern uint8_t midi_msgrx_disable;
  extern uint8_t midi_msgtx_disable;
  extern bool cursor_wrap;
  extern bool auto_save_enabled;
  extern uint8_t trig_length;
  extern uint8_t screensaver_mode;
  extern const char * const ssmodes[];

  extern OC::menu::ScreenCursor<5> showhide_cursor;

  void Init();
  void ResetMappings();
  void DrawAppletList(bool blink = false);

  // --- Quantizer helpers
  QuantEngine& GetQuantEngine(int ch);
  int GetLatestNoteNumber(int ch);
  int Quantize(int ch, int cv, int root = 0, int transpose = 0);
  int QuantizerLookup(int ch, int note);
  void QuantizerConfigure(int ch, int scale, uint16_t mask = 0xffff);
  int GetScale(int ch);
  int GetRootNote(int ch);
  int SetRootNote(int ch, int root);
  void NudgeRootNote(int ch, int dir);
  void NudgeOctave(int ch, int dir);
  void NudgeScale(int ch, int dir);
  void QuantizerEdit(int ch);
  void MidiMapEdit(int ch);
  void QEditEncoderMove(bool rightenc, int dir);
  void MEditEncoderMove(bool rightenc, int dir);
  void DrawMidiMaps(int curpos);
  void DrawConfigRow(int row, int y, bool cur, bool editing);
  void DrawPopup(const int config_cursor = 0, const int preset_id = 0, const bool blink = 0);
  void ToggleClockRun();
  void PokePopup(PopupType pop, ErrMsgIndex err = NO_ERROR);
  void PokePopup(PopupType pop, const char* msg);

  peaks::MultistageEnvelope& GetEnvelope(int index);
  util::TuringShiftRegister& GetTM(int index);
  QuantEngine& GetQEngine(int index);

} // namespace HS


// Specifies where data goes in flash storage for each selcted applet, and how big it is
struct PackLocation {
    size_t location;
    size_t size;
};

/* Add value to a 64-bit storage unit at the specified location */
constexpr void Pack(uint64_t &data, const PackLocation p, const uint64_t value) {
    data |= (value << p.location);
}

/* Retrieve value from a 64-bit storage unit at the specified location and of the specified bit size */
constexpr int Unpack(const uint64_t &data, const PackLocation p) {
    uint64_t mask = 1;
    for (size_t i = 1; i < p.size; i++) mask |= (0x01 << i);
    return (data >> p.location) & mask;
}

template <typename T>
constexpr std::pair<int, T&&> IndexedInput(int index, T&& input_map) {
  return std::pair<int, T&&>(index, std::forward<T>(input_map));
}

void gfxPos(int x, int y);
void gfxPrint(int x, int y, const char *str);
void gfxPrint(int x, int y, int num);
void gfxPrint(const char *str);
void gfxPrint(int num);
void gfxPrint(int x_adv, int num);
void gfxPrintVoltage(int cv);
void gfxPrintFreqFromPitch(int16_t pitch);
void gfxPrintIcon(const uint8_t *data, int16_t w = 8);
void gfxPrint(const HS::DigitalInputMap &map);
void gfxPrint(const HS::CVInputMap &map);
void gfxPixel(int x, int y);
void gfxFrame(int x, int y, int w, int h, bool dotted = false);
void gfxRect(int x, int y, int w, int h);
void gfxInvert(int x, int y, int w, int h);
void gfxLine(int x, int y, int x2, int y2);
void gfxDottedLine(int x, int y, int x2, int y2, uint8_t p = 2);
void gfxCircle(int x, int y, int r);
void gfxBitmap(int x, int y, int w, const uint8_t *data);
void gfxIcon(int x, int y, const uint8_t *data, bool clearfirst = false);
void gfxHeader(const char *str, const uint8_t *icon = nullptr);
void gfxFooter(const char *str, const uint8_t *icon = nullptr);

constexpr uint8_t pad(int range, int number) {
    uint8_t padding = 0;
    while (range > 1)
    {
        if (abs(number) < range) padding += 6;
        range = range / 10;
    }
    if (number < 0 && padding > 0) padding -= 6; // Compensate for minus sign
    return padding;
}

// For ascii strings of 9 characters or less, will just be the ascii bits
// concatenated together. More characters than that and the xor plus misaligned
// shifting should avoid collisions.
constexpr uint64_t strhash(const char* str) {
  uint64_t id = 0;
  for (const char* c = str; *c != '\0'; c++) {
    id = (id << 7) | (id >> (64 - 7));
    id ^= (*c);
  }
  return id;
}

/* Proportion CV values into pixels for display purposes.
 *
 * Solves this:     cv_value           ???
 *              ----------------- = ----------
 *              HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV   max_pixels
 */
const int ProportionCV(const int cv_value, const int max_pixels, const int max_cv = HEMISPHERE_MAX_CV);

void ZapScreensaver(const uint8_t stars = 0);
void BeatCounterScreensaver();
```

```
//
// Based on ProbabilityDivider (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach
// and ProbabilityMelody (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach,
```
