// Copyright (c) 2025, Andrew DeSena
//
// Based on ProbabilityDivider (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach
// and ProbabilityMelody (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach,
// modified 2025 by Beau Sterling, Nicholas Michalek, Bryan Head
//
// Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
// of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
// in the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights
// to use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell
// copies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software is
// furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions:
//
// The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all
// copies or substantial portions of the Software.
//
// THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR
// IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY,
// FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE
// AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER
// LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,
// OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE
// SOFTWARE.

// Siggy: Probabilistic rhythm + melody in one hemisphere/quadrant.
//
// Page 0 (cursor 0-5): ProbDiv-style weight sliders, loop length, quantizer select
// Page 1 (cursor 6+): Chromatic keyboard note weights + octave Walk control
//
// Note weights represent SCALE DEGREES (not absolute chromatic notes).
// The cursor navigates all 12 chromatic keys (C to B); only in-scale keys
// are editable. Changing the quantizer root or scale shifts pitches while
// preserving the musical weight pattern.
// The Walk (W) parameter lets notes span +/-n octaves during loop generation.
// AuxButton on Q cursor opens the global quantizer editor popup.
//
// Inputs:
//   D1 = Clock     D2 = Reset
//   CV1 = Loop length modulation
//   CV2 = Reseed trigger (>2.5V) / Bypass loop (<-2.5V)
// Outputs:
//   Out1 = V/Oct   Out2 = Gate

#pragma once
#include "../HemisphereApplet.h"

class Siggy : public HemisphereApplet {
public:
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_DIV_WEIGHT = 15;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT = 6;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_LOOP_LENGTH = 32;

  enum SiggyCursor {
    // Page 0
    WEIGHT1 = 0, WEIGHT2, WEIGHT4, WEIGHT8,
    LOOP_LENGTH,
    QUANT_SELECT,
    // Page 1
    NOTE_0,
    NOTE_11 = NOTE_0 + 11,
    WALK,
    LAST_CURSOR = WALK
  };

  const char* applet_name() { return "Siggy"; }
  const uint8_t* applet_icon() { return PhzIcons::probMelo; } // Assuming probMelo icon exists

  void Start() override {
    weight_1 = weight_2 = weight_4 = weight_8 = 0;
    loop_length = loop_length_mod = 0;
    loop_index = loop_step = skip_steps = 0;
    bypass_loop = reseed_high = false;
    qselect = (uint8_t)constrain(io_offset, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    // Default: emphasize root, 3rd and 5th scale degrees; mute the rest.
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) note_weights[i] = 0;
    note_weights[0] = 6; // degree 1 (root/tonic) - max weight
    note_weights[2] = 3; // degree 3 (3rd)
    note_weights[4] = 4; // degree 5 (5th)
    pitch_cv = 0;
    last_note = 0;
    walk = 0;
    pulse_animation = reseed_animation = reset_animation = value_animation = 0;
  }

  void Unload() override {}

  void Controller() override {
    loop_length_mod = loop_length;
    if (DetentedIn(0)) {
      Modulate(loop_length_mod, 0, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
    }
    bool is_looping = loop_length_mod > 0 && !bypass_loop;

    if (Clock(1)) {
      loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
      reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
    }

    if (Clock(0)) {
      int reseed_cv = DetentedIn(1);

      if (reseed_cv > (5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && !reseed_high) {
        GenerateLoop(true, false);
        reseed_high = true;
        reseed_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }
      if (reseed_cv < (5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && reseed_high) reseed_high = false;
      bypass_loop = (reseed_cv < -(5 * HS::ONE_OCTAVE >> 1));

      if (is_looping && loop_step >= loop_length_mod) {
        loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
        reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }

      if (--skip_steps > 0) {
        if (is_looping) loop_step++;
        return;
      }

      int8_t next_note = -1;
      if (is_looping) {
        skip_steps = GetNextLoopDivAndNote(next_note);
      } else {
        skip_steps = GetNextWeightedDiv();
        next_note = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      }

      if (skip_steps == 0) return;

      last_note = next_note;
      pitch_cv = NoteToCV(next_note);
      Out(0, pitch_cv);
      ClockOut(1);
      pulse_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME;
    }

    if (pulse_animation > 0) pulse_animation--;
    if (reseed_animation > 0) reseed_animation--;
    if (reset_animation > 0) reset_animation--;
    if (value_animation > 0) value_animation--;
  }

  void View() override {
    if (cursor <= QUANT_SELECT) DrawPage0();
    else DrawPage1();
  }

  void AuxButton() override {
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) HS::QuantizerEdit(qselect);
    else CancelEdit();
  }

  void OnEncoderMove(int direction) override {
    if (!EditMode()) {
      MoveCursor(cursor, direction, LAST_CURSOR);
      return;
    }
    switch (cursor) {
    case WEIGHT1: weight_1 = constrain(weight_1 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT2: weight_2 = constrain(weight_2 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT4: weight_4 = constrain(weight_4 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT8: weight_8 = constrain(weight_8 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case LOOP_LENGTH: {
      int old = loop_length;
      loop_length = constrain(loop_length + direction, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
      if (old == 0 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(true, true);
      break;
    }
    case QUANT_SELECT:
      qselect = (uint8_t)constrain((int)qselect + direction, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
      break;
    case WALK:
      walk = constrain(walk + direction, 0, 4);
      break;
    default:
      if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
        int chrom = cursor - NOTE_0;
        int degree = ChromaticToDegree(chrom);
        if (degree >= 0) {
          note_weights[degree] = constrain(note_weights[degree] + direction, 0, MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT);
          value_animation = HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS;
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    // Regenerate loop when rhythm, melody, or walk parameters change
    if (cursor < LOOP_LENGTH && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor == WALK && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
  }

  uint64_t OnDataRequest() override {
    // Pack all state into 64 bits using PackPackables.
    // note_weights stored as unsigned 3-bit values [0, 6] (weight 0 = muted/off).
    // Total: 12*3 + 4*4 + 6 + 3 + 3 = 64 bits.
    return PackPackables(
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[0]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[1]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[2]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[3]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[4]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[5]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[6]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[7]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[8]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[9]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[10]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[11]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 4>{weight_1},
      Packable<uint8_t, 4>{weight_2},
      Packable<uint8_t, 4>{weight_4},
      Packable<uint8_t, 4>{weight_8},
      Packable<uint8_t, 6>{loop_length},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{qselect},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{walk}
    );
  }

  void OnDataReceive(uint64_t data) override {
    UnpackPackables(
      data,
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[0]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[1]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[2]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[3]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[4]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[5]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[6]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[7]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[8]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[9]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[10]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{note_weights[11]},
      Packable<uint8_t, 4>{weight_1},
      Packable<uint8_t, 4>{weight_2},
      Packable<uint8_t, 4>{weight_4},
      Packable<uint8_t, 4>{weight_8},
      Packable<uint8_t, 6>{loop_length},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{qselect},
      Packable<uint8_t, 3>{walk}
    );
    CONSTRAIN(qselect, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    CONSTRAIN(walk, 0, 4);
    if (loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, true);
  }

protected:
  void SetHelp() override {
    //                    "-------" <-- Label size guide
    help[HELP_DIGITAL1] = "Clock";
    help[HELP_DIGITAL2] = "Reset";
    help[HELP_CV1]      = "Length";
    help[HELP_CV2]      = "Reseed";
    help[HELP_OUT1]     = "V/Oct";
    help[HELP_OUT2]     = "Gate";
    help[HELP_EXTRA1]   = "Pg1:Rhythm+Quant";
    help[HELP_EXTRA2]   = "Pg2:Keys+Walk";
    //                    "---------------------" <-- Extra text size guide
  }

private:
  int cursor;

  // Rhythm parameters (Page 0)
  uint8_t weight_1, weight_2, weight_4, weight_8;
  uint8_t loop_length, loop_length_mod;
  int div_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  int loop_index, loop_step, skip_steps;
  bool reseed_high, bypass_loop;

  // Melody parameters (Page 1)
  // note_weights: 0 = muted/off, 1-6 = weight (MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT = 6)
  uint8_t note_weights[12];
  int8_t melody_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  uint8_t walk; // octave walk range: melody notes span +/-walk octaves

  // Quantizer
  uint8_t qselect;

  // Output state
  int pitch_cv;
  int8_t last_note;

  // UI animations
  int pulse_animation, reseed_animation, reset_animation, value_animation;

  // Division weight pointers and values
  const uint8_t *dw[4] = {&weight_1, &weight_2, &weight_4, &weight_8};
  static constexpr int divs[4] = {1, 2, 4, 8};

  // Keyboard layout constants: 9px white keys spanning the full 63px width.
  // kx[i] = left pixel of the indicator bar; xo = kx[i] + (kp[i] ? 1 : 2) = bar center.
  static constexpr uint8_t kx[12] = {2, 7, 11, 16, 20, 29, 34, 38, 43, 47, 52, 56};
  static constexpr uint8_t kp[12] = {0, 1,  0,  1,  0,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0};
  static constexpr char    kn[12] = {'C','C','D','D','E','F','F','G','G','A','A','B'};

  // Returns the number of notes (degrees) in the selected quantizer scale.
  int GetNumNotes() const {
    return (int)OC::Scales::GetScale(HS::GetScale(qselect)).num_notes;
  }

  // Map a scale degree (0 = root) to its chromatic position 0-11.
  uint8_t DegreeToChromatic(int degree) const {
    const HS::QuantEngine& qe = HS::GetQuantEngine(qselect);
    const braids::Scale& sc = OC::Scales::GetScale(qe.scale);
    int n = (int)sc.num_notes;
    int d = ((degree % n) + n) % n;
    int semi = (int)(sc.notes[d] >> 7) % 12;
    return (uint8_t)((semi + qe.root_note) % 12);
  }

  // Map a chromatic note 0-11 to its scale degree, or -1 if out of scale.
  int ChromaticToDegree(int chrom) const {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if ((int)DegreeToChromatic(i) == chrom) return i;
    }
    return -1;
  }

  // Select a random scale degree (0-based) weighted by note_weights[].
  // Falls back to degree 0 (root) when all weights are zero/muted.
  int8_t GetNextWeightedPitch() const {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      total += note_weights[i];
    }
    if (total == 0) return 0; // root degree
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if (note_weights[i] > 0) {
        if (rnd <= note_weights[i]) return (int8_t)i;
        rnd -= note_weights[i];
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }

  // Convert a scale degree to pitch CV via the global quantizer.
  // HS::QuantizerLookup uses index-64 internally; +64 centers at C4/octave-0.
  // The quantizer engine then adds root note and octave offset automatically.
  int NoteToCV(int8_t degree) const {
    return HS::QuantizerLookup(qselect, (int)degree + 64);
  }

  int GetNextWeightedDiv() {
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) total += *dw[i];
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      if (rnd <= *dw[i] && *dw[i] > 0) return divs[i];
      rnd -= *dw[i];
    }
    return 0;
  }

  int GetNextLoopDivAndNote(int8_t& note_out) {
    int idx = loop_index;
    int value = div_loop[idx];
    if (value == 0) {
      loop_index = loop_step = 0;
      idx = 0;
      value = div_loop[0];
    }
    note_out = melody_loop[idx];
    loop_index++;
    loop_step++;
    return value;
  }

  void GenerateLoop(bool reseed, bool restart) {
    memset(div_loop, 0, sizeof(div_loop));
    memset(melody_loop, 0, sizeof(melody_loop));
    if (restart) { loop_step = loop_index = 0; }
    int seed = (weight_1 << 12) | (weight_2 << 8) | (weight_4 << 4) | weight_8;
    seed ^= (qselect << 16);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) seed ^= note_weights[i] << i;
    if (reseed) { randomSeed(micros()); seed ^= (int)micros(); }
    randomSeed(seed);
    int idx = 0, counter = 0;
    while (counter < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH && idx < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH) {
      int div = GetNextWeightedDiv();
      if (div == 0) break;
      div_loop[idx] = div;
      int8_t degree = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      if (walk > 0) {
        int oct = random(-walk, walk + 1);
        degree = (int8_t)(degree + oct * 12); // octave jumps in semitones
      }
      melody_loop[idx] = degree;
      idx++;
      counter += div;
    }
  }

  void DrawPage0() {
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      gfxPrint(1, 15 + i * 10, "/");
      gfxPrint(divs[i]);
      DrawSlider(20, 15 + i * 10, 40, *dw[i], MAX_DIV_WEIGHT, cursor == i);
      if (pulse_animation > 0 && skip_steps == divs[i])
        gfxInvert(1, 15 + i * 10, 12, 8);
    }

    // Bottom row: loop icon + length + quantizer select
    gfxIcon(4, 55, LOOP_ICON);
    if (reseed_animation > 0) gfxInvert(4, 55, 12, 8);

    if (loop_length_mod == 0) {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, "off");
    } else {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, loop_length_mod);
    }
    if (cursor == LOOP_LENGTH) gfxCursor(19, 63, 18);

    gfxPrint(40, 55, "Q");
    gfxPrint(46, 55, qselect + 1);
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) gfxCursor(40, 63, 18);

    if (reset_animation > 0) gfxPrint(57, 55, "R");
  }

  void DrawPage1() {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();

    // Header: Note info and Walk value
    gfxPrint(46, 13, "W:");
    gfxPrint(56, 13, walk);
    if (cursor == WALK) gfxCursor(46, 15, 16);

    // Piano keyboard: 9px white keys, 63px total width
    gfxFrame(0, 27, 63, 32);

    // White key dividers (between natural notes at 9px each)
    for (uint8_t wx = 1; wx < 7; ++wx) {
      if (wx == 3) gfxLine(wx * 9, 27, wx * 9, 58); // E-F boundary, full height
      else         gfxLine(wx * 9, 43, wx * 9, 58);
    }

    // Black keys: C#, D#, F#, G#, A#
    static constexpr uint8_t bk_x[5] = {6, 15, 33, 42, 51};
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 5; ++i) gfxInvert(bk_x[i], 28, 5, 15);

    // Weight bars at each in-scale degree's chromatic position
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      uint8_t c  = DegreeToChromatic(i);
      uint8_t xo = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yo = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      bool is_cur = EditMode() && cursor == NOTE_0 + (int)c;
      bool has_weight = note_weights[i] > 0;
      bool is_black = kp[c];
      if (has_weight || is_cur) {
        gfxDottedLine(xo, yo, xo, yo + 10);
        if (has_weight) {
          int bar_height = (note_weights[i] * 10) / MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT;
          int bar_y = yo + 10 - bar_height;
          if (is_black) {
            gfxInvert(xo - 1, bar_y, 3, bar_height);
          } else {
            gfxLine(xo - 1, bar_y, xo + 1, bar_y);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Active note triangle: map last_note (with octave offset) to chromatic key
    {
      int d = ((int)last_note % num_notes + num_notes) % num_notes;
      uint8_t c = DegreeToChromatic(d);
      gfxIcon(kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2) - 3, 59, UP_TRI_R_HALF);
    }

    // Keyboard cursor: all 12 chromatic positions are valid to navigate
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c  = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      uint8_t xc = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yc = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      if (EditMode() || CursorBlink()) gfxLine(xc, yc, xc, yc + 10);
    }

    // Persistent note info: note name + weight (or N/A if out of scale)
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      int degree = ChromaticToDegree(c);
      gfxRect(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxPos(17, 14);
      graphics.printf("%c", kn[c]);
      if (kp[c]) gfxPrint(23, 14, "#");
      if (degree < 0) {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, "N/A");
      } else {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, ":");
        gfxPrint(33, 14, note_weights[degree]);
      }
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
    }
  }
};
```

> Attempt to fix lint errors? (Y)es/(N)o [Yes]: y  

Looking at the errors, the main issues are:

1. **`PhzIcons::probMelo`** doesn't exist - should use `PhzIcons::probMeloD` (from ProbabilityMelody)
2. **`HS::ONE_OCTAVE`** is not a member of `HS` namespace - it's defined as a macro in `HSUtils.h`, so just use `ONE_OCTAVE`
3. **`HS::QuantizerEdit`** - need to check the correct signature
4. **`CONSTRAIN`** macro used on references in `UnpackPackables` - the `Packable` references need to bind correctly
5. **`SetHelp()`** is `protected` in base class, so `override` is correct but the `help` array access needs the correct scope
6. **`GetNumNotes()`** uses `HS::GetScale()` which returns `int`, but `OC::Scales::GetScale()` expects `int` - this is fine, but `num_notes` access needs care
7. **`GetNextWeightedPitch()` const** - modifies `note_weights` indirectly? No, it reads it. But `GetNumNotes()` calls `HS::GetQuantEngine()` which is not const. Need to remove `const` or fix the chain.

Let me look at the patterns from `ProbabilityMelody` and `ProbabilityDivider` more carefully and fix `Siggy.h`:

software/src/applets/Siggy.h
```
// Copyright (c) 2025, Andrew DeSena
//
// Based on ProbabilityDivider (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach
// and ProbabilityMelody (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach,
// modified 2025 by Beau Sterling, Nicholas Michalek, Bryan Head
//
// Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
// of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
// in the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights
// to use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell
// copies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software is
// furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions:
//
// The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all
// copies or substantial portions of the Software.
//
// THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR
// IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY,
// FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE
// AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER
// LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,
// OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE
// SOFTWARE.

// Siggy: Probabilistic rhythm + melody in one hemisphere/quadrant.
//
// Page 0 (cursor 0-5): ProbDiv-style weight sliders, loop length, quantizer select
// Page 1 (cursor 6+): Chromatic keyboard note weights + octave Walk control
//
// Note weights represent SCALE DEGREES (not absolute chromatic notes).
// The cursor navigates all 12 chromatic keys (C to B); only in-scale keys
// are editable. Changing the quantizer root or scale shifts pitches while
// preserving the musical weight pattern.
// The Walk (W) parameter lets notes span +/-n octaves during loop generation.
// AuxButton on Q cursor opens the global quantizer editor popup.
//
// Inputs:
//   D1 = Clock     D2 = Reset
//   CV1 = Loop length modulation
//   CV2 = Reseed trigger (>2.5V) / Bypass loop (<-2.5V)
// Outputs:
//   Out1 = V/Oct   Out2 = Gate

#pragma once
#include "../HemisphereApplet.h"

class Siggy : public HemisphereApplet {
public:
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_DIV_WEIGHT = 15;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT = 6;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_LOOP_LENGTH = 32;

  enum SiggyCursor {
    // Page 0
    WEIGHT1 = 0, WEIGHT2, WEIGHT4, WEIGHT8,
    LOOP_LENGTH,
    QUANT_SELECT,
    // Page 1
    NOTE_0,
    NOTE_11 = NOTE_0 + 11,
    WALK,
    LAST_CURSOR = WALK
  };

  const char* applet_name() { return "Siggy"; }
  const uint8_t* applet_icon() { return PhzIcons::probMeloD; }

  void Start() override {
    weight_1 = weight_2 = weight_4 = weight_8 = 0;
    loop_length = loop_length_mod = 0;
    loop_index = loop_step = skip_steps = 0;
    bypass_loop = reseed_high = false;
    qselect = (uint8_t)constrain(io_offset, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    // Default: emphasize root, 3rd and 5th scale degrees; mute the rest.
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) note_weights[i] = 0;
    note_weights[0] = 6; // degree 1 (root/tonic) - max weight
    note_weights[2] = 3; // degree 3 (3rd)
    note_weights[4] = 4; // degree 5 (5th)
    pitch_cv = 0;
    last_note = 0;
    walk = 0;
    pulse_animation = reseed_animation = reset_animation = value_animation = 0;
  }

  void Unload() override {}

  void Controller() override {
    loop_length_mod = loop_length;
    if (DetentedIn(0)) {
      Modulate(loop_length_mod, 0, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
    }
    bool is_looping = loop_length_mod > 0 && !bypass_loop;

    if (Clock(1)) {
      loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
      reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
    }

    if (Clock(0)) {
      int reseed_cv = DetentedIn(1);

      if (reseed_cv > (5 * ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && !reseed_high) {
        GenerateLoop(true, false);
        reseed_high = true;
        reseed_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }
      if (reseed_cv < (5 * ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && reseed_high) reseed_high = false;
      bypass_loop = (reseed_cv < -(5 * ONE_OCTAVE >> 1));

      if (is_looping && loop_step >= loop_length_mod) {
        loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
        reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }

      if (--skip_steps > 0) {
        if (is_looping) loop_step++;
        return;
      }

      int8_t next_note = -1;
      if (is_looping) {
        skip_steps = GetNextLoopDivAndNote(next_note);
      } else {
        skip_steps = GetNextWeightedDiv();
        next_note = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      }

      if (skip_steps == 0) return;

      last_note = next_note;
      pitch_cv = NoteToCV(next_note);
      Out(0, pitch_cv);
      ClockOut(1);
      pulse_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME;
    }

    if (pulse_animation > 0) pulse_animation--;
    if (reseed_animation > 0) reseed_animation--;
    if (reset_animation > 0) reset_animation--;
    if (value_animation > 0) value_animation--;
  }

  void View() override {
    if (cursor <= QUANT_SELECT) DrawPage0();
    else DrawPage1();
  }

  void AuxButton() override {
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) HS::QuantizerEdit(qselect);
    else CancelEdit();
  }

  void OnEncoderMove(int direction) override {
    if (!EditMode()) {
      MoveCursor(cursor, direction, LAST_CURSOR);
      return;
    }
    switch (cursor) {
    case WEIGHT1: weight_1 = constrain(weight_1 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT2: weight_2 = constrain(weight_2 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT4: weight_4 = constrain(weight_4 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT8: weight_8 = constrain(weight_8 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case LOOP_LENGTH: {
      int old = loop_length;
      loop_length = constrain(loop_length + direction, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
      if (old == 0 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(true, true);
      break;
    }
    case QUANT_SELECT:
      qselect = (uint8_t)constrain((int)qselect + direction, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
      break;
    case WALK:
      walk = constrain(walk + direction, 0, 4);
      break;
    default:
      if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
        int chrom = cursor - NOTE_0;
        int degree = ChromaticToDegree(chrom);
        if (degree >= 0) {
          note_weights[degree] = constrain(note_weights[degree] + direction, 0, MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT);
          value_animation = HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS;
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    // Regenerate loop when rhythm, melody, or walk parameters change
    if (cursor < LOOP_LENGTH && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor == WALK && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
  }

  uint64_t OnDataRequest() override {
    uint64_t data = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < 12; ++i) {
      Pack(data, PackLocation {i*3, 3}, note_weights[i]);
    }
    Pack(data, PackLocation {36, 4}, weight_1);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {40, 4}, weight_2);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {44, 4}, weight_4);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {48, 4}, weight_8);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {52, 6}, loop_length);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {58, 3}, qselect);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {61, 3}, walk);
    return data;
  }

  void OnDataReceive(uint64_t data) override {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < 12; ++i) {
      note_weights[i] = Unpack(data, PackLocation {i*3, 3});
    }
    weight_1 = Unpack(data, PackLocation {36, 4});
    weight_2 = Unpack(data, PackLocation {40, 4});
    weight_4 = Unpack(data, PackLocation {44, 4});
    weight_8 = Unpack(data, PackLocation {48, 4});
    loop_length = Unpack(data, PackLocation {52, 6});
    qselect = Unpack(data, PackLocation {58, 3});
    walk = Unpack(data, PackLocation {61, 3});
    CONSTRAIN(qselect, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    CONSTRAIN(walk, 0, 4);
    if (loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, true);
  }

protected:
  void SetHelp() override {
    //                    "-------" <-- Label size guide
    help[HELP_DIGITAL1] = "Clock";
    help[HELP_DIGITAL2] = "Reset";
    help[HELP_CV1]      = "Length";
    help[HELP_CV2]      = "Reseed";
    help[HELP_OUT1]     = "V/Oct";
    help[HELP_OUT2]     = "Gate";
    help[HELP_EXTRA1]   = "Pg1:Rhythm+Quant";
    help[HELP_EXTRA2]   = "Pg2:Keys+Walk";
    //                    "---------------------" <-- Extra text size guide
  }

private:
  int cursor;

  // Rhythm parameters (Page 0)
  uint8_t weight_1, weight_2, weight_4, weight_8;
  uint8_t loop_length, loop_length_mod;
  int div_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  int loop_index, loop_step, skip_steps;
  bool reseed_high, bypass_loop;

  // Melody parameters (Page 1)
  // note_weights: 0 = muted/off, 1-6 = weight (MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT = 6)
  uint8_t note_weights[12];
  int8_t melody_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  uint8_t walk; // octave walk range: melody notes span +/-walk octaves

  // Quantizer
  uint8_t qselect;

  // Output state
  int pitch_cv;
  int8_t last_note;

  // UI animations
  int pulse_animation, reseed_animation, reset_animation, value_animation;

  // Division weight pointers and values
  const uint8_t *dw[4] = {&weight_1, &weight_2, &weight_4, &weight_8};
  static constexpr int divs[4] = {1, 2, 4, 8};

  // Keyboard layout constants: 9px white keys spanning the full 63px width.
  // kx[i] = left pixel of the indicator bar; xo = kx[i] + (kp[i] ? 1 : 2) = bar center.
  static constexpr uint8_t kx[12] = {2, 7, 11, 16, 20, 29, 34, 38, 43, 47, 52, 56};
  static constexpr uint8_t kp[12] = {0, 1,  0,  1,  0,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0};
  static constexpr char    kn[12] = {'C','C','D','D','E','F','F','G','G','A','A','B'};

  // Returns the number of notes (degrees) in the selected quantizer scale.
  int GetNumNotes() {
    return (int)OC::Scales::GetScale(HS::GetScale(qselect)).num_notes;
  }

  // Map a scale degree (0 = root) to its chromatic position 0-11.
  uint8_t DegreeToChromatic(int degree) {
    const HS::QuantEngine& qe = HS::GetQuantEngine(qselect);
    const braids::Scale& sc = OC::Scales::GetScale(qe.scale);
    int n = (int)sc.num_notes;
    int d = ((degree % n) + n) % n;
    int semi = (int)(sc.notes[d] >> 7) % 12;
    return (uint8_t)((semi + qe.root_note) % 12);
  }

  // Map a chromatic note 0-11 to its scale degree, or -1 if out of scale.
  int ChromaticToDegree(int chrom) {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if ((int)DegreeToChromatic(i) == chrom) return i;
    }
    return -1;
  }

  // Select a random scale degree (0-based) weighted by note_weights[].
  // Falls back to degree 0 (root) when all weights are zero/muted.
  int8_t GetNextWeightedPitch() {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      total += note_weights[i];
    }
    if (total == 0) return 0; // root degree
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if (note_weights[i] > 0) {
        if (rnd <= note_weights[i]) return (int8_t)i;
        rnd -= note_weights[i];
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }

  // Convert a scale degree to pitch CV via the global quantizer.
  // HS::QuantizerLookup uses index-64 internally; +64 centers at C4/octave-0.
  // The quantizer engine then adds root note and octave offset automatically.
  int NoteToCV(int8_t degree) {
    return HS::QuantizerLookup(qselect, (int)degree + 64);
  }

  int GetNextWeightedDiv() {
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) total += *dw[i];
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      if (rnd <= *dw[i] && *dw[i] > 0) return divs[i];
      rnd -= *dw[i];
    }
    return 0;
  }

  int GetNextLoopDivAndNote(int8_t& note_out) {
    int idx = loop_index;
    int value = div_loop[idx];
    if (value == 0) {
      loop_index = loop_step = 0;
      idx = 0;
      value = div_loop[0];
    }
    note_out = melody_loop[idx];
    loop_index++;
    loop_step++;
    return value;
  }

  void GenerateLoop(bool reseed, bool restart) {
    memset(div_loop, 0, sizeof(div_loop));
    memset(melody_loop, 0, sizeof(melody_loop));
    if (restart) { loop_step = loop_index = 0; }
    int seed = (weight_1 << 12) | (weight_2 << 8) | (weight_4 << 4) | weight_8;
    seed ^= (qselect << 16);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) seed ^= note_weights[i] << i;
    if (reseed) { randomSeed(micros()); seed ^= (int)micros(); }
    randomSeed(seed);
    int idx = 0, counter = 0;
    while (counter < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH && idx < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH) {
      int div = GetNextWeightedDiv();
      if (div == 0) break;
      div_loop[idx] = div;
      int8_t degree = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      if (walk > 0) {
        int oct = random(-walk, walk + 1);
        degree = (int8_t)(degree + oct * 12); // octave jumps in semitones
      }
      melody_loop[idx] = degree;
      idx++;
      counter += div;
    }
  }

  void DrawPage0() {
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      gfxPrint(1, 15 + i * 10, "/");
      gfxPrint(divs[i]);
      DrawSlider(20, 15 + i * 10, 40, *dw[i], MAX_DIV_WEIGHT, cursor == i);
      if (pulse_animation > 0 && skip_steps == divs[i])
        gfxInvert(1, 15 + i * 10, 12, 8);
    }

    // Bottom row: loop icon + length + quantizer select
    gfxIcon(4, 55, LOOP_ICON);
    if (reseed_animation > 0) gfxInvert(4, 55, 12, 8);

    if (loop_length_mod == 0) {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, "off");
    } else {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, loop_length_mod);
    }
    if (cursor == LOOP_LENGTH) gfxCursor(19, 63, 18);

    gfxPrint(40, 55, "Q");
    gfxPrint(46, 55, qselect + 1);
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) gfxCursor(40, 63, 18);

    if (reset_animation > 0) gfxPrint(57, 55, "R");
  }

  void DrawPage1() {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();

    // Header: Note info and Walk value
    gfxPrint(46, 13, "W:");
    gfxPrint(56, 13, walk);
    if (cursor == WALK) gfxCursor(46, 15, 16);

    // Piano keyboard: 9px white keys, 63px total width
    gfxFrame(0, 27, 63, 32);

    // White key dividers (between natural notes at 9px each)
    for (uint8_t wx = 1; wx < 7; ++wx) {
      if (wx == 3) gfxLine(wx * 9, 27, wx * 9, 58); // E-F boundary, full height
      else         gfxLine(wx * 9, 43, wx * 9, 58);
    }

    // Black keys: C#, D#, F#, G#, A#
    static constexpr uint8_t bk_x[5] = {6, 15, 33, 42, 51};
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 5; ++i) gfxInvert(bk_x[i], 28, 5, 15);

    // Weight bars at each in-scale degree's chromatic position
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      uint8_t c  = DegreeToChromatic(i);
      uint8_t xo = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yo = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      bool is_cur = EditMode() && cursor == NOTE_0 + (int)c;
      bool has_weight = note_weights[i] > 0;
      bool is_black = kp[c];
      if (has_weight || is_cur) {
        gfxDottedLine(xo, yo, xo, yo + 10);
        if (has_weight) {
          int bar_height = (note_weights[i] * 10) / MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT;
          int bar_y = yo + 10 - bar_height;
          if (is_black) {
            gfxInvert(xo - 1, bar_y, 3, bar_height);
          } else {
            gfxLine(xo - 1, bar_y, xo + 1, bar_y);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Active note triangle: map last_note (with octave offset) to chromatic key
    {
      int d = ((int)last_note % num_notes + num_notes) % num_notes;
      uint8_t c = DegreeToChromatic(d);
      gfxIcon(kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2) - 3, 59, UP_TRI_R_HALF);
    }

    // Keyboard cursor: all 12 chromatic positions are valid to navigate
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c  = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      uint8_t xc = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yc = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      if (EditMode() || CursorBlink()) gfxLine(xc, yc, xc, yc + 10);
    }

    // Persistent note info: note name + weight (or N/A if out of scale)
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      int degree = ChromaticToDegree(c);
      gfxRect(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxPos(17, 14);
      graphics.printf("%c", kn[c]);
      if (kp[c]) gfxPrint(23, 14, "#");
      if (degree < 0) {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, "N/A");
      } else {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, ":");
        gfxPrint(33, 14, note_weights[degree]);
      }
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
    }
  }
};

> Attempt to fix lint errors? (Y)es/(N)o [Yes]: y  

Looking at the errors, I need to understand the issues:

1. **`PhzIcons::probMeloD`** - need to check if this icon exists in `icons.h`
2. **`HS::QuantizerEdit(qselect)`** - need to check the correct signature
3. **`HS::QuantizerLookup(qselect, ...)` - the first param is a quantizer channel, but `qselect` is 0-7 while `io_offset` affects which quantizer is used. Looking at `HemisphereApplet.h`, the base class `QuantizerLookup` adds `io_offset` to the channel. But Siggy uses `qselect` directly as a global quantizer channel index (0-7), not offset by hemisphere. Need to use `HS::QuantizerLookup(qselect, ...)` directly (the free function, not the member).
4. **`GetNumNotes()`** calls `HS::GetScale(qselect)` which returns `int`, then `OC::Scales::GetScale()` - this is fine. But `HS::GetQuantEngine(qselect)` is not const, so `DegreeToChromatic` can't be const. Already not const, so fine.

software/src/applets/Siggy.h
```
// Copyright (c) 2025, Andrew DeSena
//
// Based on ProbabilityDivider (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach
// and ProbabilityMelody (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach,
// modified 2025 by Beau Sterling, Nicholas Michalek, Bryan Head
//
// Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
// of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
// in the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights
// to use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell
// copies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software is
// furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions:
//
// The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all
// copies or substantial portions of the Software.
//
// THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR
// IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY,
// FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE
// AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER
// LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,
// OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE
// SOFTWARE.

// Siggy: Probabilistic rhythm + melody in one hemisphere/quadrant.
//
// Page 0 (cursor 0-5): ProbDiv-style weight sliders, loop length, quantizer select
// Page 1 (cursor 6+): Chromatic keyboard note weights + octave Walk control
//
// Note weights represent SCALE DEGREES (not absolute chromatic notes).
// The cursor navigates all 12 chromatic keys (C to B); only in-scale keys
// are editable. Changing the quantizer root or scale shifts pitches while
// preserving the musical weight pattern.
// The Walk (W) parameter lets notes span +/-n octaves during loop generation.
// AuxButton on Q cursor opens the global quantizer editor popup.
//
// Inputs:
//   D1 = Clock     D2 = Reset
//   CV1 = Loop length modulation
//   CV2 = Reseed trigger (>2.5V) / Bypass loop (<-2.5V)
// Outputs:
//   Out1 = V/Oct   Out2 = Gate

#pragma once
#include "../HemisphereApplet.h"

class Siggy : public HemisphereApplet {
public:
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_DIV_WEIGHT = 15;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT = 6;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_LOOP_LENGTH = 32;

  enum SiggyCursor {
    // Page 0
    WEIGHT1 = 0, WEIGHT2, WEIGHT4, WEIGHT8,
    LOOP_LENGTH,
    QUANT_SELECT,
    // Page 1
    NOTE_0,
    NOTE_11 = NOTE_0 + 11,
    WALK,
    LAST_CURSOR = WALK
  };

  const char* applet_name() { return "Siggy"; }
  const uint8_t* applet_icon() { return PhzIcons::probDiv; }

  void Start() override {
    weight_1 = weight_2 = weight_4 = weight_8 = 0;
    loop_length = loop_length_mod = 0;
    loop_index = loop_step = skip_steps = 0;
    bypass_loop = reseed_high = false;
    qselect = (uint8_t)constrain(io_offset, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) note_weights[i] = 0;
    note_weights[0] = 6;
    note_weights[2] = 3;
    note_weights[4] = 4;
    pitch_cv = 0;
    last_note = 0;
    walk = 0;
    pulse_animation = reseed_animation = reset_animation = value_animation = 0;
  }

  void Unload() override {}

  void Controller() override {
    loop_length_mod = loop_length;
    if (DetentedIn(0)) {
      Modulate(loop_length_mod, 0, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
    }
    bool is_looping = loop_length_mod > 0 && !bypass_loop;

    if (Clock(1)) {
      loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
      reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
    }

    if (Clock(0)) {
      int reseed_cv = DetentedIn(1);

      if (reseed_cv > (5 * ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && !reseed_high) {
        GenerateLoop(true, false);
        reseed_high = true;
        reseed_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }
      if (reseed_cv < (5 * ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && reseed_high) reseed_high = false;
      bypass_loop = (reseed_cv < -(5 * ONE_OCTAVE >> 1));

      if (is_looping && loop_step >= loop_length_mod) {
        loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
        reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }

      if (--skip_steps > 0) {
        if (is_looping) loop_step++;
        return;
      }

      int8_t next_note = -1;
      if (is_looping) {
        skip_steps = GetNextLoopDivAndNote(next_note);
      } else {
        skip_steps = GetNextWeightedDiv();
        next_note = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      }

      if (skip_steps == 0) return;

      last_note = next_note;
      pitch_cv = NoteToCV(next_note);
      Out(0, pitch_cv);
      ClockOut(1);
      pulse_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME;
    }

    if (pulse_animation > 0) pulse_animation--;
    if (reseed_animation > 0) reseed_animation--;
    if (reset_animation > 0) reset_animation--;
    if (value_animation > 0) value_animation--;
  }

  void View() override {
    if (cursor <= QUANT_SELECT) DrawPage0();
    else DrawPage1();
  }

  void AuxButton() override {
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) {
      HS::QuantizerEdit(qselect);
    }
    else CancelEdit();
  }

  void OnEncoderMove(int direction) override {
    if (!EditMode()) {
      MoveCursor(cursor, direction, LAST_CURSOR);
      return;
    }
    switch (cursor) {
    case WEIGHT1: weight_1 = constrain(weight_1 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT2: weight_2 = constrain(weight_2 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT4: weight_4 = constrain(weight_4 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT8: weight_8 = constrain(weight_8 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case LOOP_LENGTH: {
      int old = loop_length;
      loop_length = constrain(loop_length + direction, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
      if (old == 0 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(true, true);
      break;
    }
    case QUANT_SELECT:
      qselect = (uint8_t)constrain((int)qselect + direction, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
      break;
    case WALK:
      walk = constrain(walk + direction, 0, 4);
      break;
    default:
      if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
        int chrom = cursor - NOTE_0;
        int degree = ChromaticToDegree(chrom);
        if (degree >= 0) {
          note_weights[degree] = constrain(note_weights[degree] + direction, 0, MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT);
          value_animation = HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS;
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    if (cursor < LOOP_LENGTH && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor == WALK && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
  }

  uint64_t OnDataRequest() override {
    uint64_t data = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) {
      Pack(data, PackLocation {i * 3, 3}, note_weights[i]);
    }
    Pack(data, PackLocation {36, 4}, weight_1);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {40, 4}, weight_2);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {44, 4}, weight_4);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {48, 4}, weight_8);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {52, 6}, loop_length);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {58, 3}, qselect);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {61, 3}, walk);
    return data;
  }

  void OnDataReceive(uint64_t data) override {
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) {
      note_weights[i] = Unpack(data, PackLocation {i * 3, 3});
    }
    weight_1 = Unpack(data, PackLocation {36, 4});
    weight_2 = Unpack(data, PackLocation {40, 4});
    weight_4 = Unpack(data, PackLocation {44, 4});
    weight_8 = Unpack(data, PackLocation {48, 4});
    loop_length = Unpack(data, PackLocation {52, 6});
    qselect = Unpack(data, PackLocation {58, 3});
    walk = Unpack(data, PackLocation {61, 3});
    CONSTRAIN(qselect, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    CONSTRAIN(walk, 0, 4);
    if (loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, true);
  }

protected:
  void SetHelp() override {
    //                    "-------" <-- Label size guide
    help[HELP_DIGITAL1] = "Clock";
    help[HELP_DIGITAL2] = "Reset";
    help[HELP_CV1]      = "Length";
    help[HELP_CV2]      = "Reseed";
    help[HELP_OUT1]     = "V/Oct";
    help[HELP_OUT2]     = "Gate";
    help[HELP_EXTRA1]   = "Pg1:Rhythm+Quant";
    help[HELP_EXTRA2]   = "Pg2:Keys+Walk";
    //                    "---------------------" <-- Extra text size guide
  }

private:
  int cursor;

  // Rhythm parameters (Page 0)
  uint8_t weight_1, weight_2, weight_4, weight_8;
  uint8_t loop_length, loop_length_mod;
  int div_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  int loop_index, loop_step, skip_steps;
  bool reseed_high, bypass_loop;

  // Melody parameters (Page 1)
  uint8_t note_weights[12];
  int8_t melody_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  uint8_t walk;

  // Quantizer
  uint8_t qselect;

  // Output state
  int pitch_cv;
  int8_t last_note;

  // UI animations
  int pulse_animation, reseed_animation, reset_animation, value_animation;

  // Division weight pointers and values
  const uint8_t *dw[4] = {&weight_1, &weight_2, &weight_4, &weight_8};
  static constexpr int divs[4] = {1, 2, 4, 8};

  // Keyboard layout constants
  static constexpr uint8_t kx[12] = {2, 7, 11, 16, 20, 29, 34, 38, 43, 47, 52, 56};
  static constexpr uint8_t kp[12] = {0, 1,  0,  1,  0,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0};
  static constexpr char    kn[12] = {'C','C','D','D','E','F','F','G','G','A','A','B'};

  int GetNumNotes() {
    return (int)OC::Scales::GetScale(HS::GetScale(qselect)).num_notes;
  }

  uint8_t DegreeToChromatic(int degree) {
    const HS::QuantEngine& qe = HS::GetQuantEngine(qselect);
    const braids::Scale& sc = OC::Scales::GetScale(qe.scale);
    int n = (int)sc.num_notes;
    int d = ((degree % n) + n) % n;
    int semi = (int)(sc.notes[d] >> 7) % 12;
    return (uint8_t)((semi + qe.root_note) % 12);
  }

  int ChromaticToDegree(int chrom) {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if ((int)DegreeToChromatic(i) == chrom) return i;
    }
    return -1;
  }

  int8_t GetNextWeightedPitch() {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      total += note_weights[i];
    }
    if (total == 0) return 0;
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if (note_weights[i] > 0) {
        if (rnd <= note_weights[i]) return (int8_t)i;
        rnd -= note_weights[i];
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }

  int NoteToCV(int8_t degree) {
    return HS::QuantizerLookup(qselect, (int)degree + 64);
  }

  int GetNextWeightedDiv() {
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) total += *dw[i];
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      if (rnd <= *dw[i] && *dw[i] > 0) return divs[i];
      rnd -= *dw[i];
    }
    return 0;
  }

  int GetNextLoopDivAndNote(int8_t& note_out) {
    int idx = loop_index;
    int value = div_loop[idx];
    if (value == 0) {
      loop_index = loop_step = 0;
      idx = 0;
      value = div_loop[0];
    }
    note_out = melody_loop[idx];
    loop_index++;
    loop_step++;
    return value;
  }

  void GenerateLoop(bool reseed, bool restart) {
    memset(div_loop, 0, sizeof(div_loop));
    memset(melody_loop, 0, sizeof(melody_loop));
    if (restart) { loop_step = loop_index = 0; }
    int seed = (weight_1 << 12) | (weight_2 << 8) | (weight_4 << 4) | weight_8;
    seed ^= (qselect << 16);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) seed ^= note_weights[i] << i;
    if (reseed) { randomSeed(micros()); seed ^= (int)micros(); }
    randomSeed(seed);
    int idx = 0, counter = 0;
    while (counter < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH && idx < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH) {
      int div = GetNextWeightedDiv();
      if (div == 0) break;
      div_loop[idx] = div;
      int8_t degree = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      if (walk > 0) {
        int oct = random(-walk, walk + 1);
        degree = (int8_t)(degree + oct * 12);
      }
      melody_loop[idx] = degree;
      idx++;
      counter += div;
    }
  }

  void DrawPage0() {
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      gfxPrint(1, 15 + i * 10, "/");
      gfxPrint(divs[i]);
      DrawSlider(20, 15 + i * 10, 40, *dw[i], MAX_DIV_WEIGHT, cursor == i);
      if (pulse_animation > 0 && skip_steps == divs[i])
        gfxInvert(1, 15 + i * 10, 12, 8);
    }

    gfxIcon(4, 55, LOOP_ICON);
    if (reseed_animation > 0) gfxInvert(4, 55, 12, 8);

    if (loop_length_mod == 0) {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, "off");
    } else {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, loop_length_mod);
    }
    if (cursor == LOOP_LENGTH) gfxCursor(19, 63, 18);

    gfxPrint(40, 55, "Q");
    gfxPrint(46, 55, qselect + 1);
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) gfxCursor(40, 63, 18);

    if (reset_animation > 0) gfxPrint(57, 55, "R");
  }

  void DrawPage1() {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();

    gfxPrint(46, 13, "W:");
    gfxPrint(56, 13, walk);
    if (cursor == WALK) gfxCursor(46, 15, 16);

    gfxFrame(0, 27, 63, 32);

    for (uint8_t wx = 1; wx < 7; ++wx) {
      if (wx == 3) gfxLine(wx * 9, 27, wx * 9, 58);
      else         gfxLine(wx * 9, 43, wx * 9, 58);
    }

    static constexpr uint8_t bk_x[5] = {6, 15, 33, 42, 51};
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 5; ++i) gfxInvert(bk_x[i], 28, 5, 15);

    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      uint8_t c  = DegreeToChromatic(i);
      uint8_t xo = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yo = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      bool is_cur = EditMode() && cursor == NOTE_0 + (int)c;
      bool has_weight = note_weights[i] > 0;
      bool is_black = kp[c];
      if (has_weight || is_cur) {
        gfxDottedLine(xo, yo, xo, yo + 10);
        if (has_weight) {
          int bar_height = (note_weights[i] * 10) / MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT;
          int bar_y = yo + 10 - bar_height;
          if (is_black) {
            gfxInvert(xo - 1, bar_y, 3, bar_height);
          } else {
            gfxLine(xo - 1, bar_y, xo + 1, bar_y);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    {
      int d = ((int)last_note % num_notes + num_notes) % num_notes;
      uint8_t c = DegreeToChromatic(d);
      gfxIcon(kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2) - 3, 59, UP_TRI_R_HALF);
    }

    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c  = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      uint8_t xc = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yc = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      if (EditMode() || CursorBlink()) gfxLine(xc, yc, xc, yc + 10);
    }

    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      int degree = ChromaticToDegree(c);
      gfxRect(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxPos(17, 14);
      graphics.printf("%c", kn[c]);
      if (kp[c]) gfxPrint(23, 14, "#");
      if (degree < 0) {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, "N/A");
      } else {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, ":");
        gfxPrint(33, 14, note_weights[degree]);
      }
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
    }
  }
};
```

6. **`Pack`/`Unpack`** are free functions from `HSUtils.h` - should be visible through the include chain.
7. **`CONSTRAIN`** macro - should be available through `OC_core.h` or `Arduino.h`.

The main issue is likely the `QuantizerLookup` call being ambiguous between the member function and the free function. Let me fix that and also double-check everything else against the patterns in `ProbabilityDivider.h` and `ProbabilityMelody.h`.

Looking at `ProbabilityMelody.h`, it uses `MIDIQuantizer::CV(...)` for output, not `HS::QuantizerLookup`. But `Siggy` is different - it uses the global quantizer system. The `HS::QuantizerLookup` free function takes `(int ch, int note)` where `ch` is the global quantizer channel (0-7) and `note` is the note number. This is correct for Siggy's use case.

The fix: use `::HS::QuantizerLookup(...)` to avoid the member function shadowing.

Also, I notice `ProbabilityMelody.h` uses `OC::DAC::kOctaveZero` in its output. Siggy should account for this too when outputting CV.

Let me also check: `ProbabilityDivider` outputs `ClockOut(0)` and `ClockOut(1)` on different channels, while Siggy outputs `Out(0, pitch_cv)` and `ClockOut(1)`. This matches the spec (Out1 = V/Oct, Out2 = Gate).

One more thing: `ProbabilityMelody` uses `MIDIQuantizer::CV(pitch[ch] + (12*OC::DAC::kOctaveZero))` for output. Siggy's `NoteToCV` uses `HS::QuantizerLookup(qselect, (int)degree + 64)`. The `+64` centers around note 64 (MIDI note 64 = C4 approximately). The quantizer then adds root note and octave. This should work, but the output might need the `OC::DAC::kOctaveZero` offset too.

Let me rewrite `Siggy.h` with all these fixes:

software/src/applets/Siggy.h
```
// Copyright (c) 2025, Andrew DeSena
//
// Based on ProbabilityDivider (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach
// and ProbabilityMelody (c) 2022 Benjamin Rosenbach,
// modified 2025 by Beau Sterling, Nicholas Michalek, Bryan Head
//
// Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
// of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
// in the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights
// to use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell
// copies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software is
// furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions:
//
// The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all
// copies or substantial portions of the Software.
//
// THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR
// IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY,
// FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE
// AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER
// LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,
// OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE
// SOFTWARE.

// Siggy: Probabilistic rhythm + melody in one hemisphere/quadrant.
//
// Page 0 (cursor 0-5): ProbDiv-style weight sliders, loop length, quantizer select
// Page 1 (cursor 6+): Chromatic keyboard note weights + octave Walk control
//
// Note weights represent SCALE DEGREES (not absolute chromatic notes).
// The cursor navigates all 12 chromatic keys (C to B); only in-scale keys
// are editable. Changing the quantizer root or scale shifts pitches while
// preserving the musical weight pattern.
// The Walk (W) parameter lets notes span +/-n octaves during loop generation.
// AuxButton on Q cursor opens the global quantizer editor popup.
//
// Inputs:
//   D1 = Clock     D2 = Reset
//   CV1 = Loop length modulation
//   CV2 = Reseed trigger (>2.5V) / Bypass loop (<-2.5V)
// Outputs:
//   Out1 = V/Oct   Out2 = Gate

#pragma once
#include "../HemisphereApplet.h"

class Siggy : public HemisphereApplet {
public:
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_DIV_WEIGHT = 15;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT = 6;
  static constexpr uint8_t MAX_LOOP_LENGTH = 32;

  enum SiggyCursor {
    // Page 0
    WEIGHT1 = 0, WEIGHT2, WEIGHT4, WEIGHT8,
    LOOP_LENGTH,
    QUANT_SELECT,
    // Page 1
    NOTE_0,
    NOTE_11 = NOTE_0 + 11,
    WALK,
    LAST_CURSOR = WALK
  };

  const char* applet_name() { return "Siggy"; }
  const uint8_t* applet_icon() { return PhzIcons::probDiv; }

  void Start() override {
    weight_1 = weight_2 = weight_4 = weight_8 = 0;
    loop_length = loop_length_mod = 0;
    loop_index = loop_step = skip_steps = 0;
    bypass_loop = reseed_high = false;
    qselect = (uint8_t)constrain(io_offset, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) note_weights[i] = 0;
    note_weights[0] = 6;
    note_weights[2] = 3;
    note_weights[4] = 4;
    pitch_cv = 0;
    last_note = 0;
    walk = 0;
    pulse_animation = reseed_animation = reset_animation = value_animation = 0;
  }

  void Unload() override {}

  void Controller() override {
    loop_length_mod = loop_length;
    if (DetentedIn(0)) {
      Modulate(loop_length_mod, 0, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
    }
    bool is_looping = loop_length_mod > 0 && !bypass_loop;

    if (Clock(1)) {
      loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
      reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
    }

    if (Clock(0)) {
      int reseed_cv = DetentedIn(1);

      if (reseed_cv > (5 * ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && !reseed_high) {
        GenerateLoop(true, false);
        reseed_high = true;
        reseed_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }
      if (reseed_cv < (5 * ONE_OCTAVE >> 1) && reseed_high) reseed_high = false;
      bypass_loop = (reseed_cv < -(5 * ONE_OCTAVE >> 1));

      if (is_looping && loop_step >= loop_length_mod) {
        loop_step = loop_index = skip_steps = 0;
        reset_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME_LONG;
      }

      if (--skip_steps > 0) {
        if (is_looping) loop_step++;
        return;
      }

      int8_t next_note = -1;
      if (is_looping) {
        skip_steps = GetNextLoopDivAndNote(next_note);
      } else {
        skip_steps = GetNextWeightedDiv();
        next_note = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      }

      if (skip_steps == 0) return;

      last_note = next_note;
      pitch_cv = NoteToCV(next_note);
      Out(0, pitch_cv);
      ClockOut(1);
      pulse_animation = HEMISPHERE_PULSE_ANIMATION_TIME;
    }

    if (pulse_animation > 0) pulse_animation--;
    if (reseed_animation > 0) reseed_animation--;
    if (reset_animation > 0) reset_animation--;
    if (value_animation > 0) value_animation--;
  }

  void View() override {
    if (cursor <= QUANT_SELECT) DrawPage0();
    else DrawPage1();
  }

  void AuxButton() override {
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) {
      HS::QuantizerEdit(qselect);
    }
    else CancelEdit();
  }

  void OnEncoderMove(int direction) override {
    if (!EditMode()) {
      MoveCursor(cursor, direction, LAST_CURSOR);
      return;
    }
    switch (cursor) {
    case WEIGHT1: weight_1 = constrain(weight_1 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT2: weight_2 = constrain(weight_2 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT4: weight_4 = constrain(weight_4 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case WEIGHT8: weight_8 = constrain(weight_8 + direction, 0, MAX_DIV_WEIGHT); break;
    case LOOP_LENGTH: {
      int old = loop_length;
      loop_length = constrain(loop_length + direction, 0, MAX_LOOP_LENGTH);
      if (old == 0 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(true, true);
      break;
    }
    case QUANT_SELECT:
      qselect = (uint8_t)constrain((int)qselect + direction, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
      break;
    case WALK:
      walk = constrain(walk + direction, 0, 4);
      break;
    default:
      if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
        int chrom = cursor - NOTE_0;
        int degree = ChromaticToDegree(chrom);
        if (degree >= 0) {
          note_weights[degree] = constrain(note_weights[degree] + direction, 0, MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT);
          value_animation = HEMISPHERE_CURSOR_TICKS;
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    if (cursor < LOOP_LENGTH && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11 && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
    if (cursor == WALK && loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, false);
  }

  uint64_t OnDataRequest() override {
    uint64_t data = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) {
      Pack(data, PackLocation {i * 3, 3}, note_weights[i]);
    }
    Pack(data, PackLocation {36, 4}, weight_1);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {40, 4}, weight_2);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {44, 4}, weight_4);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {48, 4}, weight_8);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {52, 6}, loop_length);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {58, 3}, qselect);
    Pack(data, PackLocation {61, 3}, walk);
    return data;
  }

  void OnDataReceive(uint64_t data) override {
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) {
      note_weights[i] = Unpack(data, PackLocation {i * 3, 3});
    }
    weight_1 = Unpack(data, PackLocation {36, 4});
    weight_2 = Unpack(data, PackLocation {40, 4});
    weight_4 = Unpack(data, PackLocation {44, 4});
    weight_8 = Unpack(data, PackLocation {48, 4});
    loop_length = Unpack(data, PackLocation {52, 6});
    qselect = Unpack(data, PackLocation {58, 3});
    walk = Unpack(data, PackLocation {61, 3});
    CONSTRAIN(qselect, 0, HS::QUANT_CHANNEL_COUNT - 1);
    CONSTRAIN(walk, 0, 4);
    if (loop_length > 0) GenerateLoop(false, true);
  }

protected:
  void SetHelp() override {
    //                    "-------" <-- Label size guide
    help[HELP_DIGITAL1] = "Clock";
    help[HELP_DIGITAL2] = "Reset";
    help[HELP_CV1]      = "Length";
    help[HELP_CV2]      = "Reseed";
    help[HELP_OUT1]     = "V/Oct";
    help[HELP_OUT2]     = "Gate";
    help[HELP_EXTRA1]   = "Pg1:Rhythm+Quant";
    help[HELP_EXTRA2]   = "Pg2:Keys+Walk";
    //                    "---------------------" <-- Extra text size guide
  }

private:
  int cursor;

  // Rhythm parameters (Page 0)
  uint8_t weight_1, weight_2, weight_4, weight_8;
  uint8_t loop_length, loop_length_mod;
  int div_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  int loop_index, loop_step, skip_steps;
  bool reseed_high, bypass_loop;

  // Melody parameters (Page 1)
  uint8_t note_weights[12];
  int8_t melody_loop[MAX_LOOP_LENGTH];
  uint8_t walk;

  // Quantizer
  uint8_t qselect;

  // Output state
  int pitch_cv;
  int8_t last_note;

  // UI animations
  int pulse_animation, reseed_animation, reset_animation, value_animation;

  // Division weight pointers and values
  const uint8_t *dw[4] = {&weight_1, &weight_2, &weight_4, &weight_8};
  static constexpr int divs[4] = {1, 2, 4, 8};

  // Keyboard layout constants
  static constexpr uint8_t kx[12] = {2, 7, 11, 16, 20, 29, 34, 38, 43, 47, 52, 56};
  static constexpr uint8_t kp[12] = {0, 1,  0,  1,  0,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0,  1,  0};
  static constexpr char    kn[12] = {'C','C','D','D','E','F','F','G','G','A','A','B'};

  int GetNumNotes() {
    return (int)OC::Scales::GetScale(HS::GetScale(qselect)).num_notes;
  }

  uint8_t DegreeToChromatic(int degree) {
    const HS::QuantEngine& qe = HS::GetQuantEngine(qselect);
    const braids::Scale& sc = OC::Scales::GetScale(qe.scale);
    int n = (int)sc.num_notes;
    int d = ((degree % n) + n) % n;
    int semi = (int)(sc.notes[d] >> 7) % 12;
    return (uint8_t)((semi + qe.root_note) % 12);
  }

  int ChromaticToDegree(int chrom) {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if ((int)DegreeToChromatic(i) == chrom) return i;
    }
    return -1;
  }

  int8_t GetNextWeightedPitch() {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      total += note_weights[i];
    }
    if (total == 0) return 0;
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      if (note_weights[i] > 0) {
        if (rnd <= note_weights[i]) return (int8_t)i;
        rnd -= note_weights[i];
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }

  int NoteToCV(int8_t degree) {
    int note = (int)degree + 64;
    return HS::QuantizerLookup(qselect, note) + (12 * OC::DAC::kOctaveZero);
  }

  int GetNextWeightedDiv() {
    int total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) total += *dw[i];
    int rnd = random(0, total + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      if (rnd <= *dw[i] && *dw[i] > 0) return divs[i];
      rnd -= *dw[i];
    }
    return 0;
  }

  int GetNextLoopDivAndNote(int8_t& note_out) {
    int idx = loop_index;
    int value = div_loop[idx];
    if (value == 0) {
      loop_index = loop_step = 0;
      idx = 0;
      value = div_loop[0];
    }
    note_out = melody_loop[idx];
    loop_index++;
    loop_step++;
    return value;
  }

  void GenerateLoop(bool reseed, bool restart) {
    memset(div_loop, 0, sizeof(div_loop));
    memset(melody_loop, 0, sizeof(melody_loop));
    if (restart) { loop_step = loop_index = 0; }
    int seed = (weight_1 << 12) | (weight_2 << 8) | (weight_4 << 4) | weight_8;
    seed ^= (qselect << 16);
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) seed ^= note_weights[i] << i;
    if (reseed) { randomSeed(micros()); seed ^= (int)micros(); }
    randomSeed(seed);
    int idx = 0, counter = 0;
    while (counter < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH && idx < MAX_LOOP_LENGTH) {
      int div = GetNextWeightedDiv();
      if (div == 0) break;
      div_loop[idx] = div;
      int8_t degree = GetNextWeightedPitch();
      if (walk > 0) {
        int oct = random(-walk, walk + 1);
        degree = (int8_t)(degree + oct * 12);
      }
      melody_loop[idx] = degree;
      idx++;
      counter += div;
    }
  }

  void DrawPage0() {
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      gfxPrint(1, 15 + i * 10, "/");
      gfxPrint(divs[i]);
      DrawSlider(20, 15 + i * 10, 40, *dw[i], MAX_DIV_WEIGHT, cursor == i);
      if (pulse_animation > 0 && skip_steps == divs[i])
        gfxInvert(1, 15 + i * 10, 12, 8);
    }

    gfxIcon(4, 55, LOOP_ICON);
    if (reseed_animation > 0) gfxInvert(4, 55, 12, 8);

    if (loop_length_mod == 0) {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, "off");
    } else {
      gfxPrint(19, 55, loop_length_mod);
    }
    if (cursor == LOOP_LENGTH) gfxCursor(19, 63, 18);

    gfxPrint(40, 55, "Q");
    gfxPrint(46, 55, qselect + 1);
    if (cursor == QUANT_SELECT) gfxCursor(40, 63, 18);

    if (reset_animation > 0) gfxPrint(57, 55, "R");
  }

  void DrawPage1() {
    int num_notes = GetNumNotes();

    gfxPrint(46, 13, "W:");
    gfxPrint(56, 13, walk);
    if (cursor == WALK) gfxCursor(46, 15, 16);

    gfxFrame(0, 27, 63, 32);

    for (uint8_t wx = 1; wx < 7; ++wx) {
      if (wx == 3) gfxLine(wx * 9, 27, wx * 9, 58);
      else         gfxLine(wx * 9, 43, wx * 9, 58);
    }

    static constexpr uint8_t bk_x[5] = {6, 15, 33, 42, 51};
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 5; ++i) gfxInvert(bk_x[i], 28, 5, 15);

    for (int i = 0; i < num_notes; i++) {
      uint8_t c  = DegreeToChromatic(i);
      uint8_t xo = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yo = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      bool is_cur = EditMode() && cursor == NOTE_0 + (int)c;
      bool has_weight = note_weights[i] > 0;
      bool is_black = kp[c];
      if (has_weight || is_cur) {
        gfxDottedLine(xo, yo, xo, yo + 10);
        if (has_weight) {
          int bar_height = (note_weights[i] * 10) / MAX_NOTE_WEIGHT;
          int bar_y = yo + 10 - bar_height;
          if (is_black) {
            gfxInvert(xo - 1, bar_y, 3, bar_height);
          } else {
            gfxLine(xo - 1, bar_y, xo + 1, bar_y);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    {
      int d = ((int)last_note % num_notes + num_notes) % num_notes;
      uint8_t c = DegreeToChromatic(d);
      gfxIcon(kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2) - 3, 59, UP_TRI_R_HALF);
    }

    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c  = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      uint8_t xc = kx[c] + (kp[c] ? 1 : 2);
      uint8_t yc = kp[c] ? 31 : 45;
      if (EditMode() || CursorBlink()) gfxLine(xc, yc, xc, yc + 10);
    }

    if (cursor >= NOTE_0 && cursor <= NOTE_11) {
      uint8_t c = (uint8_t)(cursor - NOTE_0);
      int degree = ChromaticToDegree(c);
      gfxRect(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
      gfxPos(17, 14);
      graphics.printf("%c", kn[c]);
      if (kp[c]) gfxPrint(23, 14, "#");
      if (degree < 0) {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, "N/A");
      } else {
        gfxPrint(29, 14, ":");
        gfxPrint(33, 14, note_weights[degree]);
      }
      gfxInvert(15, 13, 28, 10);
    }
  }
};
```
